- `980038e5090eec8b` [general] Kernel docs state: 'System call filtering isn't a sandbox... It is meant to be a tool for sandbox developers to use. Beyond that, policy for logical behavior and information flow should be managed with... potentially, an LSM of your choosing.' Direct corollary: seccomp alone is insufficient as a worker sandbox; an LSM layer (Landlock is one) is the documented complement (opinion derived directly from that statement).
  links: a68feba40bd78382, 554ea35e5b4867b0, 7c6f0eb74c7ce6fc, 9a5b5080bf5a63c4
- `a495d4c500ec01b5` [general] LangGraph documents an unbounded-growth failure mode: over long conversations checkpoints accumulate, increasing latency and storage costs; the fix is pruning or a retention policy (equivalent to compaction), and DeltaChannel stores deltas instead of full values to bound checkpoint size.
  links: 7c0a290e885b0a7d, 8baa3b2f82d7faa6, 0f28c5715a50722b, e268c955dcf1bd2f
- `a4be8759582ea228` [repair] Reflexive repair loop (Jang et al., measured): on a failed call append 'ERROR: {err} Reissue this tool call with corrected, schema-valid arguments.', regenerate, up to 3 attempts (five telecom models at every precision) — collapses tool-name hallucination 38.26% → 2.90% (INT4), cuts out-of-list events 1,646→82 (20×), eliminates budget exhaustions (46→0), shortens trajectories (17.9→15.9 turns), and raises INT4 final score 65.35 → 71.27 (+5.9, CI [+1.1,+10.8], p=.017) above unmitigated BF16 (66.7%), while every other cell's repair interval includes zero — 'the mechanism is prevention rather than cure'; recommendation: report per-channel error rates and success-vs-budget alongside task reward.
  links: 83f1964c6217fb0d, 875c47689a1e1a8c, b25f3d9404e1c402, 07bdd35766db54d4
- `ac9b554f4139828d` [evidence-status] mini-agi `verify_run` returns `unverified` with the excerpt 'no deterministic verifier declared (outcome is the run's own claim)' when no gate exists.
  links: 20775a40371d1e55, 7fe943345da11d30, 8af8f2689671ac0a, 467e731dc67aed05
- `adbc0cde1ae47dc7` [general] Google Cloud splits audit logs into four named classes: Admin Activity (user-driven API calls or actions modifying resource configuration/metadata), Data Access, System Event (log entries written by Google Cloud systems that modify resource configuration and aren't driven by direct user action, e.g. autoscaling adding/removing VMs), and Policy Denied — a first-order channel filter encoding whether a human drove an entry.
  links: 6a52b10eed388a69, bdf2aeb421403f14, 51d27a5241433608, f02881fc4dc7a2ea
- `ae9c67ba8d9f53fa` [general] Compaction is defined as 'taking a conversation nearing the context window limit, summarizing its contents, and reinitiating a new context window with the summary'; in Claude Code the model is passed history to 'summarize and compress the most critical details... preserving architectural decisions, unresolved bugs, and implementation details while discarding redundant tool outputs or messages', continuing with compressed context plus the five most recently accessed files. (Anthropic Engineering, effective-context-engineering, 2025-09-29)
  links: b2fee6b8038589fb, f33c569f53a96c52, 1c1bd4db44906d9f, 692f03b97bba1e0f
- `af4621ee1fa4b90c` [general] MemGPT (paper arXiv:2310.08560 §2.2) separates main context (LLM prompt tokens: system instructions, working context, FIFO queue) from external context (archival storage, a read/write database of arbitrary-length text objects; and recall storage, the message database); messages evicted from the FIFO queue are stored indefinitely in recall storage and remain readable via MemGPT function calls.
  links: 3997ff446a6ec077, 90b6a3e4a78524a2, 7c0a290e885b0a7d, 0f3c6731224b1b90
- `b3cea14ec5ed51db` [general] GitLab shows an approval-state icon on each merge request in the project/group MR list ('required approvals missing' vs 'approvals satisfied') and a widget on the MR showing per-reviewer state (Awaiting review / In progress / Approved / Commented / Requested changes); an eligible reviewer approves from the widget or via the '/approve' quick action.
  links: 1d998782a2b7e1ae, 38fcbdc4541b046e, 7dc38bcaf329e110, fa9b089b4e75b0cb
- `b7d8338a5de669c3` [general] Filesystem-as-state is used where human readability, git, and zero-infra win, with explicit first-party limits. Claude Code: the first 200 lines of MEMORY.md, or the first 25KB, are loaded at the start of every conversation; topic files are not loaded at startup but read on demand; auto memory is machine-local and files are not shared across machines or cloud environments; CLAUDE.md should target under 200 lines per file because longer files 'consume more context and reduce adherence.'
  links: 4e221bfa0c5d7bc3, ebcb42396b17bd81, 0d0774d31bed17b3, 15090391d6c44e0d
- `c65a4ce6cdb624b8` [general] Long-term cross-thread agent memory has a different shape than run state and is DB- or vector-store-backed in the frameworks. LangGraph's long-term memory is a Store abstraction (namespace + key + JSON value) with production backends PostgresStore, MongoDBStore, RedisStore, UpstashStore and optional semantic/vector search; the reference SQL schema uses TEXT[] namespace + JSONB value + GIN index.
  links: d86f43007ae0e974, 75f9d3c529016fb3, b1016827b3e68aa6, 26963aa692752e78
- `ccbdd85316bb9ecd` [general] Reflexion's ablation on the 50 hardest HumanEval Rust problems (GPT-4 base): base 0.60; self-reflection without test execution 0.52; test execution without natural-language reflection 0.60; full Reflexion (tests+reflection) 0.68. The reflection pass added +8pp only when paired with an external evaluator; 'reflect but no tests' performed worse than the base (Reflexion §4.3, Table 3).
  links: 473e82a58eb494fe, 3e53d4e63a90eb15, 9240d1f949e33e15, b06e866e7dd44247
- `d4d8a0005c2c4c6a` [general] MemGPT (arXiv:2310.08560 Table 2) on DMR (500 MSC conversations, gpt-4-turbo) reaches 93.4% LLM-judge accuracy vs 35.3% recursive-summarization baseline, 32.1% raw GPT-4 (92.5% with MemGPT), 38.7% GPT-3.5 Turbo (66.9% with MemGPT).
  links: dfa6bac8d91fe7f9, ca79380b2a16a6e8, 8de41cd967fdda17, 15116661844082ce
- `daf69938aa31c832` [general] Notification-noise reduction is documented as threshold aggregation + severity state machines + transition-only firing + dedup keys + daily digest (LangSmith alerts, Langfuse monitors, Braintrust Topics digest), not as per-trace paging.
  links: 7cfe9661cd15e22a, b3d72fc2c65ef3e3, fe7a1f40036e0381, 1686d0bb9b2d028f
- `ddf4bfc7760221e4` [general] MCP spec: MCP itself cannot enforce security principles at the protocol level; hosts must obtain explicit user consent before invoking any tool, tool annotations should be considered untrusted unless obtained from a trusted server, and implementors SHOULD build robust consent and authorization flows.
  links: df2131dec381bc98, e232f1c2ce57f2cf, 728334f20b68d83d, c81957cf3aac4c06
- `dfa6bac8d91fe7f9` [general] MemGPT DMR accuracy 93.4% (GPT-4 Turbo + MemGPT) vs 35.3% (recursive summarization alone) [S1] §3.1.1; MemGPT reports no separate consolidation cost figures.
  links: 3997ff446a6ec077, d4d8a0005c2c4c6a, ca79380b2a16a6e8, 15116661844082ce
- `e51f44d00affc6cb` [evidence-status] SWE-bench evaluates models by applying their generated patches to real-world repositories and running the repository's tests to verify if the issue is resolved, inside a containerized Docker environment.
  links: 32bed97e81d99774, 8af8f2689671ac0a, 1ee6755802152b1d, beb33e0d0a29d161
- `ecca3611145516e6` [general] Fowler's Event Sourcing: all changes to application state are stored as a sequence of events, enabling complete rebuild by re-running events on an empty application, plus temporal query and event replay; explicitly paired with snapshots (replay from an overnight snapshot on crash).
  links: a40757229890299e, 6665ad2250b0bf6a, 8baa3b2f82d7faa6, e761f79662ed051f
- `f3583d648963c6ab` [general] Self-Refine (Madaan et al., 2023) reports ~20% absolute average gain across 7 tasks, but on GSM8K the self-feedback loop gained ≈0pp (GPT-3.5 64.1→64.1, ChatGPT 74.8→75.0, GPT-4 92.9→93.1); the paper's explanation is that the model cannot detect its own math errors — ChatGPT's feedback was 'everything looks good' in 94% of instances — while with oracle (external) error feedback the gain became 5%+ (arXiv:2303.17651v2).
  links: b66006c1f5cda644, 93d3f6c1ebd2262f, 635c8cbfd002fa86, fd14a275027254d8
- `f4090a7c63c389b2` [repair] Offline improvement of native rate (measured): RL with a fine-grained schema validator (SRL) raises valid-JSON generation 28.5→72.5% (Llama-3.2-3B) and 36.5→79.7% (Llama-3.1-8B) — the only measured route that improves what retries resample from.
  links: 3137514f89e89347, fe15f8a0ae73fa1f, a090ad17666f0913, 9edd522ac96ebcbc
- `f647744ca1f24b4f` [general] Bazel `--flaky_test_attempts` retries each failing test up to the specified count; tests needing more than one attempt are marked 'FLAKY' in the test summary. Default is one attempt for regular tests and three for tests marked flaky (flaky=1). Supports per-pattern targeting, e.g. --flaky_test_attempts=//foo/.*,-//foo/bar/.*@3.
  links: 804ffb8a7c26c4aa, fac277b31fa74875, 3880083953a8f0d3, e6b84c294456ecd4
- `f7a62764ca74d0e9` [general] Interaction-Centric Taxonomy (arXiv 2607.28802) was grounded in 40 worked examples (E1-E40) drawn from public benchmarks, model system cards, published reports, and logged agent trajectories; labels assigned by tracing the earliest unrecoverable failure ('root-cause principle'); strongest independent reasoning judge reaches Cohen's kappa=0.76 vs. human category labels, highest pairwise judge agreement kappa=0.84.
  links: aa17692c22a78738, 38956b8103d9e760, c1a0b768075c2467, 7486a7f9f63098ee
- `f83e967751bb05ea` [repair] IBM CW (system report): model-specific semantic failure — gpt-oss-120b regressed semantic 58.9→44.9 (−14.0) and 24.1%→6.9% satisfaction on v2 despite being #1 structurally (97.8%), because v2 caused it to emit stub workflows (a single comment block declaring integrations unavailable) which pass schema validation but are non-executable — 'uniquely treacherous: first on structural metrics, last on whether outputs do what users asked'.
  links: 97cf04e1f8130ba2, a4d77e88c7e61873, d84e1b455ff7763a, a2d0d1644fe1d3d4
- `fd14a275027254d8` [repair] Self-Refine caveat (measured): its headline ~20% average is driven by preference/constrained tasks (+20–49 pp) while math gains are ~0 (64.1→64.1, 92.9→93.1) because the model cannot tell its own math is wrong; its own failure analysis confirms the feedback stage, not the refiner, is the bottleneck (61% bad fix, 33% wrong location, 6% refiner error).
  links: b66006c1f5cda644, f3583d648963c6ab, 93d3f6c1ebd2262f, f8edba98b002ec5d
- `04ce20f686503fa3` [repair] Constraint Tax side-effect (measured): under joint tool-calling + JSON-schema constraints, grammar masks silently suppress tool calls — Tool Invocation Rate 100%→0% for 6 open-weight models (GPT-5.4-mini unaffected) — so in agent stacks the compliance win can hide an action-taking loss; two-pass decoupling restores tool calls.
  links: 4cb71d0255cccf49, f02881fc4dc7a2ea, 308c11e281ffbca4
- `0d9ab0996bcbca80` [general] Mix-up attacks: iss-based Authorization Response Validation mitigates them; PKCE alone does not prevent the attack because the client transmits the verifier to the attacker's token endpoint (Security Best Practices).
  links: c81957cf3aac4c06, e4344628bee35e4c, 9a9c35587bb37962
- `1412c255c7aa37da` [general] TiMem (2026 preprint) claims 75.30% on LoCoMo and 76.88% on LongMemEval-S as 'state-of-the-art' — an estimate from a recent preprint (ACL 2026 Findings claim), unverified and not independently corroborated. Source: arXiv:2601.02845 abstract.
  links: 7087735a64f76c95, 0fb38c56ff147e30, 97b9c2be82edddfc
- `15090391d6c44e0d` [general] Claude Code auto memory is keyed to git repository identity: each project gets its own memory directory at ~/.claude/projects/<project>/memory/, derived from the git repository so worktrees and subdirectories share one auto memory directory; it is machine-local, not shared across machines; only the first 200 lines / 25KB of MEMORY.md load at session start.
  links: 44f6ae81403776df, b7d8338a5de669c3, ebcb42396b17bd81
- `154306422881beb8` [general] No first-party product documentation describing deployed operator-facing confidence UIs (e.g., what ChatGPT/Claude/Vertex actually render for confidence in production) was located; the production picture is reconstructed from methods papers, first-party lab research (OpenAI Lin 2022, Anthropic Kadavath 2022), and deployment-style clinical studies — an absence-of-evidence claim, not proof of non-existence (unverified).
  links: 1b0ebc092cb2ebe8, f3b28a55a63ea68b, a2d0d1644fe1d3d4
- `1686d0bb9b2d028f` [general] Attention routing into HITL queues is overwhelmingly pre-filtering rather than pushing every trace at a human: rule + sampling-rate automations route a fraction of filtered runs into queues (LangSmith), and topic/issue classification feeds assignment and online scoring (Braintrust Topics, Datadog Patterns/Insights, LangSmith Engine).
  links: fe8d202885bb2379, df988dfd099e2766, daf69938aa31c832
- `16f2c921fa480440` [general] Staleness is an explicit knob: BigQuery max_staleness bounds lag at 30 minutes to 3 days (doc-stated, not measured); Materialize's reaction time is framed as freshness + latency (doc-reported, page not fetched directly); Oracle real-time MVs trade stored freshness for on-query computation; dbt's default is batch staleness bounded by run cadence.
  links: 8ab263a3dc129e33, e761f79662ed051f, 00d9b00db4beee22
- `176d8ed99f2c76c6` [general] gen_ai.execute_tool.duration metric: unit s; required gen_ai.tool.name; conditional error.type and gen_ai.agent.name; recommended gen_ai.tool.type.
  links: 0a74dadc333efd53, a374d61735af78e8, 55a5de088bb8a4cb
- `19d6b1929b157a24` [general] No controlled experiment was found isolating any single retention policy (e.g. decay on/off, eviction threshold, supersede vs delete) with retrieval-quality numbers; such numbers are published only for whole systems (Zep, MemGPT) on fixed benchmarks, not for the retention mechanism in isolation.
  links: c115eb02966062a5, 7c0a290e885b0a7d, 0a3013221a215aa2
- `1c1bd4db44906d9f` [general] Anthropic warns 'overly aggressive compaction can result in the loss of subtle but critical context'; guidance is to 'Start by maximizing recall to ensure your compaction prompt captures every relevant piece of information from the trace, then iterate to improve precision.' (Effective context engineering)
  links: f799ae94c5278f06, ae9c67ba8d9f53fa, e7777e2701a56d30
- `1d998782a2b7e1ae` [general] LangSmith annotation queues group runs or whole threads, prescribe a rubric ("Instructions" + feedback keys), and track reviewer progress; queues present one item at a time ("single-run") or two runs side-by-side ("pairwise"), with item states Needs Review → Needs Others' Review → Completed.
  links: 2d77bf15f0089c2f, 4d21686cc85b19ee, b3cea14ec5ed51db
- `1f4b9fc62486cff6` [general] PostgreSQL REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW completely replaces the view contents by re-executing the backing query; stock PostgreSQL has no incremental refresh path, only full recomputation.
  links: 8097fca3fceebe45, 266b8325088b1614, ae4ee7c4c7369b1f
- `1f894d76e5789412` [repair] Melo (system report; measured on internal 298-query stress-test set, each config replayed 3× at temperature 0.3, Kimi K2.5 backbone, ~10³ measurements; rates caveated as properties of the stress-test set, 'not estimates of raw production frequency'): retry fires on 5.8% of sessions; process-level recovery 59.1% (retry_relaxed 63% recovering 52% of triggers, retry_rewrite 56% recovering 48%); constraint preservation after recovery 77% (23% recovered-but-degraded, concentrated in retry_relaxed 29.4% vs retry_rewrite 14.3%; intent-preserving recovery ≈46% of triggered sessions); central caution: 'retry must not hide what it gives up' — recovery and constraint preservation evaluated as two metrics in tension.
  links: a3d101849aff988d, 70c12cac8fc7ae06, 9d1f310048daf8ef
- `23db60932cbf376c` [general] Beads (Oct 2025, MIT, ~23k stars) is a portable work ledger: each unit of work is a bead — atomic, durable, version-controlled, audit-trailed, queryable across sessions — and stands alone as a coding-agent memory/persistence layer.
  links: a602df8d9b46c6b5, 0ff9b686f9e72fc3, b91eeab1a9452dee
- `284dea68d04d19f5` [general] Tool-invocation reliability taxonomy (Huang, Malwe, Wang; arXiv 2601.16280, ICAIBD 2026) is a 12-category error taxonomy spanning tool initialization, parameter handling, execution, and result interpretation; category names not enumerated in the abstract; measured on 1,980 deterministic test instances across open-weight (Qwen2.5 series, Functionary) and proprietary models (GPT-4, Claude 3.5/3.7), with tool-initialization failures identified as the primary bottleneck for smaller models.
  links: 9ef58d49f849ae77, 0e16f2f530c68f78, 90b87f746aa7223d
- `2b319322a0428380` [general] When frameworks add persistence, they route production durability to databases, not flat files. LangGraph's checkpointer table: MemorySaver/InMemorySaver = RAM, lost on restart; SqliteSaver = 'Ideal for experimentation and local workflows… local file-based storage for development'; PostgresSaver = 'used in LangSmith. Ideal for using in production.'
  links: 40a6f5c1783caef7, 7fb535112327a3fb, 28a317b7a861c29c
- `300485f9871ece7c` [general] FrugalGPT cascade (3 LLMs, learned confidence thresholds) achieves cost savings to match the best individual LLM's accuracy: 98.3% on HEADLINES (GPT-4 best), 73.3% on OVERRULING (GPT-4), 59.2% on COQA (GPT-3 best). (FrugalGPT, arXiv 2305.05176, Table 3)
  links: 1eb6cac2ee3c0b55, f1536221b75a8013, e9faf4c799927644
- `336453c5de421eee` [general] Mem0 writes are additive-only by default: new memories are added without overwriting or deleting existing memories; update/delete are separate operations. [fact]
  links: ccecea1af23feed5, 6ed4eb78bc0729b3, 93aab397ab2425d6
- `33aa2b6ddca3a89e` [general] dbt incremental models build the table from all source rows on first run and transform only the rows the user filters for on subsequent runs; is_incremental() is true only when the target table exists, full-refresh is not passed, and the model is materialized='incremental'.
  links: 266b8325088b1614, 15355f27b297c061, ae4ee7c4c7369b1f
- `344882f912fc587f` [general] A close() on any file descriptor for a SQLite file in one thread cancels the POSIX advisory locks held by all threads of the process, which can lead to two or more threads or processes writing to the database at the same time and corrupting it; only partially fixed defensively in 3.51.0.
  links: 866588d8e2efd4e0, 3c51acc3d4b42092, 5b306c225ec47f04
- `356b94ef83a50a9a` [general] Landlock network support is late and partial: no network restriction before ABI v4 (kernel 6.7, TCP bind/connect only); UDP added in ABI v10. On older/backported kernels a best-effort policy silently drops network control. IPv4/IPv6 stream sockets that are not TCP (SMC/MPTCP/SCTP) were mis-gated by TCP rights before Erratum 1.
  links: a68feba40bd78382, 554ea35e5b4867b0, dd98058876169ac0
- `3912dd0a304826cb` [general] OpenAI Agents SDK automatically tracks token usage for every run: requests, input_tokens, output_tokens, total_tokens, per-request entries, and input_tokens_details.cached_tokens / output_tokens_details.reasoning_tokens; aggregated across tool calls and handoffs; usage is per-run and independent, and previous messages may be re-fed as input to each run which affects the input token count in subsequent turns.
  links: b1b91935c25b467a, 31afd4aa76fb215a, 7baa4e0fc447c393
- `3980f1133d5b8a7d` [ai-auditability] VC Data Integrity proof sets/chains are a standards-level mechanism for multi-party signing of one document (unordered co-signatures vs. ordered counter-signatures) — the substrate approval records could be built on.
  links: 7e6f8bd91c5c7679, d1b3d3ef01bfcfff, 77ad6114bf5546c2
- `399d42c5feedc012` [general] A 2026 preprint (arXiv:2605.27559) finds the conditional miscorrection rate dominant (53–94%) across model families and benchmarks in a 'detection-without-correction' decomposition; another 2026 preprint (arXiv:2606.02646) finds a noise placebo matches self-correction on free-form math at 4× scale — suggesting much of the gain attributed to debate/reflection may be plain re-evaluation — and that 30 dense debating agents produce no more answer diversity than one. Both are arXiv preprints.
  links: a375ddea979ace49, 8fe5600483b3642b, 33f44ff98f2913eb
- `3c50f0967b34b231` [evidence-status] Anthropic multi-agent research system: research outputs are difficult to evaluate programmatically and LLMs are a natural fit; a single judge prompt scoring 0.0-1.0 plus a pass/fail grade was most consistent, and human evaluation catches what automation misses (e.g. hallucinated answers on unusual queries).
  links: b2fee6b8038589fb, 2469f7b7d49a226e, 67ab1b96cc2d1128
- `44c1fa789d32eb44` [general] τ-bench (arXiv:2406.12045): even SOTA function-calling agents succeed on <50% of tasks and are inconsistent (pass^8 <25% in retail). Verified pass^k curves: airline claude-3-5-sonnet Pass^1/2/3/4 = 0.460/0.326/0.263/0.225; gpt-4o = 0.420/0.273/0.220/0.200; retail claude = 0.692/0.576/0.509/0.462; gpt-4o = 0.604/0.491/0.430/0.383. The monotone decay quantifies that retry-via-resampling is largely exhausted by k≈4–8. τ²-bench adds dual-control Telecom (both agent and user act); τ³-bench adds banking + voice. Repo flags the airline/retail tasks as outdated first version superseded by τ³.
  links: 4d082f928948911f, 308c11e281ffbca4, b8b7f1d1340fadc7
- `4992782a5790d742` [agent-behavior] Behavioral guideline (Karpathy): simplicity first — minimum code that solves the problem; no speculative features, no single-use abstractions, no unrequested flexibility/configurability, no error handling for impossible scenarios. enforced_by: review rubric (overengineering = FIX-MINOR)
  links: a49b169111deb842, fa58509d3523cc84, a588d4401a139d71
- `57a4ba0fa591b0c8` [general] Mini AGI checkpoint journal records BEGIN/VERIFY-PASS/VERIFY-FAIL/CHECKPOINT-ABORT in memory/episodic/checkpoints.log; a red gate hard-resets to the last BEGIN commit (rollback always lands on the last checkpoint, ADR-0004); the journal must be repaired via checkpoint.sh, never through git.
  links: e39035055344f0fc, 4e221bfa0c5d7bc3, f54b0ed68eef1372
- `57c6cc3485ade4ed` [general] DEFT (arXiv 2512.01948) was derived by grounded theory on ~1,000 reports from 10+ deep research agents: open coding by 5 LLM coders (Claude Opus 4.1, Gemini 2.5 Pro, Grok 4, DeepSeek-V3.1, Qwen3-Max-Preview) yielding 51 concepts, axial coding in 3 ICR rounds with 3 domain experts sampling 24-54 records with Krippendorff's alpha, selective coding to 3 core categories, theoretical-saturation check on unseen agents (WebThinker, OpenManus), human-LLM inter-coder reliability alpha~0.80-0.90.
  links: 973e990075c8a7fd, c1a0b768075c2467, 7486a7f9f63098ee
- `5ff5c00c83663882` [general] LangGraph durability modes trade recovery vs. performance: 'exit' persists only on exit (no mid-execution recovery after a process crash), 'async' persists during the next step (small loss window on crash), 'sync' persists every checkpoint before the next step (high durability, performance cost).
  links: b556d77f45006ee2, 8baa3b2f82d7faa6, 8c506f6dd07767f9
- `6729b6c2eba5c724` [general] Retroformer (Yao et al., 2024) argues Reflexion-style verbal feedback 'does not reason and plan in ways compatible with gradient-based learning'; its learned retrospective model tuned by policy gradient improves agents over time and outperforms baselines that do not use environment rewards — the optimization of the reflection itself is the value-adding successor step (arXiv:2308.02151).
  links: 9240d1f949e33e15, 0369c5606fbf5ccc, 1f6daa3b273092cd
- `693010a524f0a9fb` [general] Braintrust human review defines review scores (categorical, continuous, free-form) that appear in logs/experiments/datasets, and manages review work via a dedicated Review page with views (Awaiting review, Assigned to me, Completed), SQL filtering, and a kanban layout (Backlog / Pending / Complete); rows can be assigned to team members who get email (and optional Slack DM) notifications.
  links: 49b7fb8236915e10, e5e1072ffa4930c8, 5940164f5de5669e
- `6cf5f705aaedf39d` [general] Established multi-source finding: hard limits — calibrated LMs must hallucinate single-occurrence facts (Kalai & Vempala 2024) and UQ degrades under genuine ambiguity (Tomov 2025) — so confidence display must be paired with abstention/verification.
  links: ddfc343faef6bd2c, 6e30b9e0905d102a, b7ab00766ba80fd0
- `75648dd5d0625730` [general] cargo-semver-checks lints API diffs against the Cargo SemVer reference, supports deny/warn/allow lint levels and required-update (major/minor), has defined exit codes (0 clean, 100 violations, 101 error), and targets not to have false positives; its FAQ states it does not catch every violation.
  links: 9aa8824078e5adf2, 9a4df56f7d846a01, 34fc1c71481976d6
- `79088f70c96759ad` [repair] SQL Query Engine (system report): on the synthetic benchmark (75 questions, 3 DBs) Config C (generation only) 48.0% → Config A (up to 5 repairs) 57.3% for Llama 4 Scout 17B (+9.3 pp, zero regressions); on BIRD mini-dev (437 questions, 11 DBs) the full pipeline reached 49.0% execution accuracy (GPT-OSS-120B), +4.6 pp from self-healing; repair latency cost visible: Scout 1.67 s → 4.19 s mean.
  links: 135c69977d75322e, a090ad17666f0913, 5480bd685bff8004
- `7b032b76d2592172` [general] Seccomp ptrace escape: 'seccomp-based sandboxes MUST NOT allow use of ptrace, even of other sandboxed processes, without extreme care; ptracers can use this mechanism to escape' (SECCOMP_RET_TRACE lets a tracer rewrite the syscall, and before 4.8 the check was not re-run after tracer notification).
  links: d71aa36a9e81bc8c, a0ae947f3627d7ca, 76649269e1ba035d
- `7b3058140a429de1` [general] dbt's standard incremental filter is a high-watermark against the derived table itself: where event_time >= (select coalesce(max(event_time),'1900-01-01') from {{ this }}); new-rows-only semantics suit append-only sources directly.
  links: c436a95d905a7aa0, e761f79662ed051f, f89a7953b2d236cf
- `8605993e67ae595e` [general] Clippy CI integration documented by the project: rustup component add clippy; CARGO_BUILD_WARNINGS=deny cargo clippy --all-targets --all-features fails the build on any clippy or rustc warning; cargo clippy --fix auto-applies suggestions.
  links: 0658c76662a6a80e, 9f8b2d401403a107, 246f64abc4d8d6c8
- `86494d2770f9e1c6` [general] Generative Agents (Park et al., UIST 2023, arXiv:2304.03442) uses a memory stream with retrieval by recency/importance/relevance, and observations summarized on the fly into higher-level reflections stored back into the stream. No token-savings numbers are reported; the mechanism keeps only salient memories in context. Source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2304.03442
  links: 9807eb38133c8148, eee720517a9475e3, 582d97a2bb8bd903
- `86bb11459302600d` [general] File/SQLite concurrency is bounded: one writer at a time, and network-filesystem file-locking bugs can corrupt state (sqlite.org); 'scale-out' and 'concurrent writers' are exactly the cases the primary docs route to client/server DBs.
  links: 601ae8b56f00daf0, 3e5e18d029069f90, 7c86cd916f6513f8
- `87e145dc13717659` [general] Kubernetes distinguishes principals by a username convention; audit policy rules match on users and userGroups; documented examples include username system:kube-proxy, group system:authenticated, and impersonation example system:serviceaccount:default:my-controller.
  links: fe7f8b67b4c17d62, 1696dd59343bd054, 17e10ae638f998a8
- `90b6a3e4a78524a2` [general] MemGPT (arXiv:2310.08560 §2.2) queue manager enforces a warning token count (e.g. 70% of context window) that inserts a system 'memory pressure' warning so the LLM can move data to working context or archival storage, and a flush token count (e.g. 100%) at which it evicts a count of messages (e.g. 50% of context window) and generates a new recursive summary from the existing recursive summary plus evicted messages; the FIFO queue's first slot holds that recursive summary.
  links: 3997ff446a6ec077, af4621ee1fa4b90c, 7c0a290e885b0a7d
- `92823ba871a2eaae` [general] Graphiti (Zep's open-source engine) treats each fact as having a validity window: changed information is invalidated, not deleted; every entity/relationship traces back to the raw episodes that produced it; new data integrates incrementally without batch recomputation; contradictions are handled by automatic fact invalidation with temporal history preserved (bi-temporal tracking). Source: getzep/graphiti README.
  links: ccecea1af23feed5, ca509f76052d6818, 70d2e0f66bef8b38
- `952039c898e69791` [repair] Inter-agent hand-off representation (measured): schema-constrained JSON hand-off achieves feasibility 0.96 (false-NONE 0/50) vs narrative summary 0.48 (8/50) at the smallest payload (Relay); for multi-hop relays structure is 'faithful, not corrective' — injected errors persist 83–100% in every format with no cascade, and a strong relay is near-lossless in every format (≥0.973 recall after 6 hops) while under a weak relay JSON retains 0.94 of facts vs 0.74–0.85 for others.
  links: f350dbda4620ffdd, 39c9cdfc179c58ba, 7263566e1ca0c610
- `984129939642011a` [general] Longitudinal silent-failure study (Wu; arXiv 2606.14589) covered an 8-week study of one production personal-assistant runtime (~40 scheduled jobs, 8 LLM providers) with 22 documented root-caused incidents; reported ~70% of silent failures caught by human user-view observation, 0% ex-ante prevention vs. 87% regression-blocking in retrospective audit, incident latency 13 hours to 60 days.
  links: f4b23532d8f51878, c1a0b768075c2467, b023dfa34d7b6603
- `9a5b5080bf5a63c4` [general] Landlock is an LSM layer stacked over DAC/other LSMs; seccomp constrains the syscall surface; kernel docs place both as composable hardening layers, and inheritance across clone/execve is analogous for both. Nothing in the primary sources documents a way to combine them into one primitive; each keeps its separate gaps.
  links: 980038e5090eec8b, a68feba40bd78382, 554ea35e5b4867b0
- `9b9296bb8b2fc470` [general] CouchDB invalidates per-document view rows via back-references and applies changes since the last query as a batch operation, since usually multiple documents change between queries.
  links: f481b98eb5ba6799, 8c38fd0e61beee75, 8ab263a3dc129e33
- `9c3c2433df52405a` [repair] MOUD (system report): the repair loop is human-analyst-driven — a domain-expert error review identified three recurring fix classes applied as rule-based post-processing: imputing missing dosage fields from within-drug norms (Clinic B ~20% of records had key missing fields), handling monthly/weekly injectables (e.g., Vivitrol ~5% of Clinic C/E prescriptions) by setting duration from the documented schedule, and unit normalization so mass units ('250 g') are not misread as daily counts — residual semantic errors need domain rules, not more retries; authors explicitly note human-in-the-loop review is essential for high-stakes clinical use.
  links: f3b28a55a63ea68b, a090ad17666f0913, d5995f282ab01107
- `9d1f310048daf8ef` [repair] Melo (system report) is the retry-economics anchor the literature otherwise lacks: retry loop's true cost is latency arithmetic (a rare +4.1 s amortized / +15.9 s triggered measured pair), its benefit is converting otherwise-empty sessions into usable ones, and its failure mode is silent constraint degradation (23% of recoveries) — a production retry must be evaluated on recovery AND preservation, not recovery alone.
  links: 22abb140e3c15e39, 15b82401938c5a93, 1f894d76e5789412
- `9f8b2d401403a107` [general] Official CI pattern: keep a project warnings-clean via build.warnings = "deny" (config) or CARGO_BUILD_WARNINGS=deny (env), then run cargo clippy --all-targets --all-features --keep-going.
  links: 0658c76662a6a80e, 8605993e67ae595e, 81601e15dda67071
- `9fac6494bbf5da78` [general] Codex builds its instruction chain once per run: global ~/.codex/AGENTS.md (or AGENTS.override.md), then project files walking from project root down to cwd (one file per directory), concatenated root-down, deeper files override earlier; combined size capped at project_doc_max_bytes (32 KiB default).
  links: 0d0774d31bed17b3, d6482a9be6ea492f, 9a41828e542b1154
- `a49b169111deb842` [agent-behavior] Behavioral guideline (Karpathy): think before coding — state assumptions explicitly, present multiple interpretations instead of picking silently, push back when a simpler approach exists, and stop + name the confusion when something is unclear. enforced_by: review rubric (misdirection/missing tradeoff = FIX-MINOR)
  links: 4992782a5790d742, fa58509d3523cc84, a588d4401a139d71
- `a588d4401a139d71` [agent-behavior] Behavioral guideline (Karpathy): goal-driven execution — transform tasks into verifiable goals (validation -> tests first), state a brief per-step plan with a verify check per step, and loop only until verified. enforced_by: review rubric (claiming without evidence = REWORK)
  links: a49b169111deb842, 4992782a5790d742, fa58509d3523cc84
- `a602df8d9b46c6b5` [strategy] Long-running agent loops need two ingredients: (1) effectively unlimited token supply (account rotation), and (2) a work ledger (Beads): a version-controlled, audit-trailed, queryable graph of work units with dependency/parent edges, atomic claiming/leasing, gates and triggers; when a session crashes or hands off, the next session reads the ledger and continues.
  links: 0ff9b686f9e72fc3, 23db60932cbf376c, b91eeab1a9452dee
- `acbe5b346b26c040` [general] Zep/Graphiti (arXiv:2501.13956) on LongMemEval-S (~115K-token conversations): response latency 3.20s (gpt-4o-mini) / 2.58s (gpt-4o) vs full-context 31.3s / 28.9s = ~90% latency reduction while avg context drops 115K -> 1.6K tokens (Zep Table 2, §4.3.2).
  links: b6fef0270ed8d28d, cba421bad5c5a34c, 15116661844082ce
- `af500e05f86d5f38` [general] pytest.mark.xfail with strict=False can be used as a manual quarantine so a test's failure does not break the whole build, though it is rather dangerous to use permanently; the pytest API reference says strict=False is particularly useful to mark flaky tests (random failures) to be tackled later.
  links: 3880083953a8f0d3, 24f290b647ef095e, d6354b5974db8518
- `b2fee6b8038589fb` [general] Anthropic's multi-agent research system avoids a shared store: subagents have distinct tools/prompts/trajectories, write large outputs to a filesystem and pass only references to the coordinator; the shared context is the coordinator's compressed summary, minimizing the 'game of telephone'. [pattern, fact-sourced]
  links: b364a47fb85065c7, 3c50f0967b34b231, ae9c67ba8d9f53fa
- `b556d77f45006ee2` [general] LangGraph durability is tunable: "exit" persists only when execution exits (mid-run crash loses state), "async" persists while the next step executes (small risk of no checkpoint if the process crashes), "sync" persists synchronously before the next step starts — an explicit durability-vs-throughput trade mirroring WAL synchronous settings.
  links: 5ff5c00c83663882, 8baa3b2f82d7faa6, 8c506f6dd07767f9
- `baf9ded88a16fc68` [repair] LLM-judge benchmark failures (Vaghasiya et al.): rubric drift, hallucinated completion, answer-only scoring, run-to-run variance — 23 repeated LiveMCPBench runs of the same setup scored 57.9–76.8% (mean 69.4%, σ=5.4 pp, spread 18.9 pp), larger than most leaderboard margins (judge regenerates task key-points per run; default judge gpt-4.1-mini weaker than the paper's recommendation).
  links: cb3c088728b3c87c, 7b151a7d20f87ecb, bcac82d5414851c8
- `bbbe2b85e6d0db1a` [general] Wasteland (Mar 2026) is a federation feature: link Gas Towns over the shared ledger, post/claim work on a wanted board, and earn multi-dimensional stamps (quality/reliability/creativity) that compose into a portable character sheet (reputation from real work).
  links: 0ff9b686f9e72fc3, 1691fdd86c2e2438, b4ee49de2637dd9e
- `c436a95d905a7aa0` [general] dbt unique_key turns pure append into upsert semantics (update-or-ignore on match, insert on new), required to avoid bringing modified records in as duplicates; without it most adapters result in append-only behavior. incremental_predicates push predicates into the merge to limit scans of the existing table.
  links: 93aab397ab2425d6, 7b3058140a429de1, e761f79662ed051f
- `cf99d7577e330864` [general] Reported consolidation numbers across the research are self-reported on benchmark-specific setups (LoCoMo, LongMemEval, DMR, MemAgentBench, HaluMem); there is no cross-system same-harness comparison of consolidation correctness, and authority-preservation survival in a graph-based store (vs flat facts) is only tested in the flat-fact setting.
  links: 832a7c538944c10a, 9fd23bfe4eb2af7f, 3b3bae3fd783ee40
- `d2375864da7ee620` [general] Google defines a flaky test result as a test exhibiting both a passing and a failing result with the same code, and reports a continual rate of about 1.5% of all test runs being 'flaky', almost 16% of tests having some flakiness, and about 84% of observed pass-to-fail transitions involving a flaky test; these figures are single-company 2016 self-reports. A second Google tool detects changes in a test's flakiness level and works to identify the change that caused it.
  links: e6b84c294456ecd4, 3158dabb8476e655, f8edba98b002ec5d
- `dcff2b5ab8c2f74e` [ai-auditability] Opinion: in the EU AI Act these layers are combined by law, not alternatives — Art 12 mandates an automatic decision log; Art 14/26 require that human-oversight and biometric authorisation events be captured (as approvals inside the logs, recital 72); Art 19/26(6) set retention; Art 21 gives authorities log access.
  links: 8baa3b2f82d7faa6, 30a1f40562cac11e, 77ad6114bf5546c2
- `dd3d81d3e6c5124a` [general] Long-context/retrieval gains are large relative to base LLMs but far from human: LoCoMo improvements of 22-66% from long-context/RAG yet still lag human by 56%; human overall F1 87.9 vs best long-context 37.8 (GPT-3.5-turbo-16k @16k tokens) vs best RAG 41.4. Source: arXiv:2402.17753, Tables 2-3 + Section 6.
  links: 2372968ceac568ce, 65c5ee1c3d7613bb, 19ef42b700d36e05

Fixed only on kernels reporting the errata bit.
  links: 1711f8ebaaa6ff1c, d6f4e1893008deef, 356b94ef83a50a9a
- `ddfc343faef6bd2c` [general] A statistically calibrated generative LM must hallucinate on 'arbitrary' facts appearing once in the training data, at a rate bounded below by a Good-Turing estimate, independent of architecture or data quality; mitigation requires post-training or selective behavior, not better calibration alone. (Kalai & Vempala, STOC 2024; verified abstract)
  links: 74a333f06b0e1b1b, 6cf5f705aaedf39d, 1ab99428a950f6a4
- `e03589b07f291e50` [repair] Selective triggering / patch economics (measured): GGC gate (SPARQL, MCQA) gen-only 90.23 → correct-all 92.34 → selective 98.33, gate FPR 0.31%, trigger rate 9.31%, 77.7% of errors semantic, ~45% less inference; StepCache latency 2.13→0.67 s, tokens 36.1k→27.3k, correctness 72.5→100%.
  links: cee7417852b29375, 4cf12f9a8fbae069, f4596f48ade2d79f
- `e9faf4c799927644` [general] Cross-paper comparison of LLM routers is apples-to-oranges: different model pools, different quality metrics (BART score vs accuracy vs LLM-judge), different cost assumptions (e.g., RouteLLM's GPT-4/Mixtral ~100× ratio), and 2023–2025 price baselines. Confidence-threshold cascade papers (FrugalGPT, 2502.11021) report large savings but on few datasets and with authors' own thresholds.
  links: 53b44257c13294e1, 300485f9871ece7c, 4ca81b227fadc600
- `ebc840082d1b992d` [general] Hybrid (dense + sparse) and fused (dense + sparse + multi-vector) retrieval consistently beat each single signal: M3-Embedding MIRACL nDCG@10 Dense 69.2 / Sparse 53.9 / Multi-vec 70.5 / Dense+Sparse 70.4 / All 71.5; long-doc MLDR Dense 52.5 / Sparse 62.2 / Dense+Sparse 64.8 / All 65.0; NarrativeQA Dense 48.7 / Sparse 57.5 / Multi-vec 55.4 / Dense+Sparse 60.1 / All 61.7. Source: arXiv:2402.03216, Tables 1, 3, 4.
  links: 955b38582633ba0a, e18bd137f36455e4, 635c8cbfd002fa86
- `ed80831515a47d10` [general] Materialize distinguishes plain views (results recomputed from scratch each access) from indexed and materialized views, whose results are persisted in durable storage and incrementally updated as new data arrives; indexed views keep the incrementally-maintained result in cluster-local memory.
  links: 266b8325088b1614, 855675ab421a6d0b, 8ab263a3dc129e33
- `f0389e3a1b973eae` [general] The OpenAI Agents SDK traces every run as a trace (workflow_name, trace_id, optional group_id to link multiple traces from the same conversation, metadata) composed of spans linked by trace_id/parent_id; agent runs are wrapped in agent_span(), handoffs in handoff_span(), tool calls in function_span(), LLM calls in generation_span(), guardrails in guardrail_span().
  links: 59054a5c043328ad, 54f9fe2c40ea09b0, 4ac0d73a111f7e22
- `f0576e80a8acf6c5` [general] PRAGMA synchronous=OFF can corrupt an SQLite database on OS crash/power loss; SQLite should always be run with its default synchronous setting of FULL; WAL is more forgiving of out-of-order writes than rollback mode (only checkpoint needs a correct sync).
  links: 87458eb593e72446, 22b21011428f4c33, a7f8334d85e65d51
- `f0cad0a342fce769` [general] LightMem (arXiv:2510.18866, ICLR 2026) total (online+offline) token usage on LongMemEval-S, GPT-4o-mini: A-MEM 1,605.8K / MemoryOS 2,991.8K / Mem0 1,152.6K vs LightMem(r=0.7,th=512) 28.25K = 10x-38x fewer total tokens (GPT), 6.9x-21.8x (Qwen) vs baselines (LightMem Table 2).
  links: 26789dfb6ee09b3f, fe6a2d93dd65d35c, d3c25848ca456b8e
- `f7b7247ebe13d8e0` [general] Showing a model's confidence score alone calibrated people's trust and improved their performance in a mortgage-lending decision task; local explanations did not reliably add benefit, and improved trust calibration was not sufficient for accuracy gains — experts still had to bring knowledge complementary to the AI's errors. (Zhang, Liao, Bellamy 2020, FAT*)
  links: 095902827fb17ae8, 6e30b9e0905d102a, 93d3f6c1ebd2262f
- `fa58509d3523cc84` [agent-behavior] Behavioral guideline (Karpathy): surgical changes — touch only what the request demands; do not improve adjacent code/comments/formatting, do not refactor what is not broken, match existing style, and mention (not delete) unrelated dead code. enforced_by: review rubric (scope creep = FIX-MINOR)
  links: a49b169111deb842, 4992782a5790d742, a588d4401a139d71
- `fac277b31fa74875` [general] Bazel `--runs_per_test=N` runs every test N times and fails the test if any attempt fails; `--runs_per_test_detects_flakes` (default false) marks a shard FLAKY if at least one run passes and at least one fails.

  links: 16f2c921fa480440, 8ab263a3dc129e33
- `00f1658f7d8f50f6` [repair] Retry infrastructure (measured): retry storms from rate limits are preventable with jittered exponential backoff (ContinuityBench 99.20% continuity, N=750, vs stateless ~0%); unchecked retry loops are a documented $-thousands production failure class (63 incidents, 21 frameworks — Khan); agentic orchestration multiplies energy per successful goal 4.33× vs linear (Panigrahy et al.).
  links: cec104f546b03f85, b023dfa34d7b6603
- `028e8effd0ed2845` [general] LongMemEval (arXiv:2410.10813 Fig 3a): commercial memory systems on histories ~10x shorter: ChatGPT (GPT-4o) 0.5773 vs offline reading 0.9184 (37% drop); Coze 0.3299 (64% drop).
  links: 50662f026de1acfe, 0e8c9801a605c550

  links: 486c92d91a5c5d33, a4b2703c22963a98

KU error analysis states the retriever was only able to identify the updated knowledge but failed to retrieve the previous information before update, counted as retrieval failure.
  links: 0e8c9801a605c550, 348e2784f9115e96

  links: 9f8b2d401403a107, 8605993e67ae595e
- `073a5ff97a268f5f` [general] A-MEM (arXiv:2502.12110 Table 4) retrieval time grows from 0.31 microseconds (1,000 memories) to 3.70 microseconds (1,000,000 memories) with linear O(N) memory (1.46 MB to 1464.84 MB); per-operation cost about 1,200 tokens vs about 16,900 for MemGPT/LoCoMo baselines (85-93% reduction), measured by the authors on their own system.
  links: a6c8b00124bf07c1, e9488afde6379a28

  links: 71bfdcb5d6aea1fa, 7d97975df00109c5
- `0ad6075069f5864a` [general] Complementarity has narrow preconditions: a hybrid human+AI pair beats both human-only and machine-only ensembles only when the two classifiers perform within a bounded accuracy band, and the key limiting factor is the latent correlation between human and machine confidence (observed ~0.4 for human+AI vs ~0.7 human-human); effective AI advice should be accurate and statistically independent of human judgment. (Steyvers et al., PNAS 2022)
  links: fcfc5df953031f06, 6e30b9e0905d102a
- `0b44c39d153c71e2` [general] A biologically-grounded agent memory architecture combines sleep-phase consolidation, interference-based forgetting, engram maturation, reconsolidation upon retrieval, entity knowledge graphs, and hybrid multi-cue retrieval; deduplication-based consolidation achieved 97.2% retention precision with 58% store reduction (+21.8 pp over baseline). Source: 'Human-Inspired Memory Architecture for LLM Agents', arXiv 2605.08538.
  links: b6fef0270ed8d28d, 15116661844082ce
- `0f28c5715a50722b` [general] Checkpoint-style run state has a documented scaling cost: 'Over long conversations, checkpoints accumulate. This can increase latency and storage costs. Fix: prune old checkpoints or set a retention policy.'
  links: a495d4c500ec01b5, e268c955dcf1bd2f
- `0fb38c56ff147e30` [general] AgentIR (2026 preprint) reports on LoCoMo (n=1,982) that BM25 alone is already the strongest single system and a hybrid cascade can skip the dense channel with no accuracy loss — this is an estimate from a single 2026 preprint, not independently verified. Source: arXiv:2605.25092 abstract.
  links: 1412c255c7aa37da, 97b9c2be82edddfc
- `12fe0bcb03d20370` [general] Microsoft durable agents persist session state in external storage (Durable Task / Azure Storage) specifically to get multi-instance scale-out (any worker can resume a session), crash recovery, and human-in-the-loop waits of days/weeks with zero compute.
  links: 8baa3b2f82d7faa6, 28a317b7a861c29c
- `15dffb87cdfc3f1c` [general] Mem0 Dream supersede marks an older memory as superseded and links it to the newer fact; superseded memories are not deleted and not hidden by default (normal search/get still returns them, badged as superseded); latest_only=true filters them out; runs as memories are added on every plan.
  links: a1d9c54ea313be45, bd7bc6f9009fc641
- `1696dd59343bd054` [general] Kubernetes impersonation records both identities in the audit event: the authenticated caller in user and the impersonated identity in impersonatedUser, e.g. user system:serviceaccount:default:my-controller with impersonatedUser jane.doe@example.com; an audit event is logged for each impersonation request.
  links: 832663c967f96aa0, 87e145dc13717659
- `19fdc143f081ed4f` [general] Oracle offers three refresh timings: ON COMMIT (refresh as part of commit on a master table, may increase commit time), ON DEMAND (manual via DBMS_MVIEW, default), and ON STATEMENT (per-DML; only for fast-refreshable star/snowflake views that include the fact table ROWID).
  links: e1f2e874ff8f4e1d, 1a44ed072b912b06
- `1ee6755802152b1d` [general] OpenAI collaborated with SWE-bench authors to build a Docker-containerized harness because SWE-bench environments were 'difficult to reliably set up... inadvertently causing unit tests to fail regardless of the solution'.
  links: e51f44d00affc6cb, beb33e0d0a29d161
- `20b4f75f5aab7cb5` [repair] Verification feasibility bound (CRC, Kotte, [69]; theoretical): with base risk μ above the target α on the task-model pair, no verifier or abstention layer can certify the target without abstaining on ≥(μ−α)/(1−α) of outputs — NER/QA/CLS uncertifiable at α=0.10 (0% certifiable), 47/40/60% at α=0.40; JSON μ≈0 certifies at 0 abstention; heuristic abstention misses targets 7.5–12.5% — 'guaranteed ≤X% error' claims for high-base-risk tasks are only meaningful with an explicitly stated abstention rate.
  links: 2512869e54f72f96, 84c8cabac4c26d5f
- `22b21011428f4c33` [general] SQLite recovery breaks if journals are interfered with: if hot journal files are moved, deleted, or renamed after a crash or power failure, automatic recovery will not work and the database may go corrupt.
  links: f0576e80a8acf6c5, 5f26c9330e251481
- `246f64abc4d8d6c8` [general] rust-toolchain.toml pins channel, components, and targets per-project and is suitable to check in to source control; the rustup book notes that if the toolchain is pinned to a specific release, Cargo.lock should probably be tracked too.
  links: 8605993e67ae595e, 81601e15dda67071
- `26789dfb6ee09b3f` [general] LightMem's online-only cost (soft updates, no offline pass) is up to 105.9x (GPT) / 117.1x (Qwen) token reduction and 159.4x / 309.9x fewer API calls vs baselines (LightMem Abstract, §5.2).
  links: f0cad0a342fce769, fe6a2d93dd65d35c
- `26a851d4bc8775f8` [general] MCP auth applies to HTTP transports (server SHOULD conform); STDIO implementations SHOULD NOT use the OAuth spec and instead take credentials from the environment; all authorization-server endpoints must be HTTPS, redirect URIs must be localhost or HTTPS, PKCE S256 is MUST, and clients must refuse to proceed if code_challenge_methods_supported is absent (Spec 2026-07-28).
  links: c81957cf3aac4c06, 57ed22f30f0c6760
- `2a637dd506865c01` [general] opencode identity is the resolved config stack: remote (.well-known/opencode), global, custom (OPENCODE_CONFIG), project, .opencode/, inline, managed (MDM, non-overridable); project config overrides global, managed overrides all.
  links: d6482a9be6ea492f, d39c4dd366a95d2e
- `2cd8c890524c508b` [general] Unverified: whether a given distribution kernel exposes Landlock at boot (LSM ordering) is host-specific, not answered by the documented sources. Kernel-doc 'future Landlock evolutions' claims (e.g. restricting chdir/stat/...) are direction statements, not commitments.
  links: 1b0ebc092cb2ebe8, a2d0d1644fe1d3d4
- `2ce7c350e0974c56` [general] Official caveat: CI can fail due to new toolchain versions because there are limited compatibility guarantees around warnings; pinning the toolchain version with an automated toolchain-upgrade PR job is recommended.
  links: 81601e15dda67071, 0f1a1f2be8e214d4
- `2d77bf15f0089c2f` [general] LangSmith's per-run reviewer thresholds and reservation locking prevent two reviewers from grading the same comparison simultaneously in pairwise queues.
  links: 1d998782a2b7e1ae, 4d21686cc85b19ee
- `304e41006f05da56` [general] LangGraph time travel lets an operator list checkpoints (get_history), mutate state (update_state), and resume from the edited checkpoint.
  links: 7fcceed280fcc7b0, 8baa3b2f82d7faa6
- `3286897269792642` [general] Rust SDK (rmcp) CVE-2026-63127 (GHSA-33f5-2c5q-wgwj, CVSS 8.2): missing OAuth Protected Resource resource validation lets a malicious server redirect the flow to a legitimate AS and steal tokens; patched in 2.0.0.
  links: c81957cf3aac4c06, 6d2f6996dd28f2d0
- `34fc1c71481976d6` [general] mcp-hub's scripts/release.sh mechanically enforces semver: must run on main with a clean tree, bumps via npm --no-git-tag-version version patch|minor|major, gates the release on a matching ## [new-version] CHANGELOG.md entry (aborts and reverts otherwise), creates annotated tag vX.Y.Z, a single chore(release): vX.Y.Z commit, npm publish, and a GitHub release.
  links: 75648dd5d0625730, f2c01f5064674e32
- `3758f98022d19fbb` [general] SQLite database files are ordinary disk files; any process can open the file and overwrite it with garbage, there is nothing the SQLite library can do to defend against this, and there is no built-in access control — protection is whatever the OS filesystem permissions provide.
  links: d86f43007ae0e974, 3e5e18d029069f90
- `3aee4552498f51e4` [general] GCP audit logs carry the caller identity in AuditLog.authenticationInfo (principalEmail) and network origin in RequestMetadata.callerIp; for service-to-service calls inside Google's production network callerIp is redacted to 'private', and this includes calls made by Google-owned service accounts (service agents) even when initiated by a user.
  links: 00aadbc4be76ab80, bdf2aeb421403f14
- `3b19ffa7c3f7be90` [general] Sleep-time Compute (Letta, arXiv:2504.13171) targets latency by doing inference offline before queries, claiming you can 'respond at the accuracy of standard test-time compute but with far lower latencies'; it reduces test-time tokens needed to reach the same accuracy by ~5x (Stateful GSM-Symbolic, Stateful AIME) (Abstract, §5.1).
  links: 31f481abaa8a4578, b023dfa34d7b6603
- `3bdf61d670b97831` [general] gen_ai.operation.name well-known values (snapshot of main on fetch date; the enumeration will grow over time): chat, create_agent, create_memory, create_memory_store, delete_memory, delete_memory_store, embeddings, execute_tool, fetch_response, generate_content, invoke_agent, invoke_workflow, plan, retrieval, search_memory, text_completion, update_memory, upsert_memory.
  links: ccecea1af23feed5, 2eea1cc6e5d5294f
- `3c52730566932ab6` [general] SQL Server indexed views are views with a unique clustered index; the optimizer can consider the view for substitution without it being referenced in the query, and DML on base tables must update referenced indexed views, so DML query performance can degrade significantly.
  links: 1a44ed072b912b06, 8ab263a3dc129e33
- `3d4cc0a018b20c21` [evidence-status] mini-agi monitors disagreement rate as judge-drift: `eval judge-drift` computes how often judged outcomes disagree with the deterministic layer against a calibration corpus; below a precision floor it appends a recalibration trigger to the audit log. ADR-0011 names Self-Taught Evaluators (arXiv:2408.02666) as the underlying idea.
  links: 9f6d5016691fd195, 8af8f2689671ac0a
- `3e2267fb03bf9e44` [general] The closest things to evidence in the reached sources are (a) Miri demonstrably catching a concrete UB in the Book's worked example, and (b) the cargo-fuzz trophy-case list — both are existence proofs, not efficacy comparisons.
  links: ecda50a3261745b3, 91c77eb4f98dfb9a
- `3f8680deb624fd36` [general] In MCP, command execution to launch STDIO servers, server side effects (filesystem, git, DB, network, system commands), resource access, and LLM-driven tool invocation are declared intended behaviors, not reportable vulnerabilities (SECURITY.md).
  links: af0d9ca54cec9c18, c3484bbb30701da2
- `3faec3e0b1d80803` [general] pg_ivm keeps incremental maintenance efficient via an automatically-created unique index (on GROUP BY columns, DISTINCT target list, or base-table primary keys) used as scan keys to locate affected rows.
  links: 6dbd271b7ce0e25e, 8ab263a3dc129e33
- `40120af48e7950b9` [testing] mini-agi's audit_verifier_vacuous refuses to trust a verifier it could not test against a counterfactual: if the empty-dir run cannot be set up or executed, the audit errors rather than trusting the gate (crates/mini-agi-core/src/verifier.rs:246-278).
  links: eb30bfc42085c15c, 439ade46d55d5c15
- `49b7fb8236915e10` [general] Braintrust stores each reviewer's scores independently; continuous/categorical scores are averaged on the parent span, free-form scores require a reviewer (or admin) to pick a value via "Set as base span value", and an "N total reviews" indicator shows agreement vs. divergence; Braintrust explicitly does not support blind review.
  links: 7f9242706e36c133, 693010a524f0a9fb
- `4ba0795d7351a698` [general] Claude Code treats CLAUDE.md as context, not enforced configuration; to block an action regardless of what Claude decides, a PreToolUse hook is required; enforcement lives in settings (permissions, sandbox, hooks) enforced by the client and pushable to the managed scope.
  links: 8eff920971cc7319, 900ff2712391e9e4
- `5025c0afbe426e17` [general] CrewAI serializes concurrent writers at the backend: LanceDB operations are serialized with a shared lock and retried automatically on conflict, handling multiple Memory instances pointing at the same database. [fact]
  links: 866588d8e2efd4e0, 40a6f5c1783caef7
- `510db6dc2a1720ad` [general] SQLite WAL does not work over a network filesystem; all processes using a database must be on the same host computer because the wal-index lives in a shared-memory mmap.
  links: d86f43007ae0e974, 3e5e18d029069f90
- `536b91e7628f3054` [repair] IBM CW cost anchors (point estimates only; no per-cell CIs/significance reported): $0.008–$0.20/workflow; mistral-small 95.7% at $0.010/workflow, gpt-oss-120b 97.8% at $0.016, mistral-medium 96.1% at a 19× cost premium ($0.197; 0.4 pp gain), llama-4-maverick 90.5% at $0.024 and fastest (33.6 s); annualized at 1,000/day: mistral-small ≈$3,700/yr vs mistral-medium ≈$71,900/yr; latency is the piecewise cost (mistral-small 34.4→42.6 s, llama-3.3-70B 76.2→151.4 s with generation rate declining 95.3%→81.5%; gpt-oss-120b sole exception 99.3→66.2 s).
  links: 135c69977d75322e, f4596f48ade2d79f
- `541a91d2c3223ea4` [general] Cache poisoning threat model: only push, workflow_dispatch, repository_dispatch, delete, registry_package, page_build, and schedule may create/overwrite default-branch-scope caches; cache contents are unsigned and any workflow run that can read a cache may extract its contents, leading to malicious code execution.
  links: 081b26a9420810cb, 9dd4e21efea78cca
- `5480bd685bff8004` [repair] SQL Query Engine (Ijaz, arXiv:2604.16511; system report): two-stage NL→PostgreSQL pipeline with a five-strategy response-parser cascade (JSON → embedded JSON → code block → SELECT regex → raw text; tolerant parsing instead of schema enforcement) and an execution-grounded self-healing loop feeding full PostgreSQL SQLSTATE diagnostics back to the LLM.
  links: 79088f70c96759ad, 6e1f98afd067dae7
- `56702ea84de54b43` [general] Develocity/Gradle warns retry-only flake handling is not a complete solution: flaky tests go unnoticed and you will inevitably accrue more flaky tests. (gradle.com/blog/flaky-tests/)
  links: 3880083953a8f0d3, d6354b5974db8518
- `57d4732c1ffb43bf` [repair] Characterizing Faults in Agentic AI (Shah et al., arXiv:2603.06847; empirical fault taxonomy, verified in full text): 13,602 closed issues/merged PRs from 40 open-source agentic-AI repos (>1,000 stars), stratified-sampled to 385 faults, grounded-theory taxonomy (5 architectural dimensions, 13 symptom classes, 12 root-cause categories), validated by a 145-practitioner study (mean relevance 3.97/5, Cronbach's α=0.904, 83.8% had personally encountered the covered faults).
  links: c1a0b768075c2467, e5a6d2ccb1b2bd7a
- `59054a5c043328ad` [general] OpenAI Agents SDK tracing records generation/function/tool spans with usage by default; in long-running workers the batch exporter flushes every few seconds, so for per-job delivery guarantees call flush_traces() after the trace context exits.
  links: 50d864bb40f726a7, f0389e3a1b973eae
- `59e0420458d34317` [general] Seccomp no_new_privs requirement documents the setuid attack: the filter may be malicious against a setuid program — e.g. making setuid() appear to succeed without doing it, tricking a privileged program into keeping superuser privileges — which is why PR_SET_NO_NEW_PRIVS (or CAP_SYS_ADMIN) is mandatory.
  links: d71aa36a9e81bc8c, 3f11178717f2bfc4
- `5b306c225ec47f04` [general] POSIX advisory locking is buggy/unreliable on NFS; two or more clients might try to modify the same part of the same SQLite database at the same time, resulting in corruption.
  links: 344882f912fc587f, 3e5e18d029069f90
- `5f26c9330e251481` [general] At the SQLite layer crash recovery is the automatic hot-journal / WAL replay; framework-layer checkpoint/resume sits on top of that file-level guarantee.
  links: 22b21011428f4c33, 8baa3b2f82d7faa6
- `60fdbe45190d2e94` [general] pg_ivm README shows a doc example where a single-row update costs ~20.6 s via full refresh vs ~15 ms as a base-table write for an incremental MV (doc-reported example timings).
  links: ae4ee7c4c7369b1f, 5d32c1280761f55f
- `691b74f6eacc0b29` [general] On ALFWorld (134 text-world tasks), ReAct+Reflexion completed 130/134 tasks vs ReAct-only which stalled; the paper reports an absolute +22% improvement over strong baselines within 12 iterative trials (Reflexion §4.1).
  links: 83f1964c6217fb0d, 9240d1f949e33e15
- `69618f21014c3ad5` [general] A single cross-vendor 'agent identity' standard for web audit trails is not verifiable from the sources reached: the proposed 'Agent ID' specification (agent-id GitHub org and agent-id.github.io) returned 404/empty; its existence/status is unknown — not verifiable.
  links: 3b3bae3fd783ee40, a2d0d1644fe1d3d4

  links: adbc0cde1ae47dc7, edac6d9654eefceb
- `6d07ba987c2ea213` [general] Verbalized confidence: a GPT-3 model trained to express '90% confidence'/'high confidence' in words produces well-calibrated probabilities without using model logits and stays 'moderately calibrated under distribution shift.' First-party OpenAI result; basis for LLM verbal-confidence features. (Lin, Hilton, Evans 2022, arXiv 2205.14334; verified abstract)
  links: 2512869e54f72f96, a2d0d1644fe1d3d4
- `6ed4eb78bc0729b3` [general] Mem0 default add path is ADD-only extraction: a new fact is stored without silently rewriting an older one; explicit update or delete operations are used to correct or remove a memory.
  links: ccecea1af23feed5, 336453c5de421eee
- `7007a70aaca3b4a3` [general] Langfuse sessions group traces/observations into one session for replay of the full interaction.
  links: 8fb8a22a126c8ff7, 8e916f9507714e83
- `7099cb2b4578c725` [general] Prompt-cache economics: compaction invalidates the cached conversation prefix by design; a warm mid-session /compact reads the prefix from cache so it 'costs a fraction of what the context size suggests'; /rewind reuses an already-cached prefix; after long breaks Claude Code offers 'resume from a summary'. (Claude Code prompt-caching docs)
  links: 4ca81b227fadc600, e7777e2701a56d30
- `71b0fc0c3cc953e2` [general] LongMemEval (arXiv:2410.10813, ICLR 2025): long-context LLMs lose 30-60% accuracy reading the full LongMemEvalS history vs oracle retrieval; GPT-4o 0.870 oracle to 0.606 full-history (30.3% drop).
  links: 0b6bcd41fb0c4fef, 0e8c9801a605c550

  links: ddf4bfc7760221e4, e232f1c2ce57f2cf
- `75149e672ee23e31` [general] Letta sleep-time compute (2025) is idle-time batch consolidation: two agents, the sleep-time agent owns the memory-edit tools and rewrites the primary agent's in-context memory blocks; it is "the closest published analogue to 'nightly'" [S3].
  links: a1cb6948c6bd72ed, ecda50a3261745b3
- `7c6f0eb74c7ce6fc` [general] This repo's ADR-0012 chose Landlock write-containment for the worker (read+execute on the tree, write confined to workdir/state) over a full namespace sandbox, citing 'no root, no user namespaces, no external binary' and the binding risk being filesystem writes rather than network egress. The threat-model ranking ('writes are the real risk') is repo opinion.
  links: bfe28bd5ddad1fe0, 980038e5090eec8b
- `7c86cd916f6513f8` [general] SQLite lists 'very large datasets' and 'many concurrent writers' as cases where a client/server RDBMS 'may work better.'
  links: 601ae8b56f00daf0, 86bb11459302600d
- `7cfe9661cd15e22a` [general] LangSmith alert noise controls documented in-source are PagerDuty dedup (to receive the same alert again within an hour you must resolve the active incident) and a webhook payload alert_rule_id documented as a de-duplication key in the webhook service; best practices listed are starting with broader thresholds, adjusting sensitivity to criticality, and routing to appropriate on-call.
  links: fe7f8b67b4c17d62, daf69938aa31c832
- `7e6f8bd91c5c7679` [ai-auditability] W3C VC Data Integrity: cryptographic proofs make 'documents and data tamper-evident'; Proof Sets are unordered multiple signatures, Proof Chains preserve order via previousProof (spec example: a notary counter-signing), and proofPurpose constrains what a proof may be used for.
  links: 3980f1133d5b8a7d, d1b3d3ef01bfcfff
- `7f9242706e36c133` [general] LangSmith annotation-queue collaborator settings let an item be held until every workspace member marks it Done, or require a set number of reviewers per run, and can name assigned reviewers explicitly; reservations lock an item to a reviewer for a set time and expire/release it if the reviewer leaves without marking Done, so the number of items visible to each reviewer can differ from the total queue size.
  links: 49b7fb8236915e10, 4d21686cc85b19ee
- `8014b8b82d793b73` [general] A-MEM (Zettelkasten-style agentic memory) creates structured notes with links and enables memory evolution: integrating a new memory can trigger updates to contextual representations/attributes of existing memories. Source: NeurIPS 2025, arXiv 2502.12110.
  links: de06970103940658, 179f80e4011804dd
- `8097fca3fceebe45` [general] PostgreSQL REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY still re-executes the full backing query; the option only avoids locking concurrent readers, requires a plain UNIQUE index covering all rows, and works only when the view is already populated.
  links: 1f4b9fc62486cff6, e1f2e874ff8f4e1d
- `8402661c95f9d43c` [general] The semantic-conventions-genai repo covers GenAI (LLM, agent, embeddings, retrieval) and MCP operations; shared attributes not specific to GenAI (e.g. server.address, error.type, db.*) continue to live in the upstream semantic-conventions repo, pinned at a version (docs reference v1.44.0 / spec v1.56.0).
  links: ccecea1af23feed5, 4c19d9b8fed90b2c
- `854c0680bf360524` [general] MCP tools: servers MUST validate all tool inputs, implement access controls, rate-limit tool invocations, and sanitize tool outputs; clients SHOULD prompt for confirmation on sensitive operations, show inputs before calling, validate results before passing to the LLM, implement timeouts, and log usage (Spec 2026-07-28 Tools).
  links: e232f1c2ce57f2cf, fb854e84fe169d26
- `866588d8e2efd4e0` [general] SQLite allows multiple processes to do SELECT at the same time, but only one process can make changes to the database at any moment; it uses reader/writer locks and writers lock the whole database file during their update; on lock conflict the default is SQLITE_BUSY, adjustable via a busy handler/timeout.
  links: 344882f912fc587f, 5025c0afbe426e17
- `875c47689a1e1a8c` [repair] Flat Score, Amplified Failures (Jang et al., arXiv:2607.27275; τ²-bench, ten cells: two open-weight families × {dense, MoE} × two domains, 456 episodes per arm, 4-bit AWQ): 4-bit quantization produced a statistically flat final score in every cell (no change survives multiple-comparison correction; seven cells TOST-equivalent at ±7.5 pp) yet in the damaged cell (Gemma-4-31B, telecom) raised tool-name hallucination from 19.51% → 38.26% of agent tool calls (+18.75 pp, CI [14.0,23.6]) — a 2.5× growth in event volume (649→1,646) — while creating essentially no new failures (BF16/INT4 hallucinated-name distributions correlate 0.97; 23/24 invented names are real user-side tools — 'role-boundary confusion,' amplified not invented).
  links: b25f3d9404e1c402, a4be8759582ea228

  links: 9e2e2cacd723d017, 5aa99c3328323465

  links: 9b9296bb8b2fc470, 8ab263a3dc129e33

  links: 5ff5c00c83663882, b556d77f45006ee2
- `8e2d3e5a027dd99b` [ai-auditability] The KEP process's approver/reviewer approval is itself the recorded gate (approvers and reviewers for making decisions; a discoverable record), i.e., an approval step persisted as part of the decision record.
  links: 3d35f4dd5278a6c4, edac6d9654eefceb
- `8e916f9507714e83` [general] For supervising parallel agents, grouping surfaces (OpenAI group_id/workflow_name, Langfuse sessions, HumanLayer task/session views) are the documented answer; effectiveness of these mechanisms is unmeasured inference, not a primary-source claim.
  links: 7007a70aaca3b4a3, c94749b55ef6938d

  links: 7007a70aaca3b4a3, 4d21686cc85b19ee

  links: 0f1a1f2be8e214d4, 3e2267fb03bf9e44

  links: 8313bde3b0ba0e0b, ea85a2da072f7a6a

  links: ebc840082d1b992d, 832a7c538944c10a

  links: fe22d29d30fbcaf7, ecd4a6522505f64f
- `973e990075c8a7fd` [general] MAST (arXiv 2503.13657) was derived by Grounded Theory analysis of 150 traces across 5 frameworks, refined via 3 inter-annotator rounds (kappa=0.88), then LLM-as-judge annotation of 1,642 traces (kappa=0.77 vs. humans).
  links: 57c6cc3485ade4ed, c1a0b768075c2467
- `9a4df56f7d846a01` [general] Semver consequences of API design are catalogued in the Cargo Book: adding a non-defaulted trait item, changing trait signatures, tightening generic bounds, and adding enum variants without non_exhaustive are Major; adding a defaulted trait item or defaulted type parameter are Minor or Possibly-breaking; sealed traits and #[non_exhaustive] are cited as mitigation strategies.
  links: 12bee8131387de68, 75648dd5d0625730
- `9cc983109ba5b4f3` [general] CoALA never uses the term 'consolidation' — verified in full text; the concept maps to three distinct mechanisms: per-agent reflection written back to semantic memory (Reflexion/Generative Agents), save-time pipeline dedup (CrewAI), and background system-owned consolidation (Mem0 Dream). [fact]
  links: a1cb6948c6bd72ed, 93aab397ab2425d6
- `9dd4e21efea78cca` [general] Read-only cache saves do not fail the job: in low-trust contexts a denied save logs a warning and continues without saving; since June 2026 default-branch cache tokens are read-only for untrusted triggers (pull_request_target, issue_comment, fork workflow_run), so such workflows lose cache-write benefit unless a trusted push/schedule workflow maintains the cache.
  links: 081b26a9420810cb, 541a91d2c3223ea4
- `9f6d5016691fd195` [evidence-status] mini-agi's verifier-of-verifier audit records 'Recorded FPR/FNR per target in the calibration corpus' — disagreement diagnostics, not just pass/fail.   links: 3d4cc0a018b20c21, 8af8f2689671ac0a
- `a0ae947f3627d7ca` [general] Seccomp architecture/ABI confusion vectors: always check arch; on x86-64, the x32 ABI shares AUDIT_ARCH_X86_64 and a policy that does not account for __X32_SYSCALL_BIT 'can be bypassed by a malicious program that sets __X32_SYSCALL_BIT'; kernels before 5.4 permitted nr 512-547 plus x32-ORed forms that could invoke ptrace with confused x32-vs-x86_64 semantics.
  links: 7b032b76d2592172, 76649269e1ba035d
- `a4b2703c22963a98` [general] mcp-hub's CHANGELOG.md states verbatim: 'The format is based on Keep a Changelog, and this project adheres to Semantic Versioning' — the only explicit published version-policy statement found.
  links: 036d4210a72a6375, 486c92d91a5c5d33
- `a7ce42ab7d93d083` [general] OpenAI Agents SDK guardrails have three layers: input (on first-agent input, parallel or blocking), output (on final output), and tool guardrails (input checks before a function-tool runs — can skip, replace output, or raise a tripwire; output checks after); tripwires raise exceptions and halt the run; ordering vs approvals is configurable via `ToolExecutionConfig(pre_approval_tool_input_guardrails=True)`; tool guardrails apply only to `function_tool` tools, not hosted/built-in tools or handoffs.
  links: dc228d975c9158da, 84c8cabac4c26d5f
- `abb7fe3daf6e746d` [general] OpenAI docs distinguish JSON mode from Structured Outputs: JSON mode 'outputs valid JSON: Yes' but 'adheres to schema: No'; Structured Outputs adheres (first-party claim, no measured rate given).
  links: f3b28a55a63ea68b, a5b5c576f4ffd0b5
- `abb91cc1c7c944cc` [general] A temporal knowledge-graph memory improves temporal-reasoning QA by a large relative margin over full-context reading: Zep (Graphiti temporal KG) on LongMemEvalS temporal-reasoning, gpt-4o full-context 45.1% → Zep 62.4% (+38.4% rel); gpt-4o-mini 36.5% → 54.1% (+48.2% rel). Source: arXiv:2501.13956, Table 3.
  links: b6fef0270ed8d28d, dca21576c4c8bae6
- `abc0df15df514356` [general] OpenAI Agents SDK sessions are history stores keyed by session_id; different sessions maintain separate conversation histories; sharing is explicit; RedisSession exists for shared memory across workers/services; no per-agent ACL exists. [fact]
  links: 40a6f5c1783caef7, 7b02843dca2475f6
- `af2b1630bfe751ef` [general] CRITIC (Gou et al., 2023) has the model validate its own output against external tools (search engine, code interpreter) and revise; the authors report it 'consistently enhances the performance of LLMs' across free-form QA, mathematical program synthesis, and toxicity reduction, concluding external feedback is 'crucially important' for self-improvement. Caveat: only the abstract was verifiable; per-benchmark numbers were not verified (arXiv:2305.11738, ICLR 2024).
  links: 9240d1f949e33e15, b4ac4344ace6c5a2
- `b12ad7c7f07596a0` [repair] Counterpoint to always enforce (measured, 8,560 trials, 14 models): free-text tool calling (NLT) beat structured tool calling 62.3% vs 47.4% accuracy with 93% fewer critical errors and 25.2% fewer tokens — enforcement guarantees parseability, not task success, and for tool calling can add retry cost rather than remove it.
  links: 4d082f928948911f, f15a4d58c0bb1a4a
- `b1b91935c25b467a` [general] Anthropic returns a usage object on every Messages response described as 'Billing and rate-limit usage': input_tokens, cache_creation_input_tokens, cache_read_input_tokens, output_tokens; total input tokens is the summation of input_tokens, cache_creation_input_tokens, and cache_read_input_tokens, and output_tokens is the inclusive, authoritative total used for billing; token counts will not match one-to-one with the exact visible content.
  links: 3912dd0a304826cb, 31afd4aa76fb215a
- `b364a47fb85065c7` [general] Anthropic context-engineering guidance: subagents return only a condensed, distilled summary (often 1,000-2,000 tokens); detailed search context remains isolated within sub-agents. [pattern, fact-sourced]
  links: b2fee6b8038589fb, e7777e2701a56d30
- `b3d72fc2c65ef3e3` [general] Langfuse monitors are a severity state machine (OK / WARNING / ALERT / NO_DATA / PAUSED); alerts fire only on severity transitions, Renotify is off by default (re-firing every N minutes only if enabled), and no-data handling can suppress alerts (keep previous severity, show NO_DATA with no alert, or delayed sustained-NO_DATA alert); notifications go through Automations to Slack, HMAC-signed webhook, or GitHub Actions.
  links: daf69938aa31c832, 081b26a9420810cb
- `b42bd1037051c802` [general] LongMemEval storage-granularity finding: compressing sessions into individual user facts harms overall performance due to information loss but improves multi-session reasoning accuracy.
  links: 9cd6a6c67868558e, f350dbda4620ffdd
- `b7ab00766ba80fd0` [general] Established multi-source finding: explanations are a weak lever on their own — they do not reduce overreliance (Buçinca 2021) and only help when they enable verification (Fok & Weld 2024).
  links: 66da2477aa7e8e3f, 6cf5f705aaedf39d
- `bacb0e234ea35e04` [general] DeepVerifier (arXiv 2601.15808) was measured on 2,997 agent actions across 90 tasks on WebAggregatorQA (CK-Pro agent, Claude-3.7-Sonnet backbone); 555 error points annotated by two staff annotators against human reference solutions with 63% cross-annotator overlap; taxonomy built by iterative clustering/labeling of error points.
  links: 554b0217976296b3, c1a0b768075c2467
- `bdf2aeb421403f14` [general] Google Cloud MCP servers — the standard way agent frameworks invoke external tools — write Data Access audit logs with the service name format SERVICE_NAME.googleapis.com/mcp, enabling audit of agent-orchestrated tool calls inside the platform's audit log.
  links: adbc0cde1ae47dc7, 3aee4552498f51e4
- `beb33e0d0a29d161` [general] SWE-bench Verified identified three reliability problems: overly-specific/unrelated unit tests (false rejections), underspecified issue descriptions, and unreliable dev-environment setup; addressed via human annotation plus a containerized (Docker) harness.
  links: e51f44d00affc6cb, 1ee6755802152b1d
- `c115eb02966062a5` [general] The 70%/100%/50% MemGPT eviction numbers are framed as examples ('e.g.') in the paper, not a tuned optimum; no ablation of eviction fraction was found in reviewed sources.
  links: 832a7c538944c10a, 19d6b1929b157a24
- `c25768dab2411013` [general] LangChain schema contract is enforced by construction (type hints required, reserved names rejected); error handling is middleware-based with no built-in guardrail/tripwire layer in the tools docs; gating (HITL, interrupts) is a LangGraph graph-level feature, not a tool-level one.
  links: 1b415f6ca1c96c6c, 2207c9782986060d
- `c93ad27aa8c7bb3a` [general] deepseek-v4-flash API pricing (USD per 1M tokens, official DeepSeek pricing page, fetched 2026-08-06): input cache-hit $0.0028, input cache-miss $0.14, output $0.28. Source: https://api-docs.deepseek.com/quick_start/pricing
  links: d5b02e7e2030b2b9, e9e5cfc1d0b1386a
- `ca509f76052d6818` [general] Zep/Graphiti per-message ingestion: each episode triggers entity extraction (with prior n=4 messages for context), entity resolution, fact extraction, deduplication, temporal extraction, and LLM-based edge invalidation on contradictions; "Graphiti consistently prioritizes new information" [S5] §2.1–2.2.
  links: 92823ba871a2eaae, 70d2e0f66bef8b38
- `ca8c6940f3cb0005` [general] RecMem (arXiv:2605.16045, ACL 2026 Findings) frames the core dichotomy in LLM memory consolidation: existing systems invoke LLMs to process every incoming interaction for memory extraction ('eager consolidation'), which leads to substantial token consumption; RecMem's fix triggers LLM consolidation only when semantically similar interactions recur (thresholds theta_sim=0.6-0.7, theta_count=4-5), buffering everything else in an embedding-only 'subconscious' store.
  links: 4a16c7ba303c62e7, 19ef42b700d36e05
- `cec104f546b03f85` [general] ContinuityBench (arXiv:2607.15899) — retry backoff at the infrastructure layer: with history-forwarding failover, context preserved in 99.20% (95% Wilson CI 98.27–99.63%) of 750 failover events vs near-0% for stateless failover. Two failure modes at C=100 concurrency: a race condition where parallel failovers corrupt shared proxy state, and a retry-storm (thundering-herd) where naive fixed-interval retries against a rate-limited fallback self-sustain and 'permanently lock out the fallback endpoint'; exponential backoff with jitter is 'necessary and sufficient' to resolve it. (Single-team system study, not independently replicated.)
  links: b023dfa34d7b6603, 00f1658f7d8f50f6
- `cef70c90b6220520` [general] Hybrid LLM headline: up to 40% fewer calls to the large model with no drop in response quality (abstract); 22% fewer GPT-3.5-turbo calls at <1% BART-score drop (intro, Fig 1c). (Hybrid LLM, arXiv 2404.14618v1)
  links: 53b44257c13294e1, 6977145a51d83491
- `d3c25848ca456b8e` [general] LightMem reports higher ACC and lower cost than all memory baselines (LongMemEval-S GPT-4o-mini: 68.64 vs Mem0 53.61 / A-Mem 62.60 / FullText 56.80), i.e. no measured quality-cost tradeoff in their eval; ablation shows removing topic segmentation costs -6.3% ACC (GPT) (LightMem Table 2, §5.4).
  links: f0cad0a342fce769, b023dfa34d7b6603
