- `d3efc6d2b15b11ba` [general] CrewAI: a tool returns `ToolFailure(code, retryable)` instead of a string — the framework records it and 'never guesses whether a string looks like an error'; `tool_failure_policy` (`ignore`/`warn` default/`raise`) with most-specific-wins precedence (tool → task → agent → crew); `max_usage_count` limits a tool's calls.
  links: 93aab397ab2425d6, 7724947e09734d4a
- `d5728fa1ece59eee` [evidence-status] Let's Verify Step by Step (arXiv:2305.20050) establishes that step-level supervision beats outcome-only supervision for math reward models, but no production agent framework was found running step-level process supervision as its run verifier; mini-agi's plan lists it as roadmap, not shipped.
  links: 20775a40371d1e55, 8af8f2689671ac0a
- `d58b86f6de25115d` [general] Claude Code documents verification as a per-session contract bound to the repo: give Claude a check it can run (tests, build, diff-vs-fixture) and gate stops on it, via a Stop hook (deterministic), a /goal condition re-checked each turn, or a verification subagent; CLAUDE.md stores the repo's verification commands.
  links: 27cae6ee45174527, 49e58f71d2bdd715
- `d6f4e1893008deef` [general] Landlock pre-existing FD escape: 'Files or directories opened before the sandboxing are not subject to these restrictions,' and such FDs keep their Landlock properties when passed between processes 'even when some of the involved processes do not have an enforced Landlock ruleset.' A worker that inherits pre-opened writable FDs from its parent retains write access.
  links: dd98058876169ac0, 7390cd3a09477737
- `d8bd2c0e4ce8fe9c` [general] LangGraph writes a checkpoint at each super-step boundary and persists per-node writes to a checkpoint_writes table so that if one node fails, successful nodes' writes are durable ('pending writes') and are not re-run on resume; resume restarts from the last successful step.
  links: dbc7cbf9a748d51b, 7fcceed280fcc7b0
- `da2c3504efdd2f5e` [repair] Deterministic-verifier repair economics (measured): Self-Debugging shows ~10× sample-efficiency when a deterministic verifier drives the loop; difficulty-adaptive retry budgets are up to 4× more compute-efficient than uniform best-of-N (Snell et al.); test-time compute can match a 14× larger model under FLOPs matching.
  links: dc2abae1015a10fc, b06e866e7dd44247
- `dbc7cbf9a748d51b` [general] LangGraph uses a checkpointer that saves a snapshot of graph state at each super-step, organized into threads; if nodes fail at a super-step, the graph can be restarted from the last successful step.
  links: d8bd2c0e4ce8fe9c, 7fcceed280fcc7b0
- `dd8a402cb34e9585` [general] Adversarial/abstention failures: facts never stored can't be abstained on — Coze often failed to record indirectly provided user information (implicitly-stated facts never enter the store), and compressed/overwriting stores corrupt prior facts over time (ChatGPT); LoCoMo's adversarial category (2.1% for a long-context model) is the same failure surface.
  links: 0e8c9801a605c550, d32f5cbbb9dfef2a
- `df988dfd099e2766` [general] Datadog Agent Observability "Patterns" performs automated hierarchical topic clustering of production traffic to surface user intents and coverage gaps; "Insights" runs outlier detection across span name, workflow type, and Patterns topics, surfacing anomalies in the UI for the selected time window.
  links: fe8d202885bb2379, 1686d0bb9b2d028f
- `e151279bfa369f9a` [general] Hybrid LLM with thresholds tuned to keep BART drop <1%, achievable cost advantage is pair-dependent: Llama-2 7b/13b → >96% cost advantage; Llama-2 13b/GPT-3.5-turbo → only ~15%; FLAN-t5 800m/Llama-2 13b → ~5%. (Hybrid LLM, arXiv 2404.14618v1, §4.5, Table 3)
  links: 6977145a51d83491, 135c69977d75322e
- `e268c955dcf1bd2f` [general] By default LangGraph checkpoints write the full value of every state channel at each super-step; DeltaChannel stores only deltas, making checkpoint blobs 'O(1) per step instead of O(N)… at the cost of… storage-vs-latency tradeoff.'
  links: a495d4c500ec01b5, 0f28c5715a50722b
- `e5170e596b2575e1` [repair] SQL Query Engine (system report) validates retry-loop design with three explicit mechanisms: early-accept (never re-evaluate a query that executed correctly — prevents the LLM 'fixing' a good query into a bad one), best-result tracking (return the best result seen when retries exhaust — a bad retry cannot regress), and ignoring the LLM's own isValid self-assessment (always False by design; only execution outcome determines success).
  links: 3880083953a8f0d3, 7014b9ee9ab22a85
- `e6515e624c072972` [general] Uncertainty-based abstention measurably improves deployed-quality outcomes: filtering out semantically uncertain questions in a clinical radiology VLM raised answer accuracy on retained questions from 51.7% to 76.3% (GPT-4o) and 54.8% to 63.8% (GPT-4.1), both p<.001. (Wienholt et al., Eur Radiol 2026, arXiv 2510.09256; verified abstract)
  links: 6e30b9e0905d102a, 84c8cabac4c26d5f
- `e798cc05679df268` [general] Workspace-trust dialogs gate loaded skills/config: Claude project skills take effect for allowed-tools only after the trust dialog is accepted, and a skill can grant itself broad tool access; Codex loads project config only when the project is trusted.
  links: 9a41828e542b1154, 8f32a4d8dcbf2ca5
- `eab86d0eeedfb0ca` [general] Production agent-memory systems combine all three retrieval signals: Zep runs Okapi BM25, cosine embedding similarity, and breadth-first graph search in parallel, then reranks (incl. Reciprocal Rank Fusion and cross-encoder rerankers). Source: arXiv:2501.13956, Sections 3.1-3.2.
  links: b6fef0270ed8d28d, 2e0933213df8db7b
- `eb30bfc42085c15c` [evidence-status] mini-agi's verifier is audited for vacuity: `verify-audit` requires the gate to PASS on known-good work and FAIL on an empty counterfactual target, recording FPR/FNR.
  links: 439ade46d55d5c15, 40120af48e7950b9
- `eb55e129103e8a6f` [general] Estimate: snapshot frequency bounds recovery time (replay length) and storage cost (archived WAL/old snapshots); no source gives universal numbers — the etcd example snapshot of 2.1 MB for 7 keys/rev 10 is illustrative, not a rule; choose checkpoint interval per write volume and RTO/RPO.
  links: 6665ad2250b0bf6a, 8baa3b2f82d7faa6
- `ebcb42396b17bd81` [general] Flat-file memory is chosen for readability/git/zero-infra (Claude Code, MCP memory server) and its costs are explicit in primary docs: context-window limits (~200 lines/25KB), on-demand loading, and machine-locality (not shared across machines/cloud).
  links: b7d8338a5de669c3, 15090391d6c44e0d
- `ecd4a6522505f64f` [general] Synthesis opinion: only Anthropic treats skill/tool quality as a reviewable, versioned artifact with a lifecycle and re-evaluation cadence; MCP is the only framework that turns the tool contract (schema) itself into an on-the-wire, cacheable, change-notifiable object; other frameworks put enforcement in the host application rather than the artifact.
  links: 96875179b47df8ba, b023dfa34d7b6603
- `ede50378a375dcb5` [evidence-status] Terminal-Bench/Harbor: each task is an instruction + a test script + an oracle solution; the Harbor format requires `tests/test.sh` to write a reward to `/logs/verifier/reward.txt` (e.g. `1`/`0`) or `reward.json`, with a canonical pytest-exit-code -> reward mapping. The verifier can run in a separate environment so proprietary grading code is hidden from the agent, and verifier and agent have separate `timeout_sec`.
  links: 8af8f2689671ac0a, 22abb140e3c15e39
- `f1536221b75a8013` [general] FrugalGPT example learned thresholds (HEADLINES, budget $6.5 = 1/5 of GPT-4 cost): accept GPT-J's answer if score > 0.96; else query J1-L, accept if score > 0.37; else GPT-4. Result: 80% cost cut and 1.5% accuracy gain over GPT-4. (FrugalGPT, arXiv 2305.05176, §4)
  links: 1eb6cac2ee3c0b55, 300485f9871ece7c
- `f33c569f53a96c52` [general] Claude Code structured summary keeps: requests and intent, key technical concepts, files examined/modified with important code snippets, errors and how they were fixed, pending tasks, current work; it replaces verbatim conversation — full tool outputs and intermediate reasoning are gone. Detailed instructions from early in conversation may be lost; persistent rules belong in CLAUDE.md. (Claude Code docs)
  links: ae9c67ba8d9f53fa, c42c3195c61002fe
- `f775e497d7e8f748` [repair] Token-set and wording levers (measured): the token set a grammar admits moves classification correlation ~0.5 — real numbers ρ 0.42–0.61 vs integer ρ=−0.16; leading-whitespace tokens +5–10% because LW/non-LW twins are non-exchangeable ('Lost in Space'); schema key wording alone moves accuracy ±16 pp by model family (Qwen2.5-7B +6.89 pp, Llama-3.2-3B −15.77 pp); word-formats −0.12 to −0.41.
  links: edfc9ea838f66df0, 9266ab4c52fd8d11
- `f80a8893ffad1994` [general] RecMem's query-time token cost stays comparable to eager baselines (e.g. LoCoMo GPT-4o-mini: 2.73K vs Mem0 1.99K), so the saving concentrates in construction; the authors argue (opinion, their claim) that these construction-time differences accumulate over time and can dominate total LLM usage in streaming deployments (RecMem §4.2).
  links: 27cb143596b061e2, 19ef42b700d36e05
- `fa9b089b4e75b0cb` [general] GitLab's Automatic reviewer assignment assigns CODEOWNERS automatically; the GitLab Duo Agent Platform strategy chooses the minimum number of reviewers needed to satisfy each approval rule, weighing availability, review workload (open MRs awaiting their review), local time, and recency of activity.
  links: b3cea14ec5ed51db, db8bc4fd1714d223
- `fcfc5df953031f06` [general] In large-scale image classification (N=1,152,000 observations), machine confidence scores and human confidence ratings each independently and significantly improved hybrid human+AI accuracy (machine confidence log-odds effect 0.257, human confidence 0.104, class error model 0.101; all p<.001); human confidence ratings were as effective as an explicit error model. (Steyvers et al., PNAS 119(11), 2022; full text verified)
  links: 0ad6075069f5864a, 6e30b9e0905d102a
- `fe7f8b67b4c17d62` [general] LangSmith automation rules are defined as filter + sampling rate (0–1) + action, with actions including Add to annotation queue, Add to dataset, Trigger webhook, Extend data retention, run online evaluator, and Trigger alert, in a fixed ordering (queue → dataset → webhook → evaluator → alert); documented examples include sending all traces with negative feedback or 10% of all traces to an annotation queue for spot checks.
  links: 7cfe9661cd15e22a, 87e145dc13717659
- `fe8d202885bb2379` [general] Braintrust Topics auto-classifies production logs by task, sentiment, issue type, and custom patterns; logs can be filtered by topic classification (SQL on classifications.Task.label, etc.), matching logs assigned to reviewers, and topic-matching scorers run online with a sampling rate via automations.
  links: df988dfd099e2766, 1686d0bb9b2d028f
- `feeed923077d1e1f` [general] Hybrid LLM router quality transfer to new model pairs degrades with quality-gap correlation: at high correlation (r=0.76) 20% cost advantage costs <=1.6% quality, 40% costs <=4.1%; at low correlation (r=0.06) it fails. (Hybrid LLM, arXiv 2404.14618v1, §4.7, Fig 8)
  links: 53b44257c13294e1, 6977145a51d83491
- `00aadbc4be76ab80` [general] GCP attributes a human's actions executed through Google-managed service agents with the originating sign-in context, and notes that in multi-service flows, unless explicit identity delegation is in place, the service might propagate the immediate caller's origin.
  links: 3aee4552498f51e4
- `017da62a81759656` [general] Lint levels are allow, expect, warn, force-warn, deny, forbid, configurable via -A/-W/-D/-F flags, attributes, or --cap-lints; forbid cannot be overridden downward and --force-warn takes precedence over everything.
  links: 8f32a4d8dcbf2ca5
- `026757b2f7b5dc33` [general] Stripe Radar lets operators write rules that automatically place payments into review and provides Smart Refunds recommendations ranked by refund-confidence, so operators triage a ranked list rather than scan everything.
  links: 15a5494102fc23e9
- `075b797922584daa` [general] GAM explicitly decouples memory encoding from consolidation: ongoing dialogue lives in an event-progression graph and is integrated into a topic-associative network only on semantic shifts, minimizing interference while preserving long-term consistency (SOTA on LoCoMo and LongDialQA). Source: arXiv 2604.12285.
  links: 1052dd5a927ed18a
- `08460d217f2ef14e` [general] SQLAlchemy sessions use SQLAlchemy to provide a production-ready session implementation, allowing use of any database supported by SQLAlchemy (PostgreSQL, MySQL, SQLite, etc.).
  links: 40a6f5c1783caef7
- `0950282a69d5217e` [general] Anthropic agent-eval practice: a good task is one 'where two domain experts would independently reach the same pass/fail verdict'; each task should be passable given a reference solution; 'a 0% pass rate across many trials (0% pass@100) is most often a signal of a broken task, not an incapable agent'.
  links: 888b4004f537880f
- `095902827fb17ae8` [general] In AI-assisted decision making, calibrating trust case-by-case is a key to success: knowing when to trust or distrust the model lets an expert apply their knowledge, improving outcomes in cases where the model is likely to perform poorly. (Zhang, Liao, Bellamy 2020, ACM FAT*)
  links: f7b7247ebe13d8e0
- `0bcb6001a27bbb57` [ai-auditability] AI RMF Playbook Govern: GOVERN 1.1 'Legal and regulatory requirements involving AI are understood, managed, and documented'; GOVERN 1.4 urges standardized documentation (AI-actor contact info, business justification, scope and usages, expected risks, assumptions and limitations, training-data characterization, algorithmic methodology, testing and validation results, dependencies, deployment/monitoring/change-management plans) plus a 'model documentation inventory'; GOVERN 1.5 covers ongoing monitoring, incident response, and 'appeal and override' (human adjudication of system outcomes); GOVERN 2.1 requires roles, responsibilities, and lines of communication to be 'documented and clear.'
  links: 77ad6114bf5546c2
- `0d3061eff0d247c2` [general] Proptest defines property testing as checking properties over automatically generated inputs with automatic shrinking to a minimal failing case; it positions itself as a complement to, not replacement for, hand-written unit tests.
  links: e6b84c294456ecd4
- `0dd854219451e9ec` [general] The panic-vs-Result choice is codifiable as lints: clippy restriction lints such as unwrap_used exist specifically to prevent panicking in certain functions on CI.
  links: 7a5d99da7db6d539
- `0f4339615d264098` [repair] IBM enterprise automation evaluation (Wrenn et al., arXiv:2608.03311; IC2E '26 Industry; system report, 2,784 runs, 232 attempts per model per pipeline, 8 runs per cell, 29 prompts, 6 LLMs): monolithic v1 pipeline (whole workflow in one call) achieved structural success (JSON-schema valid + UI-renderable) of just 31.5–82.8% (gpt-oss-120b best 82.8%; granite-4-h-small 31.5%, mistral-small 33.2%).
  links: f0afcf1604a978a8
- `0f71428eedb1157f` [general] gen_ai.server.time_per_output_token metric: unit s; time per output token after the first; same attribute set as time_to_first_token.
  links: a374d61735af78e8
- `1262f61f6e19c143` [general] Codex has a global cross-chat memory ('Memories') stored at ~/.codex/memories/ injected into future sessions, but it is off by default, per-chat controllable, skips chats that used external context when configured, and docs say to keep required rules in AGENTS.md rather than memories.
  links: 52f81a90784ffeec
- `12bee8131387de68` [general] The Cargo Book is the canonical classification of compatible/breaking changes (major/minor/possibly-breaking) and defines that 0.y.z bumps treat y as major; possibly-breaking items explicitly depend on maintainer judgment.
  links: 9a4df56f7d846a01
- `13abdc5cbdc4afeb` [general] On LoCoMo with GPT-4o-mini, construction tokens: Mem0 1,233.5K / A-Mem 1,143.3K vs RecMem 202.4K, i.e. up to ~7.8x fewer tokens for RecMem (RecMem Table 1).
  links: 27cb143596b061e2
- `13cb6f40a083137a` [general] mcp-hub's observed release history (62 versions 1.0.0 to 4.2.1) matches its declared semver policy: breaking changes shipped as majors 2.0.0 (endpoints moved from URL params to server_name body), 3.0.0 (client registration endpoints removed), 4.0.0 (marketplace API schema/endpoint migration), each with a '### Breaking Changes' section; features landed as minors, bug fixes as patches.
  links: a46362f8efe48984
- `151c07708b758bac` [general] Official guidance: keep unsafe blocks small; wrap unsafe code in a safe abstraction prevents uses of unsafe from leaking out; SAFETY: comments on unsafe fns and blocks are idiomatic.
  links: 0f1a1f2be8e214d4
- `15a5494102fc23e9` [general] Stripe Radar presents a review queue ('a prioritized list of completed or to-be-captured payments') in two modes: a list view to scan without opening details, and a detailed view with customizable risk context; operators navigate with J/K keys or Previous/Next buttons and act on each item (Approve / Refund / Refund-and-report-fraud).
  links: 026757b2f7b5dc33
- `16adddbad37f6c5c` [general] The 10 GB per-repository Actions cache limit was doubled from 5 GB in 2021.
  links: 4355cae9f899ac1c
- `1711f8ebaaa6ff1c` [general] Landlock unmediatable operations: 'It is currently not possible to restrict some file-related actions accessible through these syscall families: chdir(2), stat(2), flock(2), chmod(2), chown(2), setxattr(2), utime(2), fcntl(2), access(2).' A worker can still stat/read metadata, chdir, lock, and chmod what it can reach.
  links: dd98058876169ac0
- `179f80e4011804dd` [general] A-MEM (arXiv:2502.12110 §3.3) merges new memories into semantically near existing notes instead of deduplicating by deletion: for each near neighbor the LLM decides whether to update its context, keywords, and tags, and the evolved note replaces the original.
  links: 8014b8b82d793b73
- `17e10ae638f998a8` [general] Kubernetes audit records begin their lifecycle inside the kube-apiserver, are pre-processed according to a policy, and are written to a backend; current backend implementations include log files and webhooks.
  links: 87e145dc13717659
- `185255b5f360bea5` [general] Letta docs describe 'dreaming' (successor of MemGPT): background subagents review recent conversations, consolidate lessons, and update memory without interrupting active work, runnable after a configured number of user messages or on context-window compaction, plus a /doctor audit for placement, duplication, and system-prompt token usage.
  links: 8baa3b2f82d7faa6
- `1a17006009583ba8` [general] pg_ivm documents that IVM is not effective when a base table is modified frequently, or when a large part is modified or large data is inserted — maintenance cost can exceed refresh from scratch.
  links: 266b8325088b1614
- `1b261b0e97a18552` [general] Candidate adoption (4): per-batch witness with autonomous failure diagnosis is a candidate deepening of the planner/bg layer; mini-agi currently has only global supervision, not per-rig watchdog semantics.
  links: f54b0ed68eef1372
- `1b264bd2db12c508` [general] PyPI lookup for mcp-hub returned 404 (https://pypi.org/pypi/mcp-hub/json) — no Python distribution of that name was found; the package in question is the npm one.
  links: cc5ee7db3c43ac65
- `1b415f6ca1c96c6c` [general] LangGraph implements HITL via dynamic interrupts: the graph pauses at interrupt(...), returns an interrupt payload to the caller, and resumes by posting a Command(resume=...) with the human's input; static interrupts (interrupt_before/after) exist but the docs recommend against them for HITL, calling them debug/test tools.
  links: c25768dab2411013
- `1c641abef370327a` [general] Event sourcing replay re-runs side effects, so external systems must be wrapped with a Gateway that can detect replay mode and suppress outbound calls — the same problem durable-execution platforms solve with idempotency.
  links: a40757229890299e
- `1cebb877f366157c` [general] Invalidation/supersede is the near-universal answer to 'outdated fact' in reviewed systems: keep the history, hide or timestamp the old truth, never destroy it.
  links: 7c0a290e885b0a7d
- `1f78b4ce03e423e2` [general] TS SDK CVE-2025-66414 (GHSA-w48q-cv73-mx4w, high): DNS-rebinding protection disabled by default for HTTP servers on localhost (CWE-1188); patched in 1.24.0.
  links: 494ee4991d2c9420
- `21114a6bac5e12fc` [general] Anthropic harness guidance: each trial must start from a clean, isolated environment; 'unnecessary shared state between runs... can cause correlated failures due to infrastructure flakiness rather than agent performance' (Claude was observed gaining an unfair advantage by reading git history from prior trials).
  links: e7777e2701a56d30
- `2207c9782986060d` [general] LangChain tool execution is wrapped by middleware (e.g. `wrap_tool_call` for retry/error-to-message conversion); `ToolNode` runs tools inside the graph.
  links: c25768dab2411013
- `221140d4511ffebf` [general] A read-only mount of a WAL-mode SQLite database fails unless the -shm/-wal files already exist and are readable, or the directory is writable, or the database is opened immutable.
  links: d86f43007ae0e974
- `251b28fe96799da2` [general] LiveBench was designed to avoid test-set contamination and LLM-judge/crowdsourcing biases by scoring 'answers automatically according to objective ground-truth values'.
  links: 1052dd5a927ed18a
- `26963aa692752e78` [general] Default agent run state is in-memory in all major agent frameworks; persistence is an added layer. LangGraph splits it into checkpointers (thread-scoped short-term state) and stores (cross-thread long-term memory), and says persistence 'matters when an agent needs to continue a conversation, resume after an interruption, recover from a failure, or remember information across interactions'.
  links: c65a4ce6cdb624b8
- `26dbee59e3ecf43e` [repair] xmemory (system report) states an illustrative compounding model (not measured): at per-field accuracy 0.97 with 20 fields, single-pass record-correct probability is 0.97^20≈0.54, recovering to ≈0.98 with two retries per field.
  links: 86be403af727cc0f
- `2766f5af4ab026e3` [general] SQLite supports an unlimited number of simultaneous readers but only one writer at any instant in time.
  links: 601ae8b56f00daf0
- `27cae6ee45174527` [evidence-status] Anthropic Claude Code guidance: 'Give Claude a check it can run: tests, a build, a screenshot to compare' — the check is 'anything that returns a signal' (test suite, build exit code, linter, diff-vs-fixture script, browser screenshot). This is vendor guidance/recommendation, not an observed benchmark result.
  links: d58b86f6de25115d
- `2e0933213df8db7b` [general] Zep (arXiv:2501.13956 §2.2) two-stage dedup during ingestion: entity resolution (embedding cosine + full-text candidates then LLM duplicate check) and edge/fact dedup, where hybrid search for relevant edges is constrained to edges between the same entity pairs as the proposed new edge, significantly reducing dedup computational complexity.
  links: eab86d0eeedfb0ca
- `2eea1cc6e5d5294f` [general] gen_ai.provider.name well-known values (snapshot of main on fetch date; the enumeration will grow over time): anthropic, aws.bedrock, azure.ai.inference, azure.ai.openai, cohere, deepseek, gcp.gemini, gcp.gen_ai, gcp.vertex_ai, groq, ibm.watsonx.ai, mistral_ai, moonshot_ai, openai, perplexity, x_ai.
  links: 3bdf61d670b97831
- `31f481abaa8a4578` [general] Letta measured effect (paper): "sleep-time compute can reduce the amount of test-time compute needed to achieve the same accuracy by ~5x on Stateful GSM-Symbolic and Stateful AIME and ... scaling sleep-time compute we can further increase accuracy by up to 13% on Stateful GSM-Symbolic and 18% on Stateful AIME"; on Multi-Query GSM-Symbolic "we can decrease the average cost per query by 2.5x" [S2] abstract.
  links: 3b19ffa7c3f7be90
- `330187df00ed1779` [general] LangGraph custom-checkpointer guidance: get-by-id must be 'O(1) or close to it'; 'A scan with a value filter on every lookup works but does not scale'; index design is correctness+performance-critical.
  links: 73649fc34b71a4f6
- `3358e0f2e6ca0096` [general] REJECTED: grilling rounds (our domain-modeling is already a superset, only missing non-blocking fact dispatch), and catalog/marketplace/distribution (popularity is not a mechanism; our enforced kernel registry with hooks/one-owner/versioning is deliberately stronger).
  links: 8ffcf6eceee36cf2
- `34b2aac2c52f66d1` [general] gen_ai.invoke_agent.tool_calls metric: unit {tool_call}; recommended gen_ai.agent.name.
  links: f640281634a3ed98
- `38fcbdc4541b046e` [general] GitHub routes code review requests to specific people/teams and gives each reviewer a filterable 'awaiting your review' list; each PR is decided with one of three discrete outcomes: Comment, Approve, Request changes.
  links: b3cea14ec5ed51db
- `3900b567dfe90746` [general] Self-Refine's feedback-quality ablations: actionable feedback >> generic >> none (Code Optimization 27.5 vs 26.0 vs 24.8; Sentiment Reversal 43.2 vs 31.2 vs 0); on a weak model (Vicuna-13B) the refinement step fails entirely — the model cannot even emit feedback in the required format (Self-Refine §4, Table 2).
  links: 93d3f6c1ebd2262f
- `391ec99299379c6c` [general] Mem0 cost vs full-context on LOCOMO: full-context p95 latency ≈17.1s (p50 9.87s); Mem0 p50 0.708s / p95 1.440s; graph variant p50 1.091s / p95 2.590s; paper claims "91% lower p95 latency and saves more than 90% token cost" vs full-context [S6] abstract, §4.4.
  links: 5e22ecc2aa426efa
- `3a6abad82d4d9ab6` [evidence-status] SWE-bench instances carry FAIL_TO_PASS (tests resolved by the PR, which must pass after the patch) and PASS_TO_PASS (tests that must pass before and after); evaluation uses post-PR behavior as the reference solution.
  links: 32bed97e81d99774
- `3c233462b12abfbd` [general] Sampling multiple reasoning paths and taking the most consistent answer (self-consistency) improves accuracy on arithmetic/commonsense benchmarks (GSM8K +17.9%). (Wang et al., ICLR 2023; verified abstract)
  links: dc2abae1015a10fc
- `3c51acc3d4b42092` [general] The SQLite WAL-reset corruption bug only affects WAL-mode databases with two or more connections open on the same file in separate threads or processes that attempt to write or checkpoint at the same instant; it is present in all versions 3.7.0–3.51.2, fixed in 3.51.3 (2026-03-13), and rare with low probability.
  links: 344882f912fc587f
- `3d1604aaeeb8b61b` [general] Lost in the Middle (Liu et al., TACL 2023): models use beginning and end of context best; 'performance is often highest when relevant information occurs at the beginning or end... and significantly degrades when models must access relevant information in the middle.' Supports pruning the middle over pruning oldest turns.
  links: fe75beb9da3fa6ba
- `3d35f4dd5278a6c4` [ai-auditability] Kubernetes Enhancement Proposals (KEP): a proposal-format process with 'a clear process with approvers and reviewers for making decisions,' giving 'a discoverable record around the decisions'; each KEP is numbered by its tracking issue and required for most non-trivial changes.
  links: 8e2d3e5a027dd99b
- `3e53d4e63a90eb15` [general] On HotpotQA, an episodic-memory-only ablation (recent trajectory, no reflection) gained less than the full reflection step, which added an 8% absolute boost — the verbal reflection step, not just feeding back the failed trace, carried the gain (Reflexion §4.2).
  links: ccbdd85316bb9ecd
- `3f11178717f2bfc4` [general] Capability gating: installing a seccomp filter requires prctl(PR_SET_NO_NEW_PRIVS, 1) or CAP_SYS_ADMIN; Landlock is documented as usable by unprivileged processes ('Landlock empowers any process, including unprivileged ones, to securely restrict themselves').
  links: 59e0420458d34317
- `4355cae9f899ac1c` [general] GitHub Actions cache rate limits are 200 uploads/min and 1,500 downloads/min per repository; exceeding them fails uploads/downloads until the limit resets, signaled via the Retry-After header.
  links: 16adddbad37f6c5c
- `43f4539098e18753` [general] Dedup happens at write time across systems as a way to keep the store from growing with near-identical facts; the dedup rate (how much growth is prevented) is not reported by any reviewed source.
  links: 7c0a290e885b0a7d
- `44f6ae81403776df` [general] Claude Code auto memory is bounded: per-repo directory ~/.claude/projects/<project>/memory/, MEMORY.md index capped at the first 200 lines or first 25KB; writes over the limit return an error to rewrite the index; a modified ISO-8601 timestamp is stamped on frontmatter.
  links: 15090391d6c44e0d
- `45d823a2f58dba76` [general] pytest-rerunfailures supports --reruns N, --reruns-delay with exponential backoff (delay for n-th re-run = reruns_delay * factor**(n-1), default factor 1.0), --only-rerun/--rerun-except regex filters, per-test @pytest.mark.flaky(reruns=5), --force-reruns overrides, --reruns-mode=append (additive marker+CLI counts), --max-suite-reruns suite-wide cap. Priority order: marker > CLI > ini.
  links: 3880083953a8f0d3
- `473e82a58eb494fe` [evidence-status] Reflexion (arXiv:2303.11366): agents verbally reflect on task feedback signals; feedback may be various types (scalar values or free-form language) and sources (external or internally simulated).
  links: ccbdd85316bb9ecd
- `494ee4991d2c9420` [general] Python SDK CVE-2025-66416 (GHSA-9h52-p55h-vw2f): DNS-rebinding protection disabled by default (CWE-1188); patched in 1.23.0.
  links: 1f78b4ce03e423e2
- `4a16c7ba303c62e7` [general] RecMem keeps incoming interactions in a 'subconscious' embedding layer and invokes the LLM to extract episodic/semantic memory only on sustained recurrence of semantically similar interactions; it reduced memory-construction token cost of three SOTA memory systems by up to 87% while exceeding their accuracy. Source: ACL 2026 Findings, arXiv 2605.16045.
  links: ca8c6940f3cb0005
- `4ac0d73a111f7e22` [general] In a multi-agent framework the attribution unit is which agent performed which step in which workflow, on behalf of which conversation (group_id); linking the human/end-user at the trace level rather than the span level is a synthesis/opinion, not SDK-stated.
  links: f0389e3a1b973eae
- `50d864bb40f726a7` [general] OpenAI Agents SDK reduces captured volume rather than notification volume: tracing is on by default but can be disabled per-run, sensitive-data capture (trace_include_sensitive_data) can be turned off, and custom/sampled trace processors can be installed in place of the default exporter.
  links: 59054a5c043328ad
- `51d27a5241433608` [general] Google Cloud states 'Log entries written by Cloud Audit Logs are immutable.'
  links: adbc0cde1ae47dc7
- `52f81a90784ffeec` [general] Codex memories are opt-in generated state: off by default, stored under ~/.codex/memories/, treated as generated state not hand-edited, secrets redacted from generated fields; docs recommend keeping required rules in AGENTS.md and treating memories as a recall layer, not the sole source for rules that must always apply.
  links: 1262f61f6e19c143
- `53591a59c549819a` [general] All incremental-maintenance mechanisms impose eligibility constraints on the derivation (deterministic, restricted SQL), keep auxiliary state for incremental correctness, and expose a full-rebuild escape hatch and an explicit staleness/write-cost tradeoff.
  links: e761f79662ed051f
- `54f9fe2c40ea09b0` [general] OpenAI Agents SDK supervises concurrent agents via grouping: traces carry workflow_name and an optional group_id to link multiple traces from one conversation (e.g., a chat thread id), giving a conversation-scoped view rather than per-call noise.
  links: f0389e3a1b973eae
- `551a3a46b1d3bf43` [repair] MOUD (system report): clinic heterogeneity dominates model choice — Qwen2.5-32B scored 100/100 on Clinic A while Qwen3-32B scored 0% there (42.9% coverage); smaller models are coverage-unstable (Qwen3-8B overall coverage 48.0%, Qwen3-4B 2.2% on Clinic C) — 'model family matters as much as size' for schema-constrained extraction.
  links: d5995f282ab01107
- `554755d1ba485c5f` [ai-auditability] EU AI Act Art 12(1): 'High-risk AI systems shall technically allow for the automatic recording of events (logs) over the lifetime of the system'; logging must enable recording events relevant to identifying risk situations or substantial modification, post-market monitoring, and monitoring operation; for biometric systems minimum logged fields include the period of each use (start/end time), the reference database checked, input data that produced a match, and identification of the natural persons involved in verifying results (Art 12(2)-(3)).
  links: 30a1f40562cac11e
- `579985981421cecf` [general] MCP change notification: servers declaring `listChanged` push `notifications/tools/list_changed` over a `subscriptions/listen` stream when the tool list changes — the protocol-level equivalent of a change hook.
  links: 886f02d1ed76d192
- `592b9e5b3c016edc` [strategy] Yegge (The Shape of Things to Come, Part 1, 2026-08): agent harnesses converge on one shape — producers (crew/design) + consumers (fleet/implement) + coordinator + witness + serialized merge queue; he "excavated" the same shape twice (Gas Town, Wheelhouse) without designing it, so the shape is convergent, not invented.
  links: 3d5b6db5918b465b
- `5940164f5de5669e` [general] LaunchDarkly delivers approval requests to an Approvals dashboard, email, and in-app inbox, plus per-object 'Pending changes' panels and Slack/Teams notifications.
  links: 693010a524f0a9fb
- `5aa99c3328323465` [general] CrewAI scopes/slices are the only sources where read and write privilege are separated at the API level (read-only slices, private/source-matched visibility). [pattern, fact-sourced]
  links: 8aba31b1c97b13fb
- `5f22c03352903571` [general] Mem0 extraction pipeline: step 1 context lookup (checks related existing memories to avoid storing the same fact again), step 3 deduplication and embedding (redundant facts removed, then each memory embedded).
  links: 70d2e0f66bef8b38
- `6268ad4cbce610b0` [general] Oracle fast refresh requires materialized view logs to exist on the master tables at CREATE time, and eligibility for fast refresh is verified when the definition is created.
  links: e1f2e874ff8f4e1d
- `649f2dd5755ff2c1` [general] Uncertain, not established from these sources: no primary source quantifies end-to-end failure-recovery success rates, RTO/RPO for arbitrary agent workloads, or a cost model for choosing among the three approaches; the WAL-reset bug shows latent-corruption risk exists even in the most battle-tested implementation, and nothing in these docs proves any engine's recovery is correct under every crash point.
  links: 3b3bae3fd783ee40
- `666be2622013c3ae` [general] mcp-hub is an npm package (author 'Ravitemer'), MIT-licensed, repository git+https://github.com/ravitemer/mcp-hub.git; current latest dist-tag is 4.2.1 and every published version is a plain x.y.z with no prerelease tags.
  links: cbf6476b32f767bc
- `66da2477aa7e8e3f` [general] In a 199-participant study, adding explanations to AI recommendations did not reduce acceptance of wrong advice; forcing deliberative engagement ('cognitive forcing') did reduce overreliance but was the least-liked design and helped high 'Need for Cognition' users more. (Buçinca et al., CSCW 2021; verified abstract)
  links: b7ab00766ba80fd0
- `675e3592262ae39f` [general] mcp-hub's CONTRIBUTING.md instructs PR authors to 'Update the CHANGELOG.md with notes on your changes' as part of the pull-request process, feeding the changelog gate.
  links: d25b446e25b94b13
- `692f03b97bba1e0f` [general] Structured note-taking/agentic memory: the agent regularly writes notes persisted to memory outside the context window, pulled back into the window at later times; memory tool keeps key findings and project state across sessions out of the window. (Effective context engineering; context-management blog)
  links: ae9c67ba8d9f53fa
- `6b7f36fab910c326` [general] Mem0 (arXiv:2504.19413) reports >90% token-cost saving and 91% lower p95 latency vs processing the whole 26,031-token conversation each query (Mem0 retrieves ~1,764 tokens avg) (Mem0 Abstract, §1, Table 2).
  links: 5e22ecc2aa426efa
- `6bc015d1e12cd076` [general] Whether a log's userAgent or token fields genuinely identify an LLM agent vs. a deterministic script is not specified by the sources; k8s explicitly flags userAgent as client-supplied and untrusted.
  links: b85e02376e6436d9
- `7087735a64f76c95` [general] TiMem consolidates raw conversational observations through a Temporal Memory Tree into progressively abstracted persona representations with semantic-guided consolidation (no fine-tuning), reaching 75.30% on LoCoMo / 76.88% on LongMemEval-S while cutting recalled memory length 52.20%. Source: ACL 2026 Findings, arXiv 2601.02845.
  links: 1412c255c7aa37da
- `71bfdcb5d6aea1fa` [general] No reviewed source reports dedup rates, compression ratios, or fact-count growth over time; whether retention actually bounds storage is unanswered in every reviewed source.
  links: 08ab1305a972ff38
- `7390cd3a09477737` [general] Landlock truncate/ioctl subtleties: truncate is decoupled from WRITE_FILE (e.g. open(O_RDONLY|O_TRUNC) truncates without the write right); creat() needs the truncate right; fallocate(FALLOC_FL_COLLAPSE_RANGE) can shorten a writable file 'sidestepping the LANDLOCK_ACCESS_FS_TRUNCATE right'; several ioctls (FIOCLEX, FIONBIO, FICLONE, FIDEDUPERANGE...) are exempt from IOCTL_DEV.
  links: d6f4e1893008deef
- `75f9d3c529016fb3` [general] LangGraph's Store is key-value data accessible from any thread, isolated only by a user-chosen namespace tuple; reads match by namespace prefix; the Store API (put/get/delete/search/list_namespaces) has no permission or ownership parameter. [fact]
  links: c65a4ce6cdb624b8
- `76d2c318013e0da9` [general] Zep (arXiv:2501.13956 §2.3) community subgraphs are periodic summaries of entity clusters (label propagation, dynamically extended); the paper concedes communities gradually diverge from a complete label propagation run, so periodic community refreshes remain necessary — even the summarization tier requires scheduled re-runs as facts accumulate.
  links: 56ffb2187d17cbe9
- `77e29c7c6707642d` [general] TrustMem verifies each memory-update transition on coverage, preservation, and faithfulness via a Memory Transition Verifier, then uses preference-pair RL over candidate updates; it reduced transition-level omission, corruption, and hallucination by 40.1%, 79.1%, and 50.0% respectively versus strongest per-error baselines. Source: arXiv 2606.25161.
  links: b06e866e7dd44247
- `7a5d99da7db6d539` [general] Official warning: the clippy restriction group should emphatically not be enabled as a whole — it may lint perfectly reasonable code and lints may contradict each other; use case-by-case (e.g. unwrap_used).
  links: 0dd854219451e9ec
- `7c293e99b03b9fc2` [general] rustc lints run during compilation and can produce a warning, an error, or nothing; warn-by-default lints exist because code "might be a bug" without being wrong.
  links: 8e715184141b7060
- `7dc38bcaf329e110` [general] Google Cloud Deploy shows a Review link in the delivery-pipeline visualization when a rollout is pending approval; each pending rollout opens an Approve rollout screen with a Manifest diff tab showing exactly what changed versus the current deploy, then Approve or Reject (console or 'gcloud deploy rollouts approve|reject').
  links: b3cea14ec5ed51db
- `7fb535112327a3fb` [general] LangGraph makes the same split explicitly: SqliteSaver is 'ideal for experimentation and local workflows' while PostgresSaver (used in LangSmith) is 'ideal for use in production'.
  links: 2b319322a0428380
- `8313bde3b0ba0e0b` [general] 'Test set contamination, wherein test data from a benchmark ends up in a newer model's training set, is a well-documented obstacle for fair LLM evaluation and can quickly render benchmarks obsolete.' (LiveBench)
  links: 952e1ad776c0a506
- `832663c967f96aa0` [general] Kubernetes audit events (audit.k8s.io/v1 Event) record user ('Authenticated user information'), verb, sourceIPs, requestURI, requestReceivedTimestamp, responseStatus, userAgent, and annotations.
  links: 1696dd59343bd054
- `84257652468055af` [general] Python SDK CVE-2026-59950 (GHSA-vj7q-gjh5-988w): deprecated WebSocket transport had no Host/Origin validation (CWE-346/CWE-1385), so cross-origin pages could drive the server; patched in 1.28.1.
  links: 6d2f6996dd28f2d0
- `855675ab421a6d0b` [general] Materialize rehydrates materialized views on restart from its own storage layer, not from upstream sources: hydration reconstructs in-memory state by reading Materialize's storage layer and does not require reading the upstream system.
  links: ed80831515a47d10
- `8559cdb96268dd29` [testing] Bash `set -e` (errexit) does not abort when a failing command is part of a while/until command list, part of an if test, part of a `&&`/`||` list (except the command following the final one), or any non-last pipeline command. A verifier relying on `set -e` alone can exit 0 despite a failed intermediate check (Bash Reference Manual §4.3.1).
  links: 9ced6c2413b3c6c5
- `862b7661a840e726` [general] People avoid algorithms after seeing them err even when the algorithm outperforms them, while being more forgiving of equivalent human errors (algorithm aversion). (Dietvorst, Simmons, Massey 2015, JEPG; verified at title/metadata level, full text not fetched)
  links: 1b0ebc092cb2ebe8
- `87458eb593e72446` [general] If an application, OS, or power failure occurs mid-transaction, SQLite's partially written transaction should be automatically rolled back the next time the database file is accessed; SQLite is 'not immune' to corruption.
  links: f0576e80a8acf6c5
- `886f02d1ed76d192` [general] MCP's nearest protocol-level drift signal is `notifications/tools/list_changed` — contract-change detection (a host detects its cached contract is stale), not behavioral drift.
  links: 579985981421cecf
- `8926e976facd2be8` [general] Landlock thread semantics: landlock_restrict_self restricts the calling thread and descendants; below ABI v8 (and at v8 without LANDLOCK_RESTRICT_SELF_TSYNC) a policy 'would only be enforced for the calling thread and its children (and not for all threads, including parents and siblings).' A multithreaded worker that restricts one thread can leave sibling threads unconfined.
  links: c7f2552aa3ba9e0c
- `8c15dbef1c8dcdeb` [general] CoALA (arXiv:2309.02427v3) defines agent memory as working memory plus long-term episodic, semantic, and procedural memory; 'learning' = writing to long-term memory, retrieval = reading it back. [fact]
  links: a25a4334cdf452aa
- `8e2215c3473b8233` [eval] run codex-exp-002-rerun is a strong run (composite >= 0.9).
  links: bb358b05b518130e
- `8e715184141b7060` [general] The `warnings` group covers all lints at warn level; #[deny(warnings)] lifts every warning to an error; unsafe_code is allow by default, so it is only caught after being raised into the warnings group.
  links: 7c293e99b03b9fc2
- `8eefc75285f050fd` [general] Mem0 scopes memory by user_id/agent_id/app_id/run_id on writes and reads to prevent data mixing; unmentioned entities are not constrained — searching {user_id: alice} does not require agent_id to be null, so cross-tagged records still surface (documented subtlety). [fact]
  links: df3c5fffc23e7509
- `8ffcf6eceee36cf2` [general] ADOPTED: writing-great-skills renamed to writing-for-agents, scope widened to anything agents read (skills, AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md, docs); ported doc-writing machinery (context pointers, two loads context/cognitive, information hierarchy, leading words, negation, no-ops test) as reference while KEEPING our kernel-seam machinery (contract, one-owner, hooks, versioning — which Matt lacks).
  links: 3358e0f2e6ca0096
- `9807eb38133c8148` [general] Generative Agents (arXiv:2304.03442 §4.1) retrieval score = alpha_recency*recency + alpha_importance*importance + alpha_relevance*relevance with all alpha = 1; recency is an exponential decay over sandbox game hours since last retrieval (decay factor 0.995); importance is an LLM-assigned 1-10 integer at creation; every memory object carries creation and most-recent-access timestamps; the memory stream is unbounded and decay only re-ranks at retrieval.
  links: 86494d2770f9e1c6
- `981a18a75e3286e0` [general] GitHub announced (Sept 2025) changing cache eviction checks from every 24 hours to every hour, noting increased eviction frequency could lead to additional cache thrashing.
  links: 081b26a9420810cb
- `9a3185f8c42ed664` [general] mcp-hub documents no policy for prereleases, release candidates, or nightly builds, and none have been published (registry shows only stable x.y.z).
  links: 486c92d91a5c5d33
- `9aa8824078e5adf2` [general] Clippy is a collection of >800 lints with default levels: correctness = deny, suspicious/style/complexity/perf = warn (together clippy::all); pedantic, restriction, nursery, cargo are allow by default.
  links: 75648dd5d0625730
- `9cc229d3928af9fd` [general] RouteLLM trains a binary 'win prediction' router P_θ(wins|q) from Chatbot Arena human preference data and routes to the strong model iff P(wins|q) >= cost threshold α. Four parameterizations: similarity-weighted (SW) Bradley–Terry ranking, matrix factorization, BERT classifier, causal LLM classifier (Llama 3 8B). (RouteLLM, arXiv 2406.18665v4, §3.1, §4.2)
  links: 4cf12f9a8fbae069
- `9ced6c2413b3c6c5` [testing] Bash pipeline exit status without pipefail is the exit status of the last command; with pipefail it is the last (rightmost) command to exit non-zero. `find ... | verify` hides a failing middle stage unless pipefail is on (Bash Reference Manual §3.2.3).
  links: 8559cdb96268dd29
- `9e2e2cacd723d017` [general] CrewAI per-agent view is a scope path (e.g. memory.scope('/agent/researcher')); slices compose several scopes into one view. [fact]
  links: 8aba31b1c97b13fb
- `9e867896987f3592` [general] Both seccomp and Landlock are one-way/irreversible: seccomp filters survive execve and can only be added to; Landlock: 'Once a thread is landlocked, there is no way to remove its security policy; only adding more restrictions is allowed.'
  links: 76649269e1ba035d
- `a1d9c54ea313be45` [general] Mem0 Dream merge folds duplicates into one canonical memory; the folded record is hidden from reads by default (the canonical memory is returned), retained rather than deleted, and surfaced with include_merged=true; runs as memories are added.
  links: 15dffb87cdfc3f1c
- `a25a4334cdf452aa` [general] CoALA warns learning by writing to procedural memory is significantly riskier than writing to episodic or semantic memory, as it can easily introduce bugs or allow an agent to subvert designers' intentions. [fact]
  links: 8c15dbef1c8dcdeb
- `a2648dcfb37a9a2f` [general] Anthropic: for subjective output, constraining output format and using structured outputs for guaranteed schema compliance reduce grader-side parsing flakiness.
  links: f3b28a55a63ea68b
- `a2ba28d2061e0152` [general] Claude Code treats hooks as not the enforcement layer: 'Because the `if` filter is best-effort, use the permission system rather than a hook to enforce a hard allow or deny', and a hook 'can deny the call, but staying silent doesn't approve it.'
  links: 900ff2712391e9e4
- `a46362f8efe48984` [general] mcp-hub's practice matches its declared semver policy: the registry sequence (62 versions 1.0.0 to 4.2.1) aligns with changelog entries — 2.0.0 (endpoints moved from URL params to server_name body), 3.0.0 (client registration endpoints removed), 4.0.0 (marketplace API schema/endpoint migration) — each released as a major with a '### Breaking Changes' section; features landed as minors and bug fixes as patches.
  links: 13cb6f40a083137a
- `a5be82962e50e626` [general] LangChain drift/issue detection is externalized to LangSmith Engine, which monitors traces, detects issues, proposes fixes, and can open a PR with the fix.
  links: ecb39c6174cac241
- `a66abf4c0c1f97cf` [general] Temporal Durable Execution: a workflow maintains its state and progress across failures, crashes, or server outages because the Event History records state at each step and the execution resumes from the last recorded event.
  links: a40757229890299e
- `a6c8b00124bf07c1` [general] A-MEM cost: "approximately 1,200 tokens per memory operation, achieving an 85–93% reduction in token usage compared to baseline methods (LoCoMo and MemGPT with 16,900 tokens)"; "each memory operation costing less than $0.0003"; processing "5.4 seconds using GPT-4o-mini and only 1.1 seconds with locally-hosted Llama 3.2 1B" [S7] §4.3.
  links: 073a5ff97a268f5f
- `a7f8334d85e65d51` [general] Do not open an SQLite database connection then fork() then use that connection in the child process — locking problems result and the database can easily end up corrupt; under Unix an open SQLite database should not be carried across a fork() into the child process.
  links: f0576e80a8acf6c5
- `a9602827037b1026` [general] LoCoMo long-context QA: gpt-4-turbo scored 32.4 vs human 87.9; a 16k-context model fell to 2.1% on adversarial questions while a 4k-context model got 70.2%. (arXiv:2402.17753)
  links: a6e2c69032154967
- `adc8bcda2c13dd44` [general] Anthropic: 'An eval suite is a living artifact that needs ongoing attention and clear ownership'; effective structure was dedicated evals teams owning infrastructure while domain/product teams contribute tasks; automated evals require ongoing maintenance as product and model evolve to avoid drift.
  links: 24f290b647ef095e
- `b1016827b3e68aa6` [general] LangGraph subgraphs manage their own checkpoint namespace, so the parent does not see subgraph state changes; the docs' prescribed escape hatch for cross-boundary sharing is shared state via Store. [fact]
  links: c65a4ce6cdb624b8
- `b1aa3b401a49fed0` [general] Cargo's official CI chapter shows testing across stable/beta/nightly channels, verifying the rust-version field with cargo-hack/cargo-msrv, and a separate latest-dependencies job (with continue-on-error) to keep dependency ranges honest.
  links: 81601e15dda67071
- `b6926ac40cee68e9` [general] Zep paper caveat (arXiv:2501.13956 §4.2): DMR conversations contain only 60 messages each, easily fitting within current LLM context windows, so DMR barely exercises retention.
  links: 672b4912c42144b2
- `b7ce46afba9f4ef1` [general] MCP-38 (arXiv 2603.18063) defines a 38-category MCP threat taxonomy (tool-description poisoning, indirect prompt injection, parasitic tool chaining, dynamic trust violations) mapped to STRIDE and OWASP LLM/Agentic frameworks; a companion SoK (arXiv 2512.08290) systematizes indirect prompt injection and tool poisoning and surveys defenses (cryptographic provenance, runtime intent verification).
  links: 38956b8103d9e760
- `b85e02376e6436d9` [general] Kubernetes treats userAgent as explicitly untrusted: 'the UserAgent is provided by the client, and must not be trusted.'
  links: 6bc015d1e12cd076
- `b8a1e48bd9d892f9` [ai-auditability] EU AI Act Art 26(10): post-remote biometric identification 'shall request an authorisation, ex ante… by a judicial authority or an administrative authority whose decision is binding and subject to judicial review'; if the authorisation is rejected, use stops immediately and the linked personal data is deleted.
  links: 30a1f40562cac11e
- `bb358b05b518130e` [eval] run codex-exp-003-rerun is a strong run (composite >= 0.9).
  links: 8e2215c3473b8233
- `bd7bc6f9009fc641` [general] Mem0 per-memory expiration: an expiration_date (YYYY-MM-DD, UTC, date-inclusive) stops the memory surfacing in search once the date passes; nothing is deleted — search() and get_all() skip it, fetching by ID still returns it, clearing the date restores it; no date means never expires (default); malformed dates fail open.
  links: 15dffb87cdfc3f1c
- `be804b3d215392f2` [ai-auditability] OWASP: plain logs have weak non-repudiation — 'non-repudiation is hard to achieve for logs because their trustworthiness is often just based on the logging party being audited properly while mechanisms like digital signatures are hard to utilize here.'
  links: d1b3d3ef01bfcfff
- `bf6ba5ce46a474d0` [general] Anthropic eval design principles: be task-specific, 'automate when possible' (multiple-choice, string match, code-graded, LLM-graded), and 'prioritize volume over quality' (many automated-graded questions over fewer human-graded ones).
  links: 67ab1b96cc2d1128
- `bfe28bd5ddad1fe0` [general] Mini AGI CI gate fails unless the runner identifies itself as isolated (non-root + RUNNER_NAME), so 'gate requires sandbox evidence' is literal (ADR-0009); the local worker runs under Landlock write-containment confined to workdir + its own state dir (ADR-0012).
  links: 7c6f0eb74c7ce6fc
- `c0fb76a443969f8b` [general] An adaptive conformal semantic-entropy score with an accept/abstain decision rule carries a finite-sample, distribution-free bound on error rate among accepted answers (TriviaQA AUROC 0.88 vs 0.65 for token entropy) — conformal-guaranteed abstention is deployable. (Karimi et al., IJCAI 2026, arXiv 2605.04295; verified abstract)
  links: 2512869e54f72f96
- `c5fada8b53377287` [general] LightMem runtime (memory-bank construction) speedups over eager baselines: 2.9x-12.4x (GPT), 1.6x-6.3x (Qwen) on LongMemEval-S (LightMem §5.2).
  links: fe6a2d93dd65d35c
- `c73eba32cd1e01e5` [ai-auditability] Uncertain: no single standard prescribes how to combine all three mechanisms for AI decision auditability — no primary source reached does; it is legal text plus practice guides, not one integrated spec.
  links: 3b3bae3fd783ee40
- `c7f2552aa3ba9e0c` [general] Landlock is a stackable LSM restricting operations on objects (file hierarchies via filesystem access rights; TCP/UDP ports via network access rights since ABI v4/v10; IPC via scope flags since ABI v6), enforced on a thread and its descendants (source: landlock.rst, landlock(7)).
  links: 8926e976facd2be8
- `c804819854579d9d` [general] Mem0 does incremental consolidation at write time plus an async background summarizer: extraction runs per message pair; the update phase runs an LLM tool call per candidate fact choosing ADD / UPDATE / DELETE / NOOP; the "asynchronous summary generation module that periodically refreshes the conversation summary ... operates independently of the main processing pipeline" [S6] §2.1.
  links: 93aab397ab2425d6
- `c83ae8c7cde41572` [general] SEP-1024 (Final, Standards Track) mandates for one-click local MCP server installation: a consent dialog showing the exact untruncated command, explicit affirmative approval, and a cancel path (motivated by silent command execution, lack of visibility, arbitrary code execution via crafted configs).
  links: c3484bbb30701da2
- `c94749b55ef6938d` [general] HumanLayer's original HITL-API product is deprecated — the repo README states "the code here is pretty much all deprecated"; the current product (humanlayer.com) is an AI-coding IDE whose marketing positions task/session grouping, real-time session visibility, and cloud daemons as the way to supervise many parallel agent sessions. Only the marketing homepage and repo README were verified; no detailed supervision-docs were reachable (docs.humanlayer.dev transport-error), and its efficacy as a supervision console is an unverified opinion.
  links: 8e916f9507714e83
- `cb86a545057edd22` [general] LangSmith alerts are threshold-based on aggregated metrics (run count, cost, errors count or %, average feedback score, latency) over a configurable 5 or 15 minute window, are project-scoped, can be further scoped by a filter builder (status/run type/tag/error), and route to Slack, PagerDuty, Dynatrace, or any webhook.
  links: a4d77e88c7e61873
- `cba421bad5c5a34c` [general] Zep vs full-context baseline on LongMemEval (~115k token conversations): accuracy +15.2% (gpt-4o-mini) and +18.5% (gpt-4o); latency 31.3s→3.20s and 28.9s→2.58s ("reducing response times by approximately 90%"); context tokens 115k→1.6k [S5] §4.3.2.
  links: acbe5b346b26c040
- `cbf6476b32f767bc` [general] mcp-hub is an npm package (author "Ravitemer"), MIT-licensed, repository git+https://github.com/ravitemer/mcp-hub.git; latest dist-tag is 4.2.1; every published version is a plain x.y.z with no prerelease tags.
  links: 666be2622013c3ae
- `cc5ee7db3c43ac65` [general] mcp-hub PyPI lookup returned 404 (pypi.org/pypi/mcp-hub/json) — no Python distribution of this name; the package in question is the npm one.
  links: 1b264bd2db12c508
- `ccc01d856b49501a` [general] Skill-layer changes are USER-governed (HITL): consumer and sole writer is the USER; the falsifier is gate-bound — mini-agi skill verify --all must stay 14+ PASS and one-owner lint must pass after any skill edit; to-questionnaire hook must verify the template + grill-the-send rule.
  links: da7d898b4b623136
- `ce2aee7d1f5ece13` [general] MemGPT formalized OS-style virtual context management: 'intelligently manages different memory tiers... providing extended context within the LLM's limited context window.' (Packer et al., arXiv:2310.08560)
  links: e368dbd927efdfc0
- `d0090b3e0a85c898` [general] Microsoft Agent Framework ships a separate 'vector-stores-and-embeddings' feature area, i.e. durable memory is treated as a database/vector-store concern rather than a filesystem one.
  links: ccecea1af23feed5
- `d0a70761a296b570` [general] Aegis (arXiv 2508.19504) was measured on 142 failed traces / 3,656 turns across 5 benchmarks (TauBench airline & retail, BFCL file system, CRM-Arena, MedAgentBench) and 3 models (GPT-4.1, GPT-4.1 mini, o3); failures localized to the first unsuccessful subtask using an HTN-inspired subtask abstraction; resource exhaustion accounted for 45%/83% of retail/CRM failures under GPT-4.1.
  links: 02a02d256e8ffada
- `d1df44ba19d24fa5` [general] Seccomp: BPF 'may not dereference pointers,' so pathnames, buffers, and most argument content are invisible; only register values are visible.
  links: d71aa36a9e81bc8c
- `d25b446e25b94b13` [general] mcp-hub CONTRIBUTING.md instructs PR authors to update CHANGELOG.md as part of the pull-request process — the mechanism feeding the changelog release gate.
  links: 675e3592262ae39f
- `d479456f357031ec` [general] mcp-hub documents no support/maintenance policy for older major versions (no LTS window, no deprecation/end-of-life statement for past majors).
  links: 486c92d91a5c5d33
- `d58edf0e65fb0f8f` [general] CouchDB caches reduce results in B-tree interior nodes, so range-aggregate queries are answered from cached partial reductions, recalculating only a minimum number of nodes.
  links: 6dbd271b7ce0e25e
- `d5b02e7e2030b2b9` [general] DeepSeek officially warns it plans to raise overall API pricing significantly in the near future, subject to official notice — so deepseek-v4-flash rates are time-sensitive and only valid until the pricing page changes.
  links: c93ad27aa8c7bb3a
- `da7d898b4b623136` [general] ADOPTED: to-questionnaire — new repo-local, hook-bound skill for decisions a THIRD PARTY holds: decision the user can't answer becomes a questionnaire DOC (async), grill only the SEND (who + what you need back), most-important-first, one idea per question, disable-model-invocation. Composes with domain-modeling.
  links: ccc01d856b49501a
- `db8bc4fd1714d223` [general] GitHub code review auto assignment picks reviewers by one of two algorithms: round robin (who has received the least recent request) or load balance (counts recent and outstanding reviews, aiming for an equal number per member within any 30-day period); members whose status is 'Busy' are excluded.
  links: fa9b089b4e75b0cb
- `dc228d975c9158da` [general] OpenAI Agents SDK guardrails are model-as-judge: input guardrails run before the main model, output guardrails validate final output, tool guardrails check arguments/results, and a tripwire blocks the run. Boundary caveat: input guardrails run only for the first agent in a chain and output guardrails only at the final-output agent, so checks belong next to the side-effecting tool in manager-style workflows.
  links: a7ce42ab7d93d083
- `de06970103940658` [general] A-MEM (2025) does per-interaction continuous evolution: each new interaction triggers note construction (LLM keywords/tags/context), top-k link generation, and "memory evolution" that rewrites the contextual representation of existing memories [S7] §3.1–3.3.
  links: 8014b8b82d793b73
- `df2131dec381bc98` [general] Sandbox manifests treat path grants as trusted config: treat manifests that contain extra_path_grants as trusted configuration, do not load grants from model output or other untrusted payloads unless the application has already approved those host paths, and read_only=True is recommended for host data the sandbox should not modify.
  links: ddf4bfc7760221e4
- `df3c5fffc23e7509` [general] Mem0 documents a leakage-by-default rule: unmentioned entities in a search filter are not constrained, so cross-tagged records still surface if the caller is careless. [fact]
  links: 8eefc75285f050fd
- `dfaec127fda77e74` [repair] Melo grounding ablation (measured): entity misidentification 17.4% → 9.6% (−7.8 pp) with full defense, at +2.5 s UNDERSTAND / +1.8 s PLAN P50 latency; L3 alone −1.2 pp, +L2 −3.8 pp, +L1 −7.8 pp cumulative — catalog evidence alone is not enough, the LLM needs explicit weighting rules.
  links: 70c12cac8fc7ae06
- `e113f24a5245b284` [general] gen_ai.invoke_workflow.duration metric: unit s; conditional error.type and gen_ai.workflow.name.
  links: 55a5de088bb8a4cb
- `e39035055344f0fc` [general] SQLite checkpoint starvation: if concurrent overlapping readers mean there is always at least one active reader, checkpoints never complete and the WAL grows without bound, causing excess disk use and slow queries.
  links: 57a4ba0fa591b0c8
- `e4a2fe363829afa9` [repair] Melo online A/B (1M users, April 2–May 10): >2 pp primary playlist-retention lift and >1 min core engagement lift, reported as lower bounds with no CIs/significance; novelty and product-surface effects not fully ruled out; node-level cost dominated by LLM reasoning not tool dispatch (UNDERSTAND 8.0 s + PLAN 8.2 s + SYNTHESIZE 10.3 s P50 of 26.0 s end-to-end; EXECUTE 0.04 s; triggered-session REFLECT adds 6.4 s P50 LLM call).
  links: 70c12cac8fc7ae06
- `e5e1072ffa4930c8` [general] Braintrust score visibility can restrict scores to specific members/groups or show them conditionally (e.g., only when a triage score indicates deeper review is needed), and is documented as a display filter, not an access control.
  links: 693010a524f0a9fb
- `e88f28106f9d0f13` [general] Conformal prediction for generative outputs: a calibrated stopping+rejection rule over LM sampling returns a small set of candidate answers containing at least one acceptable answer with high probability, and can flag individual spans (phrases/sentences) as non-hallucinated with statistical guarantees. (Quach et al., Conformal Language Modeling, ICLR 2024)
  links: 67ab1b96cc2d1128
- `e9488afde6379a28` [general] A-MEM retrieval scales to 1M memories at 3.70 µs with linear storage [S7] §4.6; on DialSim F1 3.45 vs LoCoMo 2.55 and MemGPT 1.18 [S7] §4.3.
  links: 073a5ff97a268f5f
- `e9e5cfc1d0b1386a` [general] deepseek-v4-flash pricing has exactly three cells per model (cache-hit input, cache-miss input, output) — cache-hit vs cache-miss is the only split; there is no separate standard-vs-thinking input tier, though thinking mode is billed at the same input/output rates.
  links: c93ad27aa8c7bb3a
- `ea85a2da072f7a6a` [general] LiveBench mitigates contamination by (1) frequently updated questions drawn from recent math competitions, arXiv papers, news, and datasets ('questions are added and updated on a monthly basis'), and (2) harder 'contamination-limited' versions of prior benchmarks.
  links: 952e1ad776c0a506
- `ec84de5da723d4a8` [general] SQLite WAL mode lets readers and writers proceed concurrently, but since there is only one WAL file there can only be one writer at a time.
  links: 601ae8b56f00daf0
- `ecb39c6174cac241` [general] LangSmith Engine automatically detects recurring issues in traces, diagnoses root cause, and proposes resolution, as an alternative to manually triaging every failed run.
  links: a5be82962e50e626
- `eee720517a9475e3` [general] Generative Agents (arXiv:2304.03442 §4.2) reflection recursively synthesizes observations into higher-level 'reflections' appended to the memory stream; the stream is compacted in meaning, not in size.
  links: 86494d2770f9e1c6
- `ef358e65af2c75f9` [general] Landlock does not and cannot filter syscalls or deny syscall-level primitives (e.g. ptrace of a peer is mediated via domain-subset rules, but no generic syscall kill).
  links: a68feba40bd78382
- `f0ed662457e1c79e` [repair] TCMIIES (system report): measured per-task API cost anchor of roughly $0.38/1,000 papers (DeepSeek v4-flash) to $3.41 (Qwen max) at ~2,000 input + 500 output tokens per paper — a rare measured per-task cost figure for extraction (not isolating retry cost).
  links: 73454769bc163ce3
- `f2c01f5064674e32` [general] mcp-hub's scripts/release.sh mechanically enforces semver: must run on main with a clean tree, bumps via 'npm --no-git-tag-version version', gates the release on a matching '## [new-version]' entry in CHANGELOG.md (aborts and reverts otherwise), creates annotated tag vX.Y.Z, one 'chore(release): vX.Y.Z' commit, npm publish, and a GitHub release.
  links: 34fc1c71481976d6
- `f4790ce2a495fb98` [ai-auditability] OWASP log separation: process monitoring, audit, and transaction logs/trails are usually collected for different purposes than security event logging, and this often means they should be kept separate.
  links: 77ad6114bf5546c2
- `f481b98eb5ba6799` [general] CouchDB runs the map function over all documents only once, on first query; when a document is changed, the map function runs only once for that single document.
  links: 9b9296bb8b2fc470
- `f63bce506848d83d` [general] Seccomp unknown actions default to kill (kernel treats unknown action as SECCOMP_RET_KILL_PROCESS), so forward-compatibility mistakes tend to fail-safe; filter size caps: BPF_MAXINSNS 4096 per program, MAX_INSNS_PER_PATH 32768 total.
  links: d71aa36a9e81bc8c
- `f640281634a3ed98` [general] gen_ai.invoke_agent.inference_calls metric: unit {inference_call}; recommended gen_ai.agent.name.
  links: 34b2aac2c52f66d1
- `f6efa46afbd3c05c` [evidence-status] mini-agi `loop verify` keeps the claim held on disagreement (test: 'claim must stay held when the verifier disagrees').
  links: 8af8f2689671ac0a
- `f799ae94c5278f06` [general] Anthropic's engineering blog 'Effective context engineering for AI agents' (2025) asserts that giving the model less context is generally better: every unneeded token is both a confusion risk and a budget waste. Core techniques are context narrowing (retrieval pulling only relevant snippets), dynamic context assembly per call, entity memory/scratchpads, and summarization (a separate compression category for high-level understanding only). It warns against dumping full call transcripts into subagent calls. Source: https://www.anthropic.com/engineering/effective-context-engineering-for-agents
  links: 1c1bd4db44906d9f
- `f89a7953b2d236cf` [general] Kafka's log-compaction and EventStoreDB projections as mechanisms for derived state are unverified: the Kafka documentation site served only a JS redirect and EventStoreDB's projections docs were unreachable, so compacted-topic-as-derived-state and projection-over-event-stream claims must not be attributed to primary sources until fetched directly.
  links: 7b3058140a429de1
- `f8fc8c1992134bc3` [general] mcp-hub states no minimum-version-support promise for Node beyond the README runtime requirement 'Node.js >= 18.0.0'.
  links: 486c92d91a5c5d33
- `f9a27846604a85ce` [general] NOMAD (Giannouris & Ananiadou; arXiv 2511.22409) presents an error taxonomy for LLM-generated UML diagrams covering structural, relationship, and semantic/logical errors.
  links: 4d082f928948911f
- `fac488c6012dc0f4` [general] MCP identity is carried per-request in _meta (io.modelcontextprotocol/clientInfo); the protocol is stateless and does not dictate how applications use LLMs or manage context; local (stdio) servers serve a single client, remote (Streamable HTTP) servers serve many; scoping which servers a project sees is entirely a client-side concern.
  links: d39c4dd366a95d2e
- `fb854e84fe169d26` [general] MCP human-in-the-loop: for trust & safety and security there SHOULD always be a human in the loop able to deny tool invocations, with visible tools and confirmation prompts (Spec 2026-07-28 Tools).
  links: 854c0680bf360524
- `fb857bcd7b9b0020` [general] Google runs an automated quarantine tool that monitors test flakiness and, when flakiness is 'too high' (threshold not specified in the source), automatically removes the test from the critical path and files a bug to reduce flakiness; this could mask a real race condition or other bug. (Google Testing Blog, 2016)
  links: 3158dabb8476e655
- `fe75beb9da3fa6ba` [general] Chroma context rot (18 models, Jul 2025): significantly higher performance on focused prompts (~300 tokens) vs full prompts (~113k tokens) on LongMemEval; distractor effects amplify with length; even simple tasks degrade as input grows. Supports pruning irrelevant context rather than growing the window.
  links: 3d1604aaeeb8b61b
- `fe7a1f40036e0381` [general] Braintrust's anti-noise mechanism is a beta daily "Topics digest" posted once a day to Slack covering the previous 24h — summary of activity shift, biggest topic mover, highlight traces, and one takeaway — rather than per-event alerts.
  links: daf69938aa31c832
- `008019449a1687e0` [ai-auditability] SLSA provenance is 'an attestation that a particular build platform produced a set of software artifacts through execution of the buildDefinition'; the model separates buildDefinition (buildType, untrusted externalParameters, trusted internalParameters, resolvedDependencies with digests) from runDetails (builder.id, invocationId, timestamps, byproducts); 'Consumers MUST accept only specific signer-builder pairs.'
- `00b76328e304b094` [general] The semantic-conventions-genai repo's Schema URL field is still marked TODO.
- `01c89c6dfe6fb3b6` [general] OpenAI regression-eval recipe: define one eval (data source + grader), run a baseline run against the current prompt, then a candidate ('regression-run') after a prompt change; the lower score on the same dataset flags the regression before shipping.
- `027989abc31d7253` [general] CrewAI: failure recording + event bus provide raw data for drift analysis but no drift detection policy itself.
- `029d332977786104` [general] Linking two copies of SQLite into one application, using two different locking protocols, unlinking/renaming the file while open, and multiple hard/symlinks to the file are all documented sources of SQLite corruption.
- `06b55f283657c3cb` [general] Mem0 Dream synthesis distills memories into higher-order pattern memories written alongside existing memories, never in place of them, gated on at least 20 memories per user and a cadence (7 days Pro, daily Enterprise).
- `08873b7cec6db336` [evidence-status] mini-agi ADR-0011: runs may declare two optional fields, `verify_command` (e.g. `make verify`, `npx tsc && node --test`) and `verify_target`; `mini-agi run verify <run.json>` executes the command in the target repo.
- `090f7beeffd3f929` [general] No vendor documentation was verified that quantifies how much notification noise these controls actually reduce — no measured alert-fatigue data was found in primary docs, and no claim about effectiveness is made.
- `0a125f2cbc31901b` [general] An empirical MCPApps study (arXiv 2607.25635, 1,723 apps) found only 37.2% gate tool execution behind a blocking approval step, leaving the LLM able to invoke any enabled tool unconditionally in most apps (study-reported).
- `0b5271a53bb638e2` [ai-auditability] EU AI Act retention: providers must keep logs 'for a period appropriate to the intended purpose… of at least six months' (Art 19(1)); deployers have the same six-month minimum (Art 26(6)); financial institutions keep them as part of internal-governance documentation.
- `0b8e26b881b8812b` [general] OpenAI (SDK + platform): no behavioral-drift detection appears in any page reached; the only lifecycle primitive is the SDK's observability hooks, and the platform skills guide advises 'inspect … before using', not periodic re-validation.
- `0b90b61b3081c07d` [general] Mini AGI makes memory writes human-signed: contested facts and dream-loop results route through memory signoff / a human queue (ADR-0010 signoff); the reviewer gate is human by design.
- `0bcee47092412e6c` [strategy] D3: Work graph (ADR-0008): tickets carry `blocked_by` edges; claims
- `0d7dd4cfacab9117` [strategy] D9: Claims/leases recorded only by CLI commands, never hand-edited;
- `0f170eb63104f9a4` [general] OpenAI Agents SDK hooks: `RunHooks`/`AgentHooks` receive `on_tool_start`/`on_tool_end` (immediately before/after a local tool is invoked) plus `on_agent_start/end`, `on_handoff`, `on_llm_start/end`, attached per-run or per-agent; the `@tool` wrapper adds the runtime pipeline (schema validation, context injection, guardrails, timeouts, failure handling, tracing) around the raw callable, and calling `__wrapped__` bypasses it.
- `0f35beac0db18d9e` [strategy] R1: Scorer semantics changes without ADR — rejected by contract
- `0f4166a712ef9d9d` [general] CI gate: cargo fmt --all -- --check exits non-zero if formatting changes would be made.
- `0f5173c2ca304d27` [general] OpenAI SDK architecture keeps state out of the sandbox by design: the outer runtime owns approvals, tracing, handoffs, and resume bookkeeping, while the sandbox session owns commands, file changes, and environment isolation; the conversational Session is explicitly distinct from a sandbox session.
- `0fc5cf16dc6d0efd` [general] REJECTED: web dashboard for solo kernel (AFK-SUPERVISOR v2 doc) —
- `0fe733e6d264cd16` [general] A measurement study of 6 MCP marketplaces (arXiv 2509.25292, 8,060 servers) found more than half of listed projects invalid or low-value, plus dependency-monoculture and maintenance risks (study-reported).
- `10442347209cddee` [strategy] Operational incidents: OOM pressure from 500 agent-browser processes
- `1051969642545360` [general] Liang et al. (EMNLP 2024) document 'Degeneration-of-Thought': once an LLM has established confidence, iterative self-reflection 'is unable to generate novel thoughts,' so reflection alone stagnates; their multi-agent debate outperformed it on commonsense translation and counter-intuitive arithmetic, and they find an LLM is 'not a fair judge' across different models (arXiv:2305.19118).
- `105b148c153f0970` [ai-auditability] Architecture Decision Records (ADR): a short text file recording one 'architecturally significant' decision with sections Title, Context, Decision, Status, Consequences; records numbered sequentially and monotonically, never reused; a reversed decision is kept but marked 'superseded' with a reference to its replacement; kept in version control under doc/arch/adr-NNN.md.
- `1177064e1ab14cee` [general] LAB-TEST: prototype LOGIC branch — build one shareable HTML prototype (free-play + tabbed walkthroughs, drivable by a non-developer) vs our TUI on the next logic-prototype task; no observed local TUI failure so no forced change.
- `11e6d3fb133e330e` [general] DECIDED: reviewers = devils-advocate (roast, evidence-first);
- `122ebf323a7b873b` [general] Required attributes on the inference span are gen_ai.operation.name and gen_ai.provider.name.
- `144ef8576bb7622e` [strategy] D2: Tool-mismatch signal closed as a loop (Phase 6.2): gate
- `1473b45f4850c8dd` [general] Semantic entropy (sampling-based uncertainty at the meaning level) detects hallucinations/confabulations across datasets and tasks without a priori task knowledge and without task-specific data, and helps users understand when they must take extra care with LLMs. (Farquhar et al., Nature 630:625-630, 2024)
- `15216a4151961b81` [general] MCP in-scope security categories: protocol-level flaws, auth/authz bypasses, SDK implementation bugs, sandbox escapes, session hijacking, token theft/leakage, cross-tenant access (SECURITY.md).
- `1694c889dc5b5cdf` [general] Subagent architectures bound context growth to a summary: a subagent 'returns only a condensed, distilled summary of its work (often 1,000–2,000 tokens)' and the main agent's context grows by that summary, not the full subtask transcript.
- `180a819281a6e18b` [general] Unverified: scale-out behavior of a flat-file model across many worker processes — both Claude Code and the MCP memory server are explicitly single-machine/single-process.
- `18172dbe2a271091` [general] cargo test runs unit tests in src, integration tests in tests/, compiles examples, and runs doctests.
- `1ab0e2db5aa0ab3d` [general] Skills can restrict their own invocation and tools: disable-model-invocation: true (only the user can trigger, recommended for /deploy-style skills) and allowed-tools/disallowed-tools frontmatter.
- `1afb9fd832180da7` [ai-auditability] NIST AI RMF 1.0 (released 26 Jan 2023, voluntary) organizes risk management into four functions — Govern, Map, Measure, Manage — with a companion Playbook of suggested actions per sub-category.
- `1ba70b1569d53382` [general] LangGraph's contamination boundary is convention, not enforcement — safety relies on every writer scoping to the correct namespace and every reader querying within its namespace. [analysis]
- `1c96892b7fff88dd` [general] A restore-keys hit restores the most recent prefix-matching cache, which may predate the current lockfile; docs recommend still running the install step on a non-exact hit.
- `1d90ab8fc8f95fe1` [general] Cargo compiles dependencies with --cap-lints allow so dependency warnings do not pollute build output; force-warn lints are exempt from this cap.
- `1e1b3ca106a78e3b` [testing] The formal notion of vacuity in verification originates from temporal model checking: Beer, Ben-David, Eisner, and Rodeh formalize vacuity and interesting witnesses and detect them in temporal model checking ("Efficient Detection of Vacuity in Temporal Model Checking", FMSD 18(2):141-163, 2001, DOI 10.1023/A:1008779610539).
- `1ff9a1f840eadafe` [strategy] 142 tests, 20 baseline cases, 42 canonical facts, 20 runs ingested.
- `208b7ba338e48067` [general] Anthropic: 'eval saturation occurs when an agent passes all of the solvable tasks, leaving no room for improvement'; SWE-bench Verified went from ~30% to >80% for frontier models in about a year and 'large capability improvements then appear as small increases in scores'; Anthropic's rule is to not trust scores until transcripts are read.
- `211ef594acd91688` [general] The GenAI semantic convention set is NOT yet stable — every span, attribute, and metric carries Development stability (as of fetch from main branch on 2026-08-06).
- `2142573ef60a16f5` [general] Coverage caveats: CRITIC was verified from its abstract only (no accessible full text); the 2025–2026 papers are arXiv preprints, not peer-reviewed; no claim was verified that could not be read in a primary source.
