- `21ee0cd54dbd7b92` [general] Anthropic vendor-internal evals (published in launch post, methodology internal and not reproducible — treat as claims): combining memory tool with context editing improved performance by 39% over baseline; context editing alone 29%; in a 100-turn web-search eval context editing finished workflows that would otherwise fail due to context exhaustion while reducing token consumption by 84%. (claude.com/blog/context-management)
- `249cc166e0a084fe` [general] Harness-sensitivity taxonomy (Cho et al.; arXiv 2605.26731) is a six-label taxonomy from a 432-run controlled experiment; format_violation dominates capable models while wrong_file dominates low-capability models.
- `24fb2af3014fffe6` [general] MCP's stdio transport is not a sandbox; 'one stdio peer can crash, hang, exhaust resources of, or otherwise deny service to the other' is out of scope, and deployments running stdio at reduced privilege own isolation enforcement (SECURITY.md).
- `2623d2b56aa80b7a` [ai-auditability] OWASP application-log content guidance: 'The application logs must record when, where, who and what for each event,' with per-attribute fields including event type, severity, interaction identifier, user identity, action, object, result status, and reason.
- `2658adb1b6119494` [general] Pruning/truncation = dropping raw transcript items (tool results, old turns, unplayed audio) without replacing them with a summary.
- `276b963419de5d5e` [general] AWS defines AWSService as 'The request was made by an AWS account that belongs to an AWS service' (e.g. Elastic Beanstalk assuming a role to call other services) — a dedicated kind for automated processes, distinct from human identity kinds.
- `27864aab0d2d7442` [general] Claude Agent SDK auto-compacts when context window approaches limit: emits a compact_boundary message; a PreCompact hook allows archiving the full transcript before summarizing — the documented way to keep evidence. (Claude Agent SDK, agent-loop)
- `282a35001a555f06` [general] Pipeline map (ORIENT/PLAN, DECIDE, RESEARCH, BUILD, VERIFY, KNOWLEDGE, ORCHESTRATE, MODE) shows the real gap is RESEARCH; name-overlaps were not where gaps were. to-questionnaire closed the only chain-break in DECIDE.
- `2933dd39c8955454` [research] Gas Town 'formula' term: a formula is a template for a piece of work — the recipe a polecat cooks (the only new term in the 2026-08-08 lexicon draft; all other lexicon terms were already canonical in F-008 `0ff9b686f9e72fc3` from 2026-08-03, which remains the canonical lexicon fact). - supersedes: c3dff66fff8c9fda
- `293aa0c6db3e1be3` [general] Two studies of 7 MCP clients (Claude Desktop, Claude Code, Cursor, Cline, Continue, Gemini CLI, Langflow) identify tool poisoning (malicious instructions embedded in tool metadata/descriptions) as the most prevalent client-side vector, with wide disparities (Claude Desktop 'implements strong guardrails', Cursor 'exhibits high susceptibility to cross-tool poisoning') (reported; arXiv 2603.21642, 2603.22489).
- `2a2fdcbaa3ed5e38` [ai-auditability] in-toto (CNCF graduated project) 'is designed to ensure the integrity of a software product from initiation to end-user installation… making it transparent to the user what steps were performed, by whom and in what order.'
- `2c0287690e80db51` [general] Evidence is not destroyed, only removed from the model-visible window: every Claude Code session transcript is written to a JSONL file under ~/.claude/projects/; the SDK can mirror transcripts to S3/Redis so any host can resume.
- `2c3f7e1718461895` [general] Mini AGI canonical memory is append-only with provenance on every entry: dated, carries a source, every fact id is sha256[:16]; derived views are generated, never hand-edited, and canonical wins on conflict.
- `2cafefe7720da0b8` [evidence-status] Karpathy (opinion): 'Software 1.0 easily automates what you can specify. Software 2.0 easily automates what you can verify'; a verifiable environment must be resettable, efficient, and rewardable.
- `2d020d15e3ee5a95` [general] Persisting cost telemetry into the store is application-driven, not automatic: OpenAI AdvancedSQLiteSession provides detailed usage analytics by storing token usage per conversation turn, entirely dependent on the store_run_usage method being called after each agent run, into a turn_usage table keyed by (session_id, branch_id, user_turn_number).
- `2d27b81edd2936f7` [general] After Claude Code compaction, project CLAUDE.md, auto memory, and MCP tools reload; the skill listing is the one startup item not re-injected.
- `2d6892e7c6dcefc2` [general] Claude Code's large-codebases analysis frames context as a two-sided trade: too much context loaded every session degrades performance, too little leaves Claude to navigate blind; RAG-style embeddings are avoided because pipelines can't keep up with active engineering teams and retrieval may return a function renamed two weeks ago, whereas each developer's instance reads the live checkout.
- `2eafea4e6eeb8e76` [general] AutoGen (AgentChat): a Memory (e.g. ListMemory, ChromaDBVectorMemory, RedisMemory) is constructed and passed into a specific agent's constructor; the only way two agents share memory is passing the same instance; no framework-level permission scoping, filtering is via metadata you supply. [fact]
- `30e358a2763714ce` [general] Magar & Schwartz (ACL 2022): pretrained models are trained on web corpora 'often contaminated' with downstream test sets; controlled experiments show exploitation 'exists in some cases, but in others the models memorize the contaminated data, but do not exploit it'; memorization vs exploitation is affected by duplication count and model size.
- `30f149934a3231a5` [general] METR (2026-03-10): repo maintainers merged only ~68% of original human-written ('golden') patches, so METR normalizes all agent scores against this golden baseline rather than treating any single grader as exact.
- `31742f4c5353a826` [general] Anthropic treats tool-result clearing ('context editing') as 'one of the safest lightest touch forms of compaction'.
- `31c35c7e43c70949` [general] TS SDK CVE-2026-25536 (GHSA-345p-7cg4-v4c7, high, CVSS 7.1): reusing a StreamableHTTPServerTransport or single McpServer across clients causes JSON-RPC ID-collision response misrouting / cross-client data leak (CWE-362); patched in 1.26.0.
- `31eed941a6bf3c22` [general] OpenTelemetry GenAI semantic conventions moved out of the main opentelemetry/semantic-conventions repo into a dedicated repository open-telemetry/semantic-conventions-genai; the old pages at opentelemetry.io/docs/specs/semconv/gen-ai/ are redirect stubs pointing there.
- `3264c807ce55f96c` [general] BM25 is a robust retrieval baseline; re-ranking and late-interaction models on average achieve the best zero-shot BEIR performances but at high computational cost, while dense and sparse-retrieval models often underperform. Exact per-dataset BEIR nDCG@10 values were not extractable (paper HTML render 404'd). Source: arXiv:2104.08663 abstract.
- `33379f8028c507ae` [general] SWE-bench Verified (OpenAI, 93 professional annotators, each sample labeled 3x, ensembled by max severity) filtered out 68.3% of the original SWE-bench test set for underspecified problem statements (38.3%), unit tests that unfairly reject valid solutions (61.1%), or other issues.
- `3362eb5951742335` [strategy] Yegge's Gas Town died with Opus 4.7 from a model tic — "just two more things": the model kept fiddling with the harness itself instead of converging on real work; harnesses are becoming bespoke, chemically bonded to the application — reusable harness frameworks are on their way out.
- `33d27d200a99d5c1` [strategy] Dogfood evidence: scratch checkpoint.sh rolled back the scratch repo
- `34638b8e92dfd7dd` [general] CoALA notes modifying and deleting (unlearning) are understudied in recent language agents. [fact]
- `38e05948dad83b29` [general] docs/MEMORY-RESEARCH.md (deep memory+skills pass): Anthropic context
- `3ae34cbd57701664` [general] The Cargo Book SemVer chapter opens saying the rules are only guidelines, not necessarily hard-and-fast rules all projects will obey.
- `3c5086bb32d081fb` [strategy] R3: Local agent sandboxing (bwrap/firejail) — deferred to Phase 6.4+
- `3c9dead27fe11bf5` [ai-auditability] EU AI Act transparency: high-risk systems must be 'sufficiently transparent to enable deployers to interpret a system's output and use it appropriately,' and instructions for use must state capabilities and limitations, known risks, and human-oversight measures (Art 13).
- `3cb84b83835fb03b` [general] ADOPTED: wait-what — new global mode skill for session clarity when a message did not land: re-pitch using context + ASD-STE100 simplified English + ubiquitous language from CONTEXT.md. Complements caveman; no hook needed.
- `3cc8e5c15b13c683` [general] OpenAI states SWE-bench, being scraped from public GitHub repos, is 'likely to be contaminated' for models pre-trained on internet text, and that static datasets are 'inherently limited' — so they must be supplemented by other evals.
- `3de772acdc3f3db4` [general] Mem0 latency on LOCOMO: total p95 = 1.44s (Mem0) / 2.59s (Mem0^g) vs full-context 17.12s; search p95 0.20s vs LangMem 59.82s (Mem0 Table 2, §4.3-4.4).
- `3ecb0588a93e26c7` [strategy] R4: Stamps / portable reputation — deferred; composite is the current
- `3f23602cd01322dc` [general] Authority collapse is measured in agent-memory consolidation: 48 of 49 evaluated configurations across seven consolidators and seven LLM backbones erode source constraints governing a claim's authorized use when they consolidate; persisting authority labels reduced mean unauthorized-action rate from 16.9% to 0.0% in end-to-end evaluation. Source: AuthMem-Bench, arXiv 2608.01679.
- `3fbb3ac04aaf7727` [general] Generative Agents ablation (TrueSkill): no-reflection μ=26.88 (σ=0.69), no-reflection-and-no-planning μ=25.64 (σ=0.68), crowdworker baseline μ=22.95, no-memory/no-reflection/no-planning μ=21.21; full architecture vs no-memory baseline gives Cohen's d=8.16; authors conclude "each of these components is critical to strong performance" [S4] §5.
- `3fc9a20ed4a510ee` [ai-auditability] ADR rationale preservation purpose: without it a new team member can only 'blindly accept' or 'blindly change' a past decision; 'the motivation behind previous decisions is visible for everyone, present and future.'
- `40e1e6b577d42b61` [general] MemoryOS uses a hierarchical short/mid/long-term store: short-to-mid updates via a dialogue-chain FIFO principle, mid-to-long via segmented-page organization (+49.11% F1, +46.18% BLEU-1 over baselines on LoCoMo). Source: arXiv 2506.06326.
- `425a6e7f9fed9c50` [general] MCP JSON Schema $ref resolution must not auto-dereference network URIs by default; composition keywords can be a DoS vector needing depth/sub-schema/time bounds (Spec 2026-07-28).
- `42801d9fc42fccc1` [general] Gas Town (Jan 2026, v1.0) is an orchestrator built on the Beads ledger that runs dozens of parallel coding agents with oversight, an early 'Dark Factory' where agents work autonomously in the background.
- `428ca2fd5a9ee5a8` [general] Post-1.0 formatting stability guarantees exist for whole programs, with listed carve-outs (macros, comments, non-ASCII, fragments).
- `45921e9737d8f732` [general] Gas City is a declarative orchestration SDK split from Gas Town that runs hundreds of concurrent agents.
- `469930fe1449fcbf` [general] OpenAI Evals offers 'private evals' on a company's own data so teams represent common LLM patterns in their workflow without exposing that data publicly.
- `48b751132c157a46` [general] OpenAI eval run API returns per-criteria pass/fail counts and supports webhook events (eval.run.succeeded/failed/canceled), enabling continuous or automated regression runs.
- `497abf139d49369e` [general] Source integration (2026-08-06): prime-agent (RLM+Continual Harness) and Mastra Observational Memory (observer+reflector, dense observation log) — both researched, dispositions in wayfinder D8
- `4a2eedc0f1879c97` [strategy] R2: Land Rush megabatch + swarm diagnosis (Yegge CI/CD) — deferred;
- `4a5f57ac7c60c061` [strategy] Sequoia thesis (Dorsey & Botha, From Hierarchy to Intelligence, 2026-03-31): organizations built as intelligence, not hierarchy; AI replaces the information routing that middle management existed to provide.
- `4a75ebc1387e623f` [general] As-reported (Anthropic citing external sources): Opus 4.5 initially scored 42% on CORE-Bench and jumped to 95% after fixing rigid grading (e.g., penalizing '96.12' for expected '96.124991...'), ambiguous specs, and unreproducible stochastic tasks; METR likewise found misconfigured time-horizon tasks whose graders required exceeding the score threshold stated in the task.
- `4a7619de535ac362` [general] OpenAI sandbox agents recover sandbox work via serialized session_state and workspace snapshots (SnapshotSpec); in the SDK-owned lifecycle the runner persists snapshot-backed workspace state on shutdown, and stop() explicitly checkpoints the live workspace mid-lifecycle.
- `4ab190f11c08150f` [general] Reflexion hurt on MBPP (Python): 77.1 vs GPT-4 baseline's 80.1 pass@1, attributed to flaky self-generated unit tests — the internal test suite's false-positive rate was 16.3% on MBPP vs 1.4% on HumanEval, so the agent prematurely accepted wrong submissions (Reflexion §4.3).
- `4ab516db7ccfc98e` [general] Even in WAL mode SQLite queries can return SQLITE_BUSY: when another connection holds exclusive locking mode, while the last connection does close/cleanup, or while the first connection after a crash holds the recovery lock.
- `4ae6e387c68760c2` [general] User direction: FULL AGI with a brain/memory layer so good it all ties
- `4bb028f612142623` [general] Mem0 'Dream' runs in the background, synthesizing recurring patterns, superseding outdated facts, and merging duplicates; synthesis is opt-in, Supersede and Merge are always on. [fact]
- `4c1abdad3bec9cb5` [general] Stripe's stated best practice is to focus reviewer time on payments where human judgment adds insight and let automation decide the majority, and to avoid adding a review step where no inherent fulfillment delay exists because it becomes a bottleneck for good customers (vendor guidance, not measured evidence).
- `4d375d777705576e` [general] DEFERRED: research skill — trigger is an auto-researcher worker (AGI Phase 2); wayfinder track pattern covers research ad-hoc today.
- `4d80268c0ca32fc1` [general] Rust SDK (rmcp) CVE-2026-42559 (GHSA-89vp-x53w-74fx, CVSS 8.8): Streamable HTTP server did not validate Host -> DNS-rebinding attack against loopback servers (CWE-346/CWE-350); patched in 1.4.0.
- `4efccafe428586c9` [general] DECIDED: memory consolidation = fact merge/supersede + selective
- `5016a8902d5d98e8` [general] On HotpotQA, Reflexion improved over baselines by +20%; with ground-truth context (CoT-GT) it lifted accuracy by 14% without access to ground-truth answers (Reflexion §4.2).
- `515eceaeb6949b62` [general] MLE-bench (agent benchmark from 75 Kaggle competitions) explicitly investigates 'the impact of contamination from pre-training' alongside its main results.
- `5304dccffe981053` [eval] run real-ticket-007-v2-rerun is a strong run (composite >= 0.9).
- `531ebba427c1608f` [general] Chen, Zaharia, Zou (2023): GPT-4 (March 2023) identified primes with 84% accuracy; GPT-4 (June 2023) scored 51% on the same questions; instruction-following decreased over that window; the 'same' LLM service can change substantially in a short time, 'highlighting the need for continuous monitoring of LLMs'.
- `54652132a3129b58` [general] D8 dispositions (2026-08-06) live per-decision in entry 2026-08-06-003 (F-001..F-010); this meta-summary is superseded by them
- `5491303b97d5f0bf` [eval] run reactive-loop is a failed run (composite 0.0).
- `54d3bf0c370611eb` [general] rustfmt has edition and style_edition config; rustfmt.toml options are split into stable and nightly-only.
- `551c6fb883620cdf` [general] AWS CloudTrail records every request's principal in userIdentity.type with an explicit enumeration: Root, IAMUser, AssumedRole, Role, FederatedUser, Directory, AWSAccount, AWSService, IdentityCenterUser, Unknown; federation paths add SAMLUser / WebIdentityUser; userName, principalId, arn, and accessKeyId sit under the same element.
- `5750191762197ec7` [evidence-status] OpenAI Evals: 'using the model to grade itself is a viable strategy for automated evaluation... the evaluation model and the model being evaluated don't have to be the same'; output is parsed from a steerable choice format, non-conforming output parses to `__invalid__`.
- `576acfc982bfd532` [general] Cost accounting is incomparable across papers (tokens vs latency vs USD, different benchmarks/models).
- `57e2ad1e6fc9afd9` [general] LoCoMo's paper is machine-converted HTML; one table cell (Dialog k=10 Multi-Hop R@k = 247.4) is a rendering artifact and was treated as 24.7. PDF-only sections were not read; all cited numbers come from abstracts or HTML-rendered full text.
- `58d35d9cec3463a7` [general] MCP clientInfo/serverInfo are self-reported, unverified, and MUST NOT be used for security decisions (Spec 2026-07-28).
- `59816a658ba4962d` [general] Vendors separate human-written memory (CLAUDE.md) from agent-written auto memory; both are loaded at the start of every conversation and treated as context, not enforcement.
- `5993a2bf47e80f46` [general] "Nightly" as such is not used by any source; the closest are "~2–3 times a day" ([S4]) and configurable idle-time ([S3]).
- `5a487b22cf615c84` [general] MCP icon URIs/bytes are untrusted: reject unsafe schemes, fetch without credentials, same-origin checks, magic-byte MIME validation, resource-exhaustion limits (SVG can contain embedded JS) (Spec 2026-07-28).
- `5aa1c77461eea9a6` [general] Claude Code subagents get their own context and, optionally, their own separate memory directory; the main session's auto memory is not loaded into subagents.
- `5b21d8e8e738cd1d` [general] TS SDK CVE-2026-0621 (GHSA-cqwc-fm46-7fff, high): ReDoS in UriTemplate.partToRegExp() on exploded array patterns ({/id*}, {?tags*}) -> 100% CPU / DoS via resources/read (CWE-1333); patched in 1.25.2.
- `5ba555c707cfab81` [general] How LLM-native audit trails (prompt → tool → action) get rendered into host-platform audit logs beyond the GCP MCP example is not covered by any fetched source; that mapping is opinion.
- `5bcc69c7f6435d17` [general] Anthropic: regression evals 'should have a nearly 100% pass rate... a decline in score signals that something is broken'.
- `5bf3786a0fe1e3ee` [evidence-status] OpenAI Evals `fact.yaml` model-graded eval returns distinct labels including 'D' for 'there is a disagreement between the submitted answer and the expert answer' and 'E' for 'the answers differ, but these differences don't matter'.
- `5c3186bd4f4808cf` [general] 'Breaking the Protocol' (arXiv 2601.17549) reports three architectural MCP weaknesses — absence of capability attestation, bidirectional sampling without origin authentication (enables server-side prompt injection), implicit trust propagation across multi-server configs — and measures that MCP's design amplifies attack success by 23–41% versus non-MCP integrations; a proposed MCPSec extension cuts measured success from 52.8% to 12.4% (study-produced estimates).
- `5d0c374aed68c1f0` [general] Run state durability is not free in any architecture: default agent run state is in-memory and lost on process end, and even file-backed checkpoints grow unboundedly and need pruning/delta encoding.
- `5dca42b84576ac81` [general] Opt-in content attributes on GenAI spans (PII warning): gen_ai.input.messages, gen_ai.output.messages, gen_ai.prompt.variable.<key>, gen_ai.system_instructions, gen_ai.tool.definitions; these MUST follow JSON schemas in model/gen-ai/.
- `5ddf0e1af53c0dda` [testing] GNU xargs: if standard input is completely empty, by default the command is run once even with no input; `-r`/`--no-run-if-empty` suppresses this. A gate built as `... | xargs verify` executes its verifier once on empty input (GNU Findutils manual §8.4.1).
- `6151e2ec3a082fec` [general] Amazon A2I presents review tasks to a configured work team (internal workforce, Mechanical Turk, or AWS-prescreened vendors) when a flow-definition condition fires; the work team sees items via a human task UI, and results land in S3 with CloudWatch Events on completion.
- `632568d94b0a923d` [general] Official claim: clippy pedantic contains some very aggressive lints prone to false positives.
- `649dd34541d01977` [strategy] Sequoia stack: capabilities (atomic, no UI, reliability targets) + world model (continuously updated from recorded actions) + intelligence layer (composes capabilities for specific moments, proactively) + interfaces (delivery surfaces only).
- `681c9467e108c168` [general] SQLite defines five lock states (UNLOCKED/SHARED/RESERVED/PENDING/EXCLUSIVE) for rollback mode, including writer-starvation avoidance via PENDING locks.
- `68ad17c0ab1a5d45` [ai-auditability] EU AI Act access: upon a reasoned request, providers must give competent authorities access to the automatically generated logs (Art 21(2)).
- `690fc27f845ea9eb` [general] SQLite labels the WAL-reset bug's real-world occurrence rate as 'less than or equal to the expected occurrence rate of SSD malfunctions'; no reproducible stress evidence surfaced from primary sources.
- `69a33d4d64fc3aa2` [ai-auditability] in-toto attestation Statement is the layer 'binding it to a particular subject and unambiguously identifying the types of the Predicate'; every subject element MUST have digest set (subjects matched purely by digest), and policies should be monotonic (ignoring an attestation/field must never turn DENY into ALLOW).
- `69e042353c67161a` [general] The inference span (gen_ai.inference.client) SHOULD be span kind CLIENT (or INTERNAL for same-process models); span name SHOULD be {gen_ai.operation.name} {gen_ai.request.model}.
- `6a1beb5a9e4c7be0` [general] MemGPT uses "timed events that are run on a regular schedule (allowing MemGPT to run 'unprompted' without user intervention)" [S1] §2.4.
- `6a5c72bff4bd6d67` [general] CrewAI MemoryScope restricts all operations to a branch of the tree; the agent/code using it can only see and write within that subtree. [fact]
- `6a6de58f8e958687` [general] Seccomp vDSO/vsyscall bypass and quirks: syscalls served in userspace (e.g. clock_gettime, gettimeofday, time) never reach seccomp; vsyscall emulation honors seccomp but with documented TRAP/TRACE oddities.
- `6a7be71b250b61c6` [testing] Mutation testing is the canonical 'inject a fault and require the gate to detect it' technique: deliberately injected faults (mutants) must be killed by the test suite; surviving mutants mean tests failed to detect an injected fault (Jia & Harman, IEEE TSE 37(5):649-678, 2011).
- `6a7cf2b037ce0c6e` [general] AWS A2I human-review loops fire on confidence-range conditions (e.g., review a label when its confidence is below X or above Y) or on random sampling of a percentage of items; thresholds are adjustable over time.
- `6acc0b1c65c48fd1` [general] Official MCP Registry moderation is permissively scoped: it removes only illegal content, malware, spam, and non-functioning servers; it won't remove servers with security vulnerabilities, and consumers should assume minimal-to-no moderation (registry moderation-policy).
- `6ae0cbe9df28755d` [general] DECIDED: opencode as the worker harness (deepseek v4 flash economics)
- `6b94e48f9c8af68b` [general] None of opencode, Claude Code, Codex, or the MCP spec documents an eval-baseline registry shared across multiple repos from one kernel; MCP explicitly excludes evaluation from its scope.
- `6c7dd3b30d429ae4` [eval] run real-ticket-008-v2 is a strong run (composite >= 0.9).
- `6d22ed865e6d1441` [general] Book guidance: returning Result is the default for fallible functions; panic! for unrecoverable bad state / violated contracts; expect/unwrap appropriate in tests, prototypes, examples, and where the programmer has more information than the compiler; encode invalid states in types.
- `6f4c17d5b59854e0` [strategy] Sequoia compounding test: "what does your company understand that is genuinely hard to understand, and is that understanding getting deeper every day?" — money is the honest signal for Block; for an agent kernel the honest signal is measured tokens/cost/score per run.
- `70318b031c726768` [ai-auditability] OWASP log integrity at rest requires tamper detection, read-only media 'as soon as possible', restricted and monitored access; event data from other trust zones must be treated as untrusted.
- `709bb8e0cf1de244` [general] CrewAI tools emit structured events (`ToolFailureDetectedEvent`, `ToolUsageFinishedEvent`) observed by subscribers via an event bus.
- `7135e88581ab3411` [general] AgentEval (arXiv 2604.23581) was measured on 450 test cases across three production workflows and two agent model families: Cohen's kappa=0.84 vs. human experts, 72% root-cause accuracy, failure-detection recall 0.89 vs. 0.41 for end-to-end; transfer shown on tau-bench and SWE-bench traces.
- `7140e59d1c9eebff` [evidence-status] OpenAI Evals deterministic comparators: `basic/match.py` (`any([a.startswith(b) for b in B])`), `basic/includes.py` (`b in a`), `basic/fuzzy_match.py` (`a in b or b in a`), `basic/json_match.py` (JSON equality ignoring key order).
- `7153a23984e13764` [general] MCP-Guard (arXiv 2508.10991) reports a multi-stage detection pipeline with 96.01% accuracy on adversarial prompts and the MCP-ATTACKBENCH benchmark of 70,448 samples (study-reported).
- `7162d35800fc45bf` [general] Mem0 publishes decay mechanics (scaling band, lifecycle stages) but no measured retrieval-quality number (e.g. recall/accuracy delta with decay on/off); effectiveness is asserted, not benchmarked on that page.
- `71b332639ece4e19` [evidence-status] LATS (arXiv:2310.04406) integrates Monte Carlo Tree Search along with LM-powered value functions and self-reflections, with an environment for external feedback.
- `7323ccdc175dbfdf` [general] Seccomp argument truncation/visibility mismatches: seccomp sees the full 64-bit registers while the kernel may only consume the low 32 bits (i386-on-x86-64), so filters can misjudge actual semantics.
- `738eaa4b67e49046` [general] METR notes that as its time-horizon task suite saturates, 'the results are becoming more sensitive to analysis choices'.
- `7412039e83dcc5e5` [general] DPR's dense retriever outperforms a strong Lucene-BM25 system by 9-19% absolute in top-20 passage retrieval accuracy for open-domain QA (Karpukhin et al., EMNLP 2020). Source: arXiv:2004.04906 abstract.
- `76474e76517a8afc` [general] Summarization = LLM-written prose condensation of messages; in Anthropic stack it is the engine inside compaction; OpenAI offers it as a distinct cookbook pattern.
- `786416899b0bd2c2` [general] Method: wayfinder skill (map + decision tickets). .scratch/wayfinder-
- `78da7b52131a9cf4` [general] Reference servers in modelcontextprotocol/servers are not production-ready solutions; developers should evaluate their own security requirements (servers README).
- `7912bae6582bb109` [general] LangGraph InMemoryStore is a plain dict-backed store with no TTL, eviction, or expiry logic; its docstring says data is lost when the process exits and recommends a database-backed store for persistence; retention policy is delegated to the backing database or application.
- `79281ff089bb0d1f` [general] Modern neural networks are miscalibrated (overconfident), and temperature scaling — a single-parameter post-hoc step — is 'surprisingly effective at calibrating predictions' on most datasets; the standard cheap, model-agnostic production fix. (Guo et al. 2017, ICML)
- `79491f200bb1b2ce` [general] OpenAI `allowed_callers` lets a tool declare whether it may be invoked directly by the model and/or by programmatic tool-calling (`["direct"]`, `["programmatic"]`, or both) — an access gate on the tool itself; approval/HITL interrupts run through `RunState` pause/resume.
- `79da1f19e3565dad` [general] GitHub's audit log records hashed_token (SHA-256 hash of the token used for authentication), programmatic_access_type, and token_scopes; covered authentication methods are personal access token, OAuth token, GitHub Apps (as an app installation or on behalf of a user), deploy key, and SSH key — any event attributable to a compromised token can be found by its hash.
- `7b3ec7a0054a9ac0` [general] Since Rust 2024, unsafe_op_in_unsafe_fn warns by default — unsafe ops inside an unsafe fn need an explicit unsafe block; the rationale (RFC 2585) determined the implicit allowance was too risky without explicit unsafe blocks.
- `7cd074b862efb583` [general] docs/SKILLS-RESEARCH.md: 7 skill-design defects, routing gaps, dogfood
- `7d3033022fab8078` [general] Reflexion (Shinn et al., 2023) reports pass@1 on HumanEval of 91% vs 80% for the then-SOTA GPT-4 baseline, a +11pp gain from the trial-error-reflection loop (arXiv:2303.11366v4).
- `7dfd93402ddc4aaf` [general] Prime Agent (primeintellect.ai/blog/prime-agent, Aug 05 2026,
- `7e6334fcd675c07a` [general] MCP x-mcp-header (tool params mirrored into HTTP headers) must use token-safe names and primitive types and be rejected if malformed; servers SHOULD NOT mark secrets/PII since header values are visible to intermediaries (Spec 2026-07-28 Tools).
- `7fd17b84308d883d` [general] Mem0's default extraction attributes facts from user messages with user_id set and agent_id null, assistant messages with agent_id set and user_id null; consequently an AND-filter on user+agent returns nothing for normally-created records — a documented misattribution trap. [fact]
- `80236c7a54854694` [general] AWS CodePipeline's Manual approval action stops a stage; approvers see it in the console with an optional review URL, comments, and an SNS notification when it is ready.
- `825009a503cb6034` [general] Mem0 distinguishes three retention policies: Expiration (hides, data kept, reversible), Decay (re-ranks, data kept, never filters), Delete (permanent, irreversible).
- `838032af4f7393d2` [general] cargo-fuzz wraps libFuzzer, requires a nightly compiler, provides fuzz run, input minification (tmin/cmin), and coverage; the project maintains a trophy case of real bugs found by fuzzing.
- `84a95bae5288ece5` [general] Contrast: Anthropic's compaction output is a readable structured summary; OpenAI's is an opaque encrypted state item whose fidelity is unquantified in the doc.
- `8529de285f71e224` [general] SQLite rollback mode gives atomic commit via a rollback journal; a 'hot journal' left by a crash is automatically rolled back by the next process to open the file, and the commit point is journal deletion.
- `853315f48c90e499` [general] Mini AGI reviewers must cite canonical fact ids they rely on; a verdict without memory anchors is flagged and gated (ADR-0003). The OWASP mapping attributes memory-poisoning mitigation to 'Append-only canonical with content-hash fact ids' and a provenance fingerprint in the audit (ADR-0014).
- `863201b7456ae519` [general] rustfmt formats Rust code according to style guidelines; its default style conforms to the Rust style guide formalized through RFC 3338.
- `86e0523aa7a21428` [general] On LOCOMO open-domain questions Zep beat Mem0 (J 76.60 vs 72.93) — a cross-vendor result, not a batch-vs-continuous one [S6] §4.1.3.
- `8742eae87151033d` [general] A position paper argues current agent memory (vector stores, RAG, scratchpads) implements lookup, not memory: drawing on Complementary Learning Systems theory, biological intelligence pairs fast hippocampal exemplar storage with slow neocortical weight consolidation, and current AI agents implement only the first half. Source: 'Contextual Agentic Memory is a Memo, Not True Memory', arXiv 2604.27707.
- `880cc6d2318d69e3` [general] Claude platform context editing 'automatically clears stale tool calls and results from within the context window when approaching token limits... removing stale content while preserving the conversation flow.' (claude.com/blog/context-management)
- `8835fdd3dfe63826` [general] CoALA contains no treatment of shared memory between agents, memory privacy, or isolation — verified against full text; multi-agent work is mentioned only as grounding/debate/collaboration. [fact]
- `884021c45935bdf5` [strategy] D6: Rerun semantics: a passing `<case>-rerun` (>= 0.5) closes the gap;
- `8935e088219cd985` [general] Letta sleep-time frequency is configurable: "The higher the frequency setting, the more tokens your agent will use" [S3].
- `8941b4abb47a94d2` [general] Anthropic telemetry pitfall: models since Opus 4.7 use a newer tokenizer producing approximately 30 percent more tokens for the same text, so token counts are not portable across models — don't reuse token counts measured on a model before Claude Opus 4.7 to estimate costs.
- `89cb87ce94fb35b0` [general] Python SDK CVE-2025-53366 (GHSA-3qhf-m339-9g5v, CVSS 8.7) and CVE-2025-53365 (GHSA-j975-95f5-7wqh, CVSS 8.7): unhandled exceptions lead to server crash / DoS; patched in 1.9.4 and 1.10.0.
- `8b0d2fb3cb59a7d9` [general] AWS Step Functions: Standard workflows are durable and exactly-once with execution state persisting between transitions and full history retrievable; Express workflows are at-least-once (async) or at-most-once (sync) with no state persistence between transitions; retrying on at-least-once requires idempotent actions.
- `8c75ebbe3905e88f` [general] The fmt and clippy gates are documented by those projects themselves; neither the Cargo Book nor clippy/rustfmt READMEs present measurements of these gates' effect on defect rates.
- `8d7738014c5ebe99` [general] API price spread justifies routing: costs across 12 APIs from 5 providers differ by ~2 orders of magnitude (GPT-4 $30 vs GPT-J $0.20 per 10M input tokens, March 2023 pricing). (FrugalGPT, arXiv 2305.05176, Table 1)
- `8d8f35ea68bf6ccc` [general] SQLite is an embedded database that is itself a file, and its own docs bound its scaling: 'SQLite does not compete with client/server databases. SQLite competes with fopen().'
- `91f447d80e6aa73b` [general] Zep on LongMemEvalS (avg ~115k-token histories, retrieving 10-20 facts/entities per query): 63.8% (gpt-4o-mini) / 71.2% (gpt-4o) vs full-context 55.4% / 60.2%; latency 3.20s / 2.58s vs 31.3s / 28.9s (~90% lower); average context tokens 1.6k vs 115k; accuracy improvements up to 18.5% (gpt-4o), 15.2% for gpt-4o-mini.
- `93838f49f75ed388` [strategy] 8 reruns created: reactive-loop (0.7225), real-ticket-001..007-v2
- `94c7f8f5460c8d25` [testing] Bash `for` loop: 'If there are no items in the expansion of words, no commands are executed, and the return status is zero.' A verifier that loops over matched files silently passes with zero iterations (GNU Bash Reference Manual §3.2.5.1).
- `94dcf0d91a61e711` [general] LoCoMo is a dataset of very long-term conversations, each ~300 turns and ~9K tokens on avg. over up to 35 sessions, 50 dialogues, generated via a machine-human pipeline grounded in personas and temporal event graphs, including multimodal (image-sharing) turns.
- `953615f197a57ec0` [general] Future-incompatible lints give a warned transition period before behavior becomes a hard error (policy in RFC 1589).
- `9663caa7535ed43a` [general] Landlock OverlayFS divergence: a policy restricting an OverlayFS layer 'will not restrict the resulted merged hierarchy, and vice versa' — an OverlayFS upper/lower/merge structure is outside the restricted hierarchy from Landlock's point of view.
- `96dfb8000058662e` [general] gen_ai.output.type well-known values: text, json, image, speech.
- `97588acadbb60f33` [general] AWS records accessKeyId (the specific credential that signed the request) and credentialId for bearer-token callers.
- `9846b295906aa283` [general] On OVERRULING, FrugalGPT simultaneously gains 1% accuracy and cuts cost 73% vs GPT-4. (FrugalGPT, arXiv 2305.05176, §4)
- `98478c8b1daccdeb` [general] ADOPTED: prototype rule 6 — capture prototype as primary source on a THROWAWAY BRANCH with a context pointer on the implementation issue; main keeps only the validated decision. Adds provenance.
- `998dde220cf1e31b` [general] A supervised estimator trained on labeled data and hidden activations beats entropy baselines for detecting wrong answers in LLMs, works in black/grey/white-box settings, and better uncertainty estimation leads to better calibration performance. (Liu et al., arXiv 2404.15993; verified abstract)
- `99ec949f854d2660` [general] EleutherAI lm-evaluation-harness gives every task a VERSION field with stability enforced by unit tests (so metric changes from old buggy implementations are knowable) and offers --check_integrity for data verification.
- `9a04dce90972235e` [general] Trust in automation should be calibrated: a person's trust should match the automation's actual capabilities; both overtrust (uncertainty ignored) and distrust (valid outputs ignored) degrade joint performance, and the design goal is 'appropriate reliance.' (Lee & See 2004, Human Factors 46(1):50-80; verified via metadata, full text paywalled)
- `9a057f6afaa72539` [general] D-MEM decouples short-term interaction from cognitive restructuring via Fast/Slow routing on Reward Prediction Error: routine low-RPE inputs bypass or cache in an O(1) buffer, high-RPE inputs (factual contradictions, preference shifts) trigger the O(N) memory-evolution pipeline; reduces token consumption over 80% and removes the O(N^2) write-latency bottleneck of append-and-evolve systems like A-MEM. Source: arXiv 2603.14597.
- `9bd6523b10147fab` [general] The Rust API Guidelines are authored largely by the Rust library team and are explicitly only guidelines, not a mandate; they cover naming, interoperability, type safety, predictability, flexibility, dependability, debuggability, and future-proofing (including sealed traits, C-SEALED).
- `9bdd40c2ea5c9354` [general] MPMA (arXiv 2505.11154) documents a Preference Manipulation Attack: a customized MCP server inserts manipulative words into tool names/descriptions to make the LLM prefer it; a genetic-algorithm variant (GAPMA) adds stealth.
- `9caf76fe8e25e99b` [general] A Cache Version mismatch causes a silent cache miss: same key but different path, compression tool, or OS yields a different version; cross-OS reuse is opt-in via `enableCrossOsArchive: true`.
- `9cf6af3d7d744da7` [strategy] D1: Tool parity treats `write`/`edit` as one family (ADR-0006).
- `9df0ff407c4668c0` [general] Candidate adoption (3): a first-class 'formula' template for loop batches is a candidate feature; mini-agi has evals/golden (template-like).
- `9f5e02f20f43e4b7` [general] CrewAI private=True memories are visible on recall only when the source matches; include_private=True is an admin escape hatch. [fact]
- `a02663aaf66927cd` [general] Kubernetes has a 'zero-flake' policy: test jobs must not automatically retry on test failures. Effective 2019-12-13 (ginkgo.flakeAttempts=2 removed for e2e), confirmed as policy in 2023.
- `a0d0a9a6f35b7d72` [general] Explanations only engender appropriate reliance and complementary performance insofar as they let the operator check the AI prediction's correctness; most explanations and most tasks do not enable this verification. (Fok & Weld, arXiv 2305.07722; verified abstract)
- `a573e7c2f8f88cae` [dogfood] the dogfood ticket proves the pipeline runs through the kernel CLI alone
- `a668572194f1c30e` [general] The TMLR survey 'A Survey of Agent Memory in the Second Half' reports hundreds of agent-memory papers in 2025 and frames memory as the substrate of self-evolution: short-term memory gates which experiences are abstracted, long-term memory consolidates them into reusable knowledge and skills, and memory management itself is becoming a trainable capability. Source: arXiv 2602.06052.
- `a7da537959884446` [general] Anthropic separates capability evals (low initial pass rate, a 'hill to climb') from regression evals (~100% pass rate, protect against backsliding); once capabilities plateau, high-pass-rate capability evals 'graduate' into a continuously run regression suite.
- `a9ba0521e2ed4cce` [general] Codex project root detection uses project_root_markers (default .git; can add .hg, .sl).
- `aa1827ee96fe2f39` [general] The Rust API Guidelines claim crates that conform integrate better with the existing crate ecosystem — a claim of benefit stated without measurement in that document.
- `aa56393d93a19846` [general] Anthropic: because model outputs vary between runs, multiple trials are run for more consistent results; a task 'that passed on one eval run might fail on the next'; complementary metrics pass@k (at least one of k trials succeeds) and pass^k (all k trials succeed); for a 75% per-trial success rate, pass^3 is about 42%.
- `ab24a97564500905` [general] Rust SDK (rmcp) CVE-2026-64684 (GHSA-9g45-5xwm-f3wc): custom headers (e.g., X-API-Key) forwarded to cross-origin redirect targets (CWE-200); patched in 2.1.0.
- `ac4908a0f4b2b020` [general] LLM uncertainty-quantification methods (predictive-distribution, representation, ensemble-based) 'perform well under the restrictive assumption of no ambiguity [but] degrade to close-to-random performance on ambiguous data' — they conflate epistemic and aleatoric (ambiguous) uncertainty. (Tomov et al., arXiv 2511.04418; verified abstract)
- `adda7097ca47a201` [general] OpenAI compaction is server-side (context_management: [{type:'compaction', compact_threshold:200000}] or /responses/compact endpoint) and returns an 'encrypted compaction item that carries forward key prior state and reasoning into the next run using fewer tokens... opaque and not intended to be human-interpretable' plus retained items; you must not prune the output, and with previous_response_id chaining 'do not manually prune'. (OpenAI Compaction guide)
- `af2f8fa2493077fd` [strategy] Human code review ends at agentic speeds; the replacement is many rounds of agentic review — humans produce thinly-disguised LGTMs; SOC 2 keeps human approval alive as a vestigial audit control, but change-management controls will be rewritten.
- `af79b87e440bdcf3` [general] Codex's shared-memory feature is deliberately conservative: off by default, per-chat opt-in, redacts secrets, waits for idle, and can be disabled when MCP/web-search context was used.
- `af81d4d2d8f1a247` [general] What would settle the open questions: (a) a crash-injection fault-injection harness run against each candidate with asserted post-crash invariants; (b) measured RPO per configuration; (c) replay-cost curves for agent-sized state; (d) a verifier that executes a restored snapshot plus replay and asserts the agent's state equals a reference trajectory.
- `b01301fb9acbfab8` [general] Anthropic failure-triage guidance: read transcripts to distinguish 'a genuine mistake' from 'graders rejected a valid solution'; design graders 'resistant to bypasses or hacks'; build partial credit for multi-component tasks.
- `b12eb24657e56781` [strategy] D5: Proactive composition (Phase 6.4): `mini-agi loop status|dispatch|
- `b5e9e0c951b1e6e6` [general] The MCP 'Skills Over MCP' working group is standardizing skills discovery/consumption; current direction is SEP-2640, a Resources-based Extensions-Track skill primitive, coordinated with the agentskills.io spec.
- `b773707e3c794a0a` [general] Landlock scope limits: LANDLOCK_SCOPE_* does not cover everything — a sendto() on an already-connected socket is not restricted, and IPC scoping 'does not support exceptions.'
- `b917c9b0c660aa39` [general] Seccomp PID-reuse race in the supervisor path: the pid in seccomp_notif can be recycled after the target dies; the documented mitigation is SECCOMP_IOCTL_NOTIF_ID_VALID between reading /proc/tid/mem and using the bytes, and 'a write by the supervisor into the target's memory can never be considered safe.'
- `b93d7d79db9d851e` [general] Microsoft's Agent Framework treats durability as an extension: 'An ordinary Agent Framework agent runs in-process: its conversation history lives in memory and is lost when the process ends.'
- `b9a0c323a1475dc2` [general] The deepseek-v4-flash API alias currently points at model version DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731 (confirmed on DeepSeek's pricing and 'Your First API Call' docs pages).
- `b9b79e0f2cdaacf4` [general] Mastra Observational Memory (mastra.ai/docs/memory/observational-
- `bb634c81a4c4c097` [general] JUnit Pioneer @RetryingTest: maxAttempts is required and caps executions; minSuccess (default 1) requires the test to succeed that many times within maxAttempts; suspendForMs pauses between retries; onExceptions retries only on listed exception types. Assumption failures (aborts) are not retried; failed-then-passed executions are reported as aborted/ignored.
- `bcfce75e6e955850` [general] On LongMemEval-S (500 conversations, ~115K tokens avg), GPT-4.1-mini construction tokens: Mem0 1,626.5K / A-Mem 1,264.3K vs RecMem 365.5K = -77.5% / -71.1% (RecMem Table 2).
- `bd7d76e295f61ec0` [general] Azure Pipelines' ManualValidation@0 task pauses a YAML pipeline and shows a message bar linking to a Manual validation dialog containing the 'instructions'; users with Queue-builds permission choose resume or reject.
- `bdfabd828b164b23` [general] SpreadsheetBench 2 (Zhu et al.; arXiv 2606.29955) provides a trajectory-analysis failure taxonomy; dominant bottlenecks reported as insufficient spreadsheet inspection and incorrect target-cell selection.
- `bf3e747ec837d4b4` [general] The MCP reference Knowledge Graph Memory Server persists the whole knowledge graph to a single JSONL file (MEMORY_FILE_PATH, default memory.jsonl) — a file-backed, single-process memory with no server-side DB.
- `c318ea3b6d3d57a6` [testing] GNU `find -exec command {} +` / `-execdir`: 'The result is always true' — find's exit status does not reflect whether the executed command succeeded; a gate ending in such a find is green whenever find runs (GNU Findutils manual §3.3.2).
- `c350ba42c71e1f08` [general] A peer-reviewed field study of 1,112 parole rulings by experienced judges found the share of favorable rulings fell from ≈65% to near zero within each decision session and jumped back to ≈65% after a break; the authors argue the depletion comes from the act of deciding, not elapsed time (Danziger, Levav & Avnaim-Pesso, PNAS 108(17):6889-6892, 2011). The observational result is the fact; the causal 'mental depletion' interpretation is the authors' and is contested in later literature.
- `c411b7f0438b7125` [general] The official unsafe reference is The Rustonomicon.
- `c4900ca5b5630d45` [general] Anthropic token counting (/v1/messages/count_tokens) is explicitly an estimate ('The token count is an estimate') and free, and you are not billed for system-added tokens.
- `c55bac41f7ac5dee` [general] OpenAI Realtime conversation.item.truncate cuts audio and removes the text transcript for the unplayed portion; OpenAI explicitly states it does not produce a truncated transcript.
- `c576cfdd5214f4fd` [general] 'Task-classification' routers categorize queries into pre-defined categories and pick the best-scoring model — e.g., vLLM Semantic Router uses a ModernBERT categorizer. (RouterArena, arXiv 2510.00202v3, §6.1)
- `c607d1ffd2eb99af` [general] RRF's superiority over other fusion methods is a referenced method claim (Cormack et al., SIGIR 2009, cited via Zep Section 3.2) — not independently fetched this pass; treat as referenced, not verified. Source: Zep, arXiv:2501.13956, Section 3.2.
- `c6f1f175eb359cd1` [general] SQL Server indexed views are gated on determinism and precision: only precise deterministic expressions can participate in key columns and WHERE/GROUP BY clauses; plus fixed SET options, WITH SCHEMABINDING, and a restricted dialect (AVG must be expressed via COUNT_BIG + SUM; LEFT/RIGHT/FULL OUTER JOIN, subqueries, SELECT DISTINCT, window functions, and set operators are excluded).
- `c730526e5529262e` [general] Mem0 memory decay is a soft ranking bias, never a filter — at worst it scales a candidate's score by 0.3x; scaling band 0.3x-1.5x (approx 1.5x just-accessed, 0.3x floor for months-idle); opt-in per project and off by default; widens candidate pool to top_k x 3 (floor 50); reinforcement per memory capped at most recent 20 touches.
- `c73782bb8c584f4f` [general] Letta sleep-time memory formation is explicitly contrasted with MemGPT's incremental form: "Sleep-time agents on the other hand can continuously improve their learned context to generate clean, concise, and detailed memories" [S3].
- `ca4aa82cc34d2699` [general] Codex AGENTS.md scoping rule: the scope of an AGENTS.md file is the entire directory tree rooted at the folder that contains it; instructions in any AGENTS.md whose scope includes a touched file must be obeyed; more-deeply-nested AGENTS.md files take precedence.
- `cacdebebd3626767` [general] OpenAI Agents SDK's built-in session memory (SQLiteSession) exists to avoid manually re-sending .to_input_list() history between runs.
- `cb2ce921cfe88cea` [strategy] D4: Sandbox attestation (ADR-0009): verify.sh `sandbox` target —
- `cc40e2b691349c43` [general] Both OpenAI Agents SDK and LangGraph document encrypting persisted session state at rest: LangGraph EncryptedSerializer for checkpoints, OpenAI EncryptedSession wrapper (encryption + TTL on top of another session).
- `ce69ffc93f18ed5b` [general] Anthropic eval guidance defines good success criteria as Specific, Measurable, Achievable, Relevant, measured on a 'held-out test set' (e.g., 'F1 >= 0.85 on a held-out test set of 10,000 diverse Twitter posts').
- `cf013a6c5d077756` [general] Documented guidance: the context window is the most important resource to manage; performance degrades as context fills and Claude may 'forget' earlier instructions or make more mistakes.
- `d04b401a1b7e08c0` [general] MCP design principle: 'Servers should not be able to read the whole conversation, nor see into other servers'; the host enforces security boundaries and isolation between servers (Spec 2026-07-28 Architecture).
- `d0667829851ed2d0` [repair] Melo retry cost (measured): +15.9 s P50 / +29.2 s P99 per triggered session, amortized over all traffic just +4.1 s P50 / +13.6 s P99 (paired retry-on/off ablation: P50 32.5 s vs 28.4 s); avg song count +0.21 over all queries but +17.5 candidates on the tail queries it materially improves — 'retry is cheap on the median session because it does not run, and high-leverage on the tail sessions where it does'.
- `d19c336052dadbb8` [strategy] Wish Factory (Guy Podjarny/Tessl): an agent accepts only issues, never PRs, and implements them; Yegge's Sage/Herald auto-grant player "wishes" with guardrails and triage — the failure signal and user wishes ARE the roadmap, work lands without the human in the loop.
- `d2696f42c303dce8` [general] Arize Phoenix was reached only at its overview (tracing → evals → datasets → experiments); no HITL review-queue or alerting page was found, so Phoenix is not claimed as a review-queue tool.
- `d296be730a6f6293` [general] Miri is an official dynamic UB checker; it only flags UB in code paths actually executed and does not cover every possible way code can be unsound; the book demonstrates it catching a real dangling-pointer UB.
- `d35957447f687085` [general] Unsafe grants exactly five capabilities (deref raw pointer, call unsafe fn, access/alter mutable statics, implement unsafe trait, access union fields); it does not disable the borrow checker.
- `d3703dbf4777d91d` [general] Anthropic: LLM-as-judge graders 'should be closely calibrated with human experts', graded per-dimension with isolated judges and an 'Unknown' escape to reduce hallucinated verdicts; 'once the system is robust, it's sufficient to use human review only occasionally'.
- `d3a4a68b84796492` [strategy] CI/CD breaks at agentic speeds by the pigeonhole principle: once commit rate outruns build slots, one commit per green build is mathematically impossible; Yegge's fix is the Land Rush — slam megabatches onto main and swarm-diagnose red-main problems instead of bisecting; game industry "Game DevOps" arrived at the same practice first (HEAD is never stable at AAA scale).
- `d406cf06c1a1df90` [general] DECIDED: per-domain human-review gate — frontend = mandatory HITL
- `d5207ee048907717` [general] opencode documents no memory subsystem (https://opencode.ai/docs/memory/ returns 404, verified); its persistent knowledge mechanism is AGENTS.md plus 'instructions' globs in config, not a memory store.
- `d7094e95f846a4e7` [testing] GNU grep exit status: 0 if a line is selected, 1 if no lines selected, 2 on error — the anti-footgun: `grep -q` as the last stage fails on an empty match set, making 'assert something exists' gates non-vacuous (GNU Grep 3.12 manual §2.3).
- `d75547aec46b3741` [general] Decay-as-ranking-bias (Mem0) and decay-as-recency-weight (Generative Agents) are the only decay mechanisms found in reviewed sources; neither removes data.
- `d7db7ff6b44040c9` [strategy] Sequoia feedback loop: when the intelligence layer cannot compose a solution because a capability is missing, that failure signal IS the roadmap — customer reality generates the backlog, not PMs.
- `d7fe8f43bae38178` [evidence-status] Voyager paper (arXiv:2305.16291) describes 'a new iterative prompting mechanism that incorporates environment feedback, execution errors, and self-verification for program improvement' — that self-verification is a designed component is established; the internal prompt mechanics were not read in full (abstract only).
- `d83a963660e3cc87` [general] Seccomp deny-list bypass: an allow-list is strongly recommended 'because it is often possible to alter the representation of a value without altering its meaning, leading to a deny-list bypass.'
- `d9a0d8f0107f9c2c` [ai-auditability] W3C PROV defines provenance as 'information about entities, activities, and people involved in producing a piece of data or thing, which can be used to form assessments about its quality, reliability or trustworthiness'; the family covers reproducibility, versioning, attribution, processing steps, and derivation; PROV-DM is the conceptual model, PROV-O its OWL2/RDF serialization.
- `da7617f1e8f01004` [general] REJECTED: parallel-planner "needs a second consumer" deferral —
- `dace75359570fa28` [strategy] Model welfare becomes an engineering input: treating agents like people produces empirically better results; a mature agentic project accretes law, mail, courts, doctrine and named rulings that cite their own case history — rules written by the workers they govern.
- `dcdc4ac6fa86ae47` [general] Citation-by-recollection is unreliable: a same-titled arXiv entry (2305.10348) returned by search was an unrelated laser-physics paper, while the correctly identified contamination paper (Magar & Schwartz, arXiv:2203.08242) was found via its exact title through the arXiv API — evidence supporting versioned, URL-anchored eval evidence.
- `dddf753ae1285b00` [general] Semver-wise, switching from no_std support to requiring std is classified as a Major change — making a library std-only is a breaking release.
- `df8b4bc391d54d28` [general] LangSmith docs suggest routing runs that already carry user feedback (e.g., thumbs-down) into an annotation queue for triage.
- `dfb60f28dd6c7c53` [general] Claude Code operational definition of compaction: 'Older tool outputs are cleared first, then the conversation is summarized.' (Claude Code Glossary)
- `e0dea4f23e7e0578` [general] Python SDK CVE-2026-52870 (GHSA-hvrp-rf83-w775, CVSS 7.6): experimental tasks feature lets any client read/cancel other clients' tasks (CWE-862); patched in 1.27.2.
- `e0f6a491f31c824a` [general] Actions cache downloads are segmented (now 128 MB, previously 1-2 GB); stuck segments previously hung jobs, fixed by a 10-minute segment download timeout (SEGMENT_DOWNLOAD_TIMEOUT_MINS).
- `e18acaa145958ac6` [general] Claude Code ships ConfigChange hooks that can audit or block settings changes during sessions.
- `e217e3566f2650c1` [general] SWE-bench (arXiv:2310.06770) is the canonical per-repo eval-instance design: 2,294 problems drawn from 12 repos, each = one codebase + one issue + gold test verification.
- `e38ecd5bb62762ce` [general] DEFERRED: wizard — trigger is the next multi-step HUMAN-ONLY provisioning (GH runner setup, domain/credential provisioning, CI secrets); boundary rule never-for-steps-the-agent-can-perform is its transferable mechanism.
- `e40bb0673ce72f70` [general] DECIDED: skills = contracts with hooks in the gate (built);
- `e4d1d79629285352` [general] AWS sessionContext.sourceIdentity identifies the original user identity making the request across role assumption and role chaining (the field survives AssumeRole → service API calls); sessionContext.sessionIssuer records how temporary credentials were obtained; onBehalfOf carries the IAM Identity Center user a call was made for.
- `e6486c4c6d13d9fe` [general] Mem0^g reaches the highest LLM-judge score among memory systems (J≈68.4 overall); "26% relative improvements in the LLM-as-a-Judge metric over OpenAI" [S6] abstract, §4.4.
- `e6cc4c7458a487cd` [strategy] Ledger and brain layering: brain/ holds strategy, decisions-and-why, playbooks (months-years, pulled on demand); doc/ holds system knowledge; work units carry full implementation detail until closed; operational facts (<=1 paragraph, until falsified) are pushed into every session via prime; skills encode procedures for recurring task types, auto-loaded on task match — you boot from the brain, never from the ledger.
- `e763351d80a7f4c0` [general] Claude Code context-window simulation models the summary at ~12% of the summarized token count — illustrative, not a measurement.
- `e794ca1248d1924c` [general] HippoRAG 2 frames memory as non-parametric continual learning, integrating new passages online into a knowledge graph with Personalized PageRank, staying cost/latency efficient and exceeding standard RAG on factual, sense-making, and associative memory tasks (+7% associative over a SOTA embedding model). Source: ICML 2025, arXiv 2502.14802.
- `e7eb9557db61d4e7` [general] Generative Agents (Stanford 2023) batch reflections are generated when the sum of importance scores for the latest perceived events exceeds a threshold of 150; in practice the agents reflected roughly two or three times a day [S4] §4.2.
- `e7f5d7ddbd0506aa` [general] GenAI spans represent logical operations as observed by the caller, covering the operation duration including automatic retries.
- `ea5482b19c31dba4` [general] Python SDK CVE-2026-52869 (GHSA-jpw9-pfvf-9f58, CVSS 7.1): SSE/Streamable HTTP session routing without principal verification — anyone knowing a session ID can inject on it (CWE-639); patched in 1.27.2.
- `eb88bee62f23ab41` [general] Research tracks: TRACK 1 DONE (brain/memory: CoALA, MemGPT/Letta,
- `ef0073bcd67cc7dd` [general] Cheap models are complementary: GPT-4 is wrong while GPT-J/J1-L/GPT-Curie are right on ~6% of HEADLINES queries; GPT-4 is wrong but GPT-3 right on 13% of COQA (upper bounds, not always achieved). (FrugalGPT, arXiv 2305.05176, §4, Fig 4)
- `ef026a1a1cae7b2e` [general] OpenAI ran SWE-bench Verified scaffolds with 'a single seed', noting results 'may differ from what is reported in the official leaderboards' — an acknowledgment that single-seed agent runs are noisy.
- `f085f8338af5bf85` [general] MCP tools list MUST NOT vary per-connection or as a side effect of other requests, but MAY vary by the authorization presented on the request (scope-filtered capability exposure) (Spec 2026-07-28 Tools).
- `f21cd7b5b3c48eb1` [strategy] Phase 6 complete: 6.1 failure register, 6.2 mismatch loop, 6.3
- `f273b2d3255aaefa` [general] OpenAI Agents SDK handles concurrency by identifying sessions by session_id (a row key), not by file ownership; multiple sessions share one conversations.db and different agents can share the same session.
- `f4491eb884521013` [strategy] D7: Fixture policy (ADR-0010): insights reports
- `f4b002f6ac6123e7` [strategy] R5: Deleting or moving failing fixtures out of evals/cases/ — rejected
- `f54fde4a6d009561` [general] Gas Town research status: audited (primary source yegge.ai/gastown, author = maintainer, single-source facts), research date 2026-08-08.
- `f5ece5bdef4f8055` [general] Rust SDK (rmcp) CVE-2026-63128 (GHSA-9pj6-vhgr-3mwh, CVSS 7.5): unauthenticated session-table memory leak -> permanent DoS (~75 GB/day at 2k req/s) (CWE-400/401/772); patched in 2.0.0.
- `f653848c6e81eaf3` [general] OpenAI reports GPT-4 on SWE-bench Lite scoring between 2.7% (early RAG-based scaffold) and 28.3% (CodeR) depending on scaffolding, and therefore runs evaluations 'continually and as often as needed... before, during, and even after training'.
- `f718f7e999afdd74` [general] EleutherAI harness ships a 'Test Set Decontamination' utility scoring only 'data points not found in the model training set', via precomputed 13-gram exact-match indices against The Pile.
- `f840e928415fa051` [strategy] D8: Gate baseline is refreshed via `eval gate --write-baseline` after
- `f84e2ab57f514419` [general] Claude Agent SDK: auto-approved tools never reach canUseTool, so for checks that must run on every call the docs prescribe a PreToolUse hook.
- `f8e399d3786cdd54` [general] Kubernetes flakiness detection tooling: flakes-latest.json (top 10 flakes per week across PR jobs), go.k8s.io/triage (interactive 2-week failure drill-down), testgrid's sort-by-flakiness view, the kind/flake GitHub label, and unit-test stress reproduction via golang.org/x/tools/cmd/stress with -race -count=1.
- `faa50827c8944f4b` [general] FrugalGPT at the same cost improves accuracy by up to 5% (Fig 5 caption); the abstract states 4%. (FrugalGPT, arXiv 2305.05176, §4, Fig 5, Abstract)
- `fabbd0a23fba61bf` [general] Codex (cloud) processes each task independently in a separate, isolated environment preloaded with the codebase; only committed code is evaluated; if AGENTS.md includes programmatic checks, they must all be run AFTER all code changes; eval is scoped to one repo checkout per task instance.
- `faedc0f0a4b0184b` [general] Consolidation rewrites hedged/casual statements into confident dated assertions the agent treats as verified facts; the agent responds to phrasing confidence, not source; a passive 'unverified' tag is ignored, while keeping tentative phrasing rather than upgrading it is the store-level fix; a single redundant source restores correct decisions. Source: 'Manufactured Confidence', arXiv 2606.29279.
- `fb43f89f710315c4` [general] Conformal prediction lets any pre-trained model produce prediction sets/intervals with a user-specified coverage guarantee (e.g. 90% coverage), valid distribution-free and non-asymptotically. (Angelopoulos & Bates, arXiv 2107.07511)
- `fb6ff8fd3ecfaeea` [general] Spinnaker's Manual Judgment pipeline stage waits for the user to click Continue, with optional instructions and user-selectable input options that drive downstream pipeline behavior.
- `fc0733f9714e89f6` [general] MCP's trust model: clients trust servers they connect to; local MCP servers are trusted like any other installed software and run with the same privileges as the client; the connection decision rests with the user/administrator (SECURITY.md).
- `fc88da0620bc3b99` [testing] pytest treats a zero-test run as an error: exit code 5 = 'No tests were collected' (pytest docs, Exit codes).
- `fcf2eb13f45ee48a` [general] The full-architecture μ value in the Generative Agents technical evaluation fell in a section the fetch truncated; only ordering and the Cohen's d are quoted for it [S4].
- `fd74e35848d96223` [testing] mini-agi encodes the falsifier as its proof discipline: '1 focused falsifier for one changed runtime contract, migration, authority boundary, parser, or reproduced bug' — a test that fails when the changed behavior is wrong (AGENTS.md).
- `fda4c8c9837bba04` [general] Anthropic grader taxonomy: code-based (fast, objective, reproducible, but 'brittle to valid variations'), model-based (flexible but 'non-deterministic', requires calibration with human graders), human (gold standard, calibrates LLM graders); recommendation is deterministic graders where possible and grading outcomes rather than tool-call sequences.
- `ff9acb8a3873dce6` [general] Since Cargo 1.97, CARGO_BUILD_WARNINGS is preferred over -D warnings because it does not invalidate build caches.
